IMPACTALIGN REPOSITORY SOURCE



========== FILE: CONTRIBUTING.md ==========

# Contributing

## Setup

```bash
nvm use                 # Node 22
cp .env.example .env    # placeholders only — never commit .env
npm ci
npm run dev:infra       # MongoDB + Redis via Docker
npm run dev:api         # http://localhost:4000/health
npm run dev:web         # http://localhost:3000
npm run dev:admin       # http://localhost:3001
```

## Before opening a pull request

```bash
npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm test && npm run build
```

Husky runs `lint-staged` (ESLint + Prettier) on every commit.

## Rules

1. TypeScript strict; no `any` unless unavoidable and justified in a comment.
2. Shared contracts live in `packages/types`, shared validation in `packages/validation`. Never
   duplicate business logic between `web`, `admin` and `api` (see `docs/architecture/folder-structure.md`).
3. Validate all external input; enforce authorization on the server.
4. Keep components small and reusable; use semantic HTML; keep UI accessible and responsive.
5. Add tests for critical behaviour. Security-relevant changes need a test for the failure case.
6. Record significant architecture choices as an ADR in `docs/decisions/`.
7. Never commit secrets. If you do, rotate them immediately and tell the maintainers.



========== FILE: README.md ==========

# ImpactAlign SDG

AI-assisted platform that connects activities and evidence to the SDGs
(**Goal → Target → Indicator → Evidence → Impact → Validation → Accreditation**), with human validation.
Methodology: **Diagnose → Align → Measure → Validate → Improve**.

> **Status: Phase 1 foundation + public site and dashboard _preview_.** No authentication, stored data or real SDG
> content yet. The dashboard (`/dashboard`) is an interface preview, hidden in production by default (see ADR-002).

## Stack

Next.js 16 · React 19 · TypeScript · Tailwind CSS 4 · Node.js 22 · Express 5 · MongoDB (Mongoose) · Redis ·
Zod · Vitest · Docker · GitHub Actions. See [ADR-001](docs/decisions/ADR-001-initial-architecture.md).

## Quick start

```bash
nvm use                    # Node 22
cp .env.example .env       # placeholders only; never commit .env
npm ci
npm run dev:infra          # MongoDB + Redis in Docker (127.0.0.1 only)
npm run build:packages     # shared packages must be built once (and after changing them)
npm run dev:api            # http://localhost:4000/health
npm run dev:web            # http://localhost:3000
npm run dev:admin          # http://localhost:3001
```

Check the API:

```bash
curl localhost:4000/health            # liveness
curl localhost:4000/health/database   # 200 when MongoDB is reachable, otherwise 503
curl localhost:4000/health/redis      # 200 when Redis is reachable, otherwise 503
```

Validate your environment without starting anything: `npm run check:env`.

## Commands

| Command                                      | What it does                                              |
| -------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| `npm run lint`                               | ESLint (zero warnings allowed)                            |
| `npm run format:check`                       | Prettier check (`npm run format` to fix)                  |
| `npm run typecheck`                          | Builds shared packages, then type-checks every workspace  |
| `npm test`                                   | Builds shared packages, then runs every workspace's tests |
| `npm run build`                              | Builds packages, then the API, web and admin              |
| `npm run dev:infra[:down]`                   | Start/stop local MongoDB and Redis                        |
| `npm run openapi:export -w @impactalign/api` | Regenerate `docs/api/openapi.json`                        |

## Layout

See [`docs/architecture/folder-structure.md`](docs/architecture/folder-structure.md) for the structure and the
architectural rules (dependency direction, no duplicated business logic, shared types/validation).

## Documentation

- [System architecture](docs/architecture/system-architecture.md)
- [Security architecture](docs/security/security-architecture.md) · [SECURITY.md](SECURITY.md)
- [Product overview](docs/product/product-overview.md)
- [ADR-001](docs/decisions/ADR-001-initial-architecture.md)
- [API docs](docs/api/README.md) · [Privacy](docs/privacy/README.md) · [Deployment notes](infrastructure/deployment/README.md)

Contributing: [CONTRIBUTING.md](CONTRIBUTING.md). Licence: placeholder, see [LICENSE](LICENSE).



========== FILE: SECURITY.md ==========

# Security Policy

> Phase 1 covers the project foundation only. Authentication, authorization and data-protection
> controls arrive in later phases; this file will be extended then (Phase 3).

## Reporting a vulnerability

Please **do not open a public issue** for security problems.

Report privately to: **[SECURITY CONTACT — to be set by the project owner before launch]**

Include a description, reproduction steps and affected components. Do not include real personal data.

## Ground rules for contributors

- Never commit secrets, API keys, tokens or real `.env` files. Use environment variables;
  `.env.example` contains placeholders only.
- All external input is validated (Zod) at the boundary; authorization is enforced on the server,
  never trusted from the client.
- Organization data is isolated: no query may return another organization's private data.
- AI output is always labelled AI-generated and never makes a final validation or accreditation decision.

See [`docs/security/security-architecture.md`](docs/security/security-architecture.md) for the security
architecture and its current implementation status.

## Supported versions

Pre-release: only the `main` branch is supported.

Separate, non-indexable admin console. Placeholder only: **do not expose publicly** until authentication and
role-based authorization exist (Phase 3). Port 3001.



========== FILE: apps/admin/app/console/layout.tsx ==========

import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { Container } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { getAdminSession, hasAdminAccess, logoutCurrentSession } from '@/lib/session';
import { AdminSignOutButton } from '@/components/sign-out-button';

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: { default: 'Console', template: '%s | ImpactAlign Admin' }, robots: { index: false, follow: false, nocache: true } };

export default async function ConsoleLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const session = await getAdminSession();
  if (!session) redirect('/login?reauth=1');
  if (!hasAdminAccess(session)) {
    await logoutCurrentSession(session);
    redirect('/login?reauth=1&reason=staff');
  }
  return <div className="min-h-dvh bg-navy-50">
    <header className="border-b border-navy-100 bg-white"><Container className="flex items-center justify-between py-4"><Link href="/console" className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-navy-900">ImpactAlign Admin</Link><div className="flex items-center gap-4"><span className="hidden text-sm sm:inline">{session.user.displayName}</span><AdminSignOutButton /></div></Container></header>
    <Container className="py-10">{children}</Container>
  </div>;
}

import type { Metadata, Viewport } from 'next';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';
import { SkipLink } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { siteUrl } from '@/lib/site-url';
import './globals.css';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  metadataBase: new URL(siteUrl),
  title: { default: 'ImpactAlign Admin', template: '%s | ImpactAlign Admin' },
  robots: { index: false, follow: false, nocache: true },
};

export const viewport: Viewport = { width: 'device-width', initialScale: 1 };

export default async function RootLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  // Dynamic rendering is required for the per-request CSP nonce.
  await headers();
  return (
    <html lang="en">
      <body className="min-h-dvh antialiased">
        <SkipLink />
        <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1} className="focus:outline-none">
          {children}
        </main>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}



========== FILE: apps/admin/app/login/page.tsx ==========

import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { Container } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { AdminLoginForm } from '@/components/login-form';
import { getAdminSession, hasAdminAccess } from '@/lib/session';

export const metadata = { title: 'Admin sign in', robots: { index: false, follow: false, nocache: true } };

export default async function AdminLoginPage({ searchParams }: { searchParams: Promise<{ reauth?: string; reason?: string }> }) {
  const params = await searchParams;
  if (params.reauth !== '1') {
    const session = await getAdminSession();
    if (session && hasAdminAccess(session)) redirect('/console');
  }
  return <Container className="max-w-md py-16">
    <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl font-bold text-navy-900">Admin sign in</h1>
    <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-navy-800">Sign in with your ImpactAlign staff account.</p>
    <AdminLoginForm reauth={params.reauth === '1'} reason={params.reason} />
  </Container>;
}

========== FILE: apps/admin/components/login-form.tsx ==========

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Button } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { loginSchema } from '@impactalign/validation';
import { authApi, describeError } from '@impactalign/api-client';

export function AdminLoginForm({ reauth, reason }: { reauth: boolean; reason?: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(reason === 'staff' ? 'This account does not have admin access. You have been signed out. Sign in with an administrator account.' : null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  async function submit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    const parsed = loginSchema.safeParse({ email, password });
    if (!parsed.success) {
      setError('Enter a valid email address and password.');
      return;
    }
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await authApi.login(parsed.data.email, parsed.data.password);
      // The protected console rechecks /auth/me server-side and logs non-staff sessions out.
      router.replace('/console');
      router.refresh();
    } catch (cause) {
      setError(describeError(cause));
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="mt-8 space-y-5" aria-busy={busy}>
      {reauth && !error && <p role="status" className="rounded-md border border-navy-200 p-3 text-sm">Please sign in again.</p>}
      {error && <p role="alert" className="rounded-md border border-red-700 bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-900">{error}</p>}
      <div className="space-y-1"><label htmlFor="email" className="text-sm font-semibold">Email</label><input id="email" name="email" type="email" autoComplete="username" required value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} disabled={busy} className="w-full rounded-md border border-navy-200 bg-white px-3 py-2" /></div>
      <div className="space-y-1"><label htmlFor="password" className="text-sm font-semibold">Password</label><input id="password" name="password" type="password" autoComplete="current-password" required value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} disabled={busy} className="w-full rounded-md border border-navy-200 bg-white px-3 py-2" /></div>
      <Button type="submit" size="lg" className="w-full" disabled={busy}>{busy ? 'Signing in…' : 'Sign in'}</Button>
    </form>
  );
}



========== FILE: apps/admin/components/sign-out-button.tsx ==========

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Button } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { authApi } from '@impactalign/api-client';
export function AdminSignOutButton() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  async function signOut() {
    setBusy(true);
    try { await authApi.me(); await authApi.logout(); } catch { /* Still leave the protected console. */ }
    router.replace('/login?reauth=1');
    router.refresh();
  }
  return <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={signOut} disabled={busy}>{busy ? 'Signing out…' : 'Sign out'}</Button>;
}



========== FILE: apps/admin/lib/session.test.ts ==========

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { AuthResponse } from '@impactalign/types';
import { hasAdminAccess } from './session';
const session = (roles: string[]) => ({ user: { roles } }) as AuthResponse;
describe('hasAdminAccess', () => {
  it('allows platform admins and super admins', () => {
    expect(hasAdminAccess(session(['ADMIN']))).toBe(true);
    expect(hasAdminAccess(session(['SUPER_ADMIN']))).toBe(true);
  });
  it('denies non-staff roles even if they have other roles', () => {
    expect(hasAdminAccess(session(['PUBLIC_USER']))).toBe(false);
    expect(hasAdminAccess(session(['ASSESSOR', 'ORGANIZATION_ADMIN']))).toBe(false);
  });
});



========== FILE: apps/admin/lib/session.ts ==========

import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import type { AuthResponse } from '@impactalign/types';

const apiUrl = () => (process.env.API_INTERNAL_URL ?? 'http://localhost:4000').replace(/\/$/, '');
const ADMIN_ROLES = new Set(['ADMIN', 'SUPER_ADMIN']);

export function hasAdminAccess(session: AuthResponse): boolean {
  return session.user.roles.some((role) => ADMIN_ROLES.has(role));
}

/** Validate the browser's session on the server using the API's authoritative /me endpoint. */
export async function getAdminSession(): Promise<AuthResponse | null> {
  const cookieHeader = (await cookies()).toString();
  if (!cookieHeader) return null;
  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${apiUrl()}/api/v1/auth/me`, {
      headers: { cookie: cookieHeader, accept: 'application/json' },
      cache: 'no-store',
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(4_000),
    });
    return response.ok ? ((await response.json()) as AuthResponse) : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/** Invalidate a valid non-staff session before sending the browser back to login. */
export async function logoutCurrentSession(session: AuthResponse): Promise<void> {
  const cookieHeader = (await cookies()).toString();
  try {
    await fetch(`${apiUrl()}/api/v1/auth/logout`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        cookie: cookieHeader,
        accept: 'application/json',
        'content-type': 'application/json',
        'x-csrf-token': session.csrfToken,
      },
      body: '{}',
      cache: 'no-store',
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(4_000),
    });
  } catch {
    // Fail closed: the protected console is still never rendered for non-staff users.
  }
}

========== FILE: apps/admin/next.config.ts ==========

import path from 'node:path';
import type { NextConfig } from 'next';
import { staticSecurityHeaders } from '@impactalign/config';

const nextConfig: NextConfig = {
  reactStrictMode: true,
  poweredByHeader: false,
  output: 'standalone',
  // Monorepo: trace dependencies from the repository root so standalone builds include packages/*.
  outputFileTracingRoot: path.join(__dirname, '../../'),
  async rewrites() {
    const apiInternalUrl = (process.env.API_INTERNAL_URL ?? 'http://localhost:4000').replace(/\/$/, '');
    return [{ source: '/api/v1/:path*', destination: `${apiInternalUrl}/api/v1/:path*` }];
  },
  async headers() {
    return [
      {
        source: '/:path*',
        headers: [
          ...staticSecurityHeaders.map(({ key, value }) => ({ key, value })),
          { key: 'X-Robots-Tag', value: 'noindex, nofollow, noarchive' },
        ],
      },
    ];
  },
};

export default nextConfig;

========== FILE: apps/admin/proxy.ts ==========

import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server';
import { buildContentSecurityPolicy, generateNonce } from '@impactalign/config';

/** Sets a per-request, nonce-based Content-Security-Policy (requires dynamic rendering). */
export function proxy(request: NextRequest): NextResponse {
  const nonce = generateNonce();
  const csp = buildContentSecurityPolicy({
    nonce,
    isDev: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development',
    connectSrc: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ? [process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL] : [],
  });

  const requestHeaders = new Headers(request.headers);
  requestHeaders.set('x-nonce', nonce);
  requestHeaders.set('Content-Security-Policy', csp);

  const response = NextResponse.next({ request: { headers: requestHeaders } });
  response.headers.set('Content-Security-Policy', csp);
  return response;
}

export const config = {
  matcher: [
    {
      source: '/((?!_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico).*)',
      missing: [
        { type: 'header', key: 'next-router-prefetch' },
        { type: 'header', key: 'purpose', value: 'prefetch' },
      ],
    },
  ],
};

========== FILE: apps/api/README.md ==========

# @impactalign/api

Express 5 + TypeScript REST API. Phase 1 exposes only the health endpoints.

- `src/app.ts` — builds the Express app from injected dependencies (testable without a network).
- `src/main.ts` — process bootstrap: env validation, MongoDB/Redis connections, graceful shutdown.
- `src/common/` — logger, error envelope, request helpers. `src/health/` — health router + service.
- `src/infrastructure/` — MongoDB (background retry) and Redis clients.
- `src/openapi/` — OpenAPI document.

Run: `npm run dev -w @impactalign/api` (needs `.env`, see repo root). Feature folders are added in Phase 2.

========== FILE: apps/api/server.ts ==========

import express from 'express';
import { connectMongo, pingMongo } from '@impactalign/database';
import { createLogger } from './src/common/logger';
import { loadEnv } from './src/config/env';
import { createMongoRepositories } from './src/data/mongo-repositories';
import { HealthService } from './src/health/health.service';
import { createRedisClient, pingRedis } from './src/infrastructure/redis';
import { createApp } from './src/app';
import { NoopEmailSender } from './src/auth/email';
import { createServices } from './src/services';

const env = loadEnv();
const logger = createLogger(env);
const redis = createRedisClient(env.REDIS_URL, logger);

const healthService = new HealthService(
  {
    pingDatabase: pingMongo,
    pingRedis: () => pingRedis(redis),
  },
  {
    serviceName: 'impactalign-api',
    version: process.env['npm_package_version'] ?? '0.1.0',
    timeoutMs: env.HEALTH_CHECK_TIMEOUT_MS,
    logger,
  },
);

const services = createServices({
  env,
  logger,
  repos: createMongoRepositories(),
  email: new NoopEmailSender(),
});

const api = createApp({ env, logger, healthService, services });
const handler = express();

let mongoConnection: Promise<unknown> | undefined;

handler.use(async (_req, _res, next) => {
  try {
    if (!mongoConnection) {
      mongoConnection = connectMongo({ uri: env.MONGODB_URI }).catch((error: unknown) => {
        mongoConnection = undefined;
        throw error;
      });
    }

    await mongoConnection;
    next();
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
});

handler.use(api);

export default handler;


========== FILE: apps/api/src/api-router.ts ==========

import { Router } from 'express';
import type { ApiEnv } from '@impactalign/config';
import { createAuthRouter } from './auth/auth.router';
import { authenticate, csrfGuard, originGuard, requireJson } from './auth/middleware';
import { createRateLimiter } from './common/rate-limit';
import { createOrganizationsRouter } from './organizations/organizations.router';
import { createUsersRouter } from './users/users.router';
import type { Services } from './services';
import { createSdgsRouter } from './sdgs/sdgs.router';

/** Everything under /api/v1. Order matters: origin → JSON → session → CSRF → routes. */
export function createApiRouter(env: ApiEnv, services: Services): Router {
  const router = Router();
  const allowedOrigins = new Set(env.API_CORS_ORIGINS.map((origin) => new URL(origin).origin));
  const authLimiter = createRateLimiter({ windowMs: 15 * 60_000, limit: env.AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_MAX });

  router.use(originGuard(allowedOrigins));
  router.use(requireJson);
  router.use(authenticate(services.auth, services.cookie));
  router.use(csrfGuard);

  router.use(
    '/auth',
    createAuthRouter({ auth: services.auth, cookie: services.cookie, limiter: authLimiter }),
  );
  router.use('/users', createUsersRouter(services.users));
  router.use('/organizations', createOrganizationsRouter(services.organizations));
  router.use('/sdgs', createSdgsRouter(services.sdgs));

  return router;
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/app.test.ts ==========

import request from 'supertest';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { buildTestApp } from './test-utils';

describe('API hardening', () => {
  const app = buildTestApp();

  it('sets security headers and hides the framework', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/health');
    expect(res.headers['x-powered-by']).toBeUndefined();
    expect(res.headers['x-content-type-options']).toBe('nosniff');
    expect(res.headers['content-security-policy']).toContain("default-src 'none'");
    expect(res.headers['strict-transport-security']).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('returns a JSON 404 envelope with a request id', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/nope');
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
    expect(res.body.error.code).toBe('NOT_FOUND');
    expect(res.body.error.requestId).toBe(res.headers['x-request-id']);
  });

  it('echoes a well-formed X-Request-Id and replaces a malformed one', async () => {
    const good = await request(app).get('/health').set('X-Request-Id', 'abcd-1234-efgh');
    expect(good.headers['x-request-id']).toBe('abcd-1234-efgh');
    const bad = await request(app).get('/health').set('X-Request-Id', 'bad id <script>');
    expect(bad.headers['x-request-id']).not.toContain('<script>');
  });

  it('only allows configured CORS origins', async () => {
    const allowed = await request(app).get('/health').set('Origin', 'http://localhost:3000');
    expect(allowed.headers['access-control-allow-origin']).toBe('http://localhost:3000');
    const denied = await request(app).get('/health').set('Origin', 'https://evil.example');
    expect(denied.headers['access-control-allow-origin']).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('rejects oversized bodies with 413 and malformed JSON with 400', async () => {
    const big = await request(app)
      .post('/anything')
      .set('Content-Type', 'application/json')
      .send(JSON.stringify({ blob: 'x'.repeat(200_000) }));
    expect(big.status).toBe(413);
    const bad = await request(app)
      .post('/anything')
      .set('Content-Type', 'application/json')
      .send('{not json');
    expect(bad.status).toBe(400);
    expect(bad.body.error.code).toBe('INVALID_JSON');
  });

  it('rate limits non-health routes but never health probes', async () => {
    const limited = buildTestApp({}, { RATE_LIMIT_MAX: '2' });
    const statuses: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) statuses.push((await request(limited).get('/nope')).status);
    expect(statuses).toEqual([404, 404, 429, 429]);
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i += 1) {
      expect((await request(limited).get('/health')).status).toBe(200);
    }
  });

  it('does not expose API docs unless explicitly enabled', async () => {
    expect((await request(app).get('/docs/openapi.json')).status).toBe(404);
    const withDocs = buildTestApp({}, { API_SWAGGER_ENABLED: 'true' });
    expect((await request(withDocs).get('/docs/openapi.json')).status).toBe(200);
  });
});



========== FILE: apps/api/src/app.ts ==========

import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import cookieParser from 'cookie-parser';
import cors from 'cors';
import express, { type Express } from 'express';
import helmet from 'helmet';
import { pinoHttp } from 'pino-http';
import swaggerUi from 'swagger-ui-express';
import type { ApiEnv } from '@impactalign/config';
import { createApiRouter } from './api-router';
import { errorHandler, notFoundHandler } from './common/error-handler';
import type { AppLogger } from './common/logger';
import { createRateLimiter } from './common/rate-limit';
import { createHealthRouter } from './health/health.router';
import type { HealthService } from './health/health.service';
import { openApiDocument } from './openapi/openapi';
import type { Services } from './services';

export interface AppDependencies {
  readonly env: ApiEnv;
  readonly logger: AppLogger;
  readonly healthService: HealthService;
  readonly services: Services;
}

const REQUEST_ID_PATTERN = /^[A-Za-z0-9-]{8,64}$/;

export function createApp({ env, logger, healthService, services }: AppDependencies): Express {
  const app = express();
  app.disable('x-powered-by');
  app.set('trust proxy', env.API_TRUST_PROXY);

  // Request ID + structured request logging. Client-supplied IDs are accepted only if well-formed.
  app.use(
    pinoHttp({
      logger,
      genReqId: (req, res) => {
        const incoming = req.headers['x-request-id'];
        const id =
          typeof incoming === 'string' && REQUEST_ID_PATTERN.test(incoming)
            ? incoming
            : randomUUID();
        res.setHeader('X-Request-Id', id);
        return id;
      },
      autoLogging: { ignore: (req) => req.url?.startsWith('/health') ?? false },
    }),
  );

  // API docs UI (never in production — enforced by env validation). Needs its own relaxed CSP.
  if (env.API_SWAGGER_ENABLED) {
    app.use('/docs', helmet({ contentSecurityPolicy: false }));
    app.get('/docs/openapi.json', (_req, res) => {
      res.json(openApiDocument);
    });
    app.use('/docs', swaggerUi.serve, swaggerUi.setup(openApiDocument));
  }

  app.use(
    helmet({
      contentSecurityPolicy: {
        useDefaults: false,
        directives: { 'default-src': ["'none'"], 'frame-ancestors': ["'none'"] },
      },
      referrerPolicy: { policy: 'no-referrer' },
    }),
  );

  const allowedOrigins = new Set(env.API_CORS_ORIGINS.map((origin) => new URL(origin).origin));
  app.use(
    cors({
      origin: (origin, callback) => callback(null, !origin || allowedOrigins.has(origin)),
      methods: ['GET', 'POST', 'PUT', 'PATCH', 'DELETE'],
      credentials: true,
      maxAge: 600,
    }),
  );

  // Health endpoints are mounted before rate limiting so orchestrator probes are never throttled.
  app.use('/health', createHealthRouter(healthService));

  app.use(createRateLimiter({ windowMs: env.RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS, limit: env.RATE_LIMIT_MAX }));
  app.use(express.json({ limit: '100kb' }));
  app.use(cookieParser());

  app.use('/api/v1', createApiRouter(env, services));

  app.use(notFoundHandler);
  app.use(errorHandler);
  return app;
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/auth/audit.service.ts ==========

import type { AuditEntry, AuditRepository } from '../data/repositories';
import type { AppLogger } from '../common/logger';

/** Writes security events to the audit log and the structured log. Never throws into a request. */
export class AuditService {
  constructor(
    private readonly repo: AuditRepository,
    private readonly logger: AppLogger,
  ) {}

  async record(entry: AuditEntry): Promise<void> {
    this.logger.info(
      {
        securityEvent: entry.action,
        outcome: entry.outcome,
        actorId: entry.actorId,
        requestId: entry.requestId,
        ...entry.metadata,
      },
      'security event',
    );
    try {
      await this.repo.insert(entry);
    } catch (err) {
      this.logger.error({ err, securityEvent: entry.action }, 'Failed to write audit log');
    }
  }
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/auth/auth.router.ts ==========

import { Router, type RequestHandler } from 'express';
import {
  changePasswordSchema,
  forgotPasswordSchema,
  loginSchema,
  registerSchema,
  resetPasswordSchema,
  verifyEmailSchema,
} from '@impactalign/validation';
import type { AuthResponse } from '@impactalign/types';
import { validateBody } from '../common/validate';
import type { AuthService } from './auth.service';
import { clearSessionCookie, setSessionCookie, type CookieSettings } from './cookies';
import { requestContext, requireAuth } from './middleware';
import { toPublicUser } from './public-user';

export interface AuthRouterDeps {
  auth: AuthService;
  cookie: CookieSettings;
  /** Tight per-IP limiter for credential endpoints. */
  limiter: RequestHandler;
}

export function createAuthRouter({ auth, cookie, limiter }: AuthRouterDeps): Router {
  const router = Router();

  router.post('/register', limiter, validateBody(registerSchema), async (req, res) => {
    await auth.register(req.body, requestContext(req));
    // Identical response whether or not the address is already registered.
    res
      .status(202)
      .json({ message: 'If this address can be used, a verification email has been sent.' });
  });

  router.post('/verify-email', limiter, validateBody(verifyEmailSchema), async (req, res) => {
    await auth.verifyEmail(req.body.token, requestContext(req));
    res.json({ message: 'Email verified. You can now sign in.' });
  });

  router.post('/login', limiter, validateBody(loginSchema), async (req, res) => {
    const result = await auth.login(req.body, requestContext(req));
    setSessionCookie(res, cookie, result.sessionToken, result.expiresAt);
    const body: AuthResponse = { user: toPublicUser(result.user), csrfToken: result.csrfToken };
    res.json(body);
  });

  router.post('/logout', requireAuth, async (req, res) => {
    await auth.logout(req.auth!, requestContext(req));
    clearSessionCookie(res, cookie);
    res.status(204).end();
  });

  router.get('/me', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
    const body: AuthResponse = {
      user: toPublicUser(req.auth!.user),
      csrfToken: req.auth!.session.csrfToken,
    };
    res.json(body);
  });

  router.post('/forgot-password', limiter, validateBody(forgotPasswordSchema), async (req, res) => {
    await auth.forgotPassword(req.body.email);
    res
      .status(202)
      .json({ message: 'If an account exists for this address, a reset link has been sent.' });
  });

  router.post('/reset-password', limiter, validateBody(resetPasswordSchema), async (req, res) => {
    await auth.resetPassword(req.body.token, req.body.password, requestContext(req));
    clearSessionCookie(res, cookie);
    res.json({ message: 'Password updated. Sign in with your new password.' });
  });

  router.post(
    '/change-password',
    requireAuth,
    validateBody(changePasswordSchema),
    async (req, res) => {
      await auth.changePassword(
        req.auth!,
        req.body.currentPassword,
        req.body.newPassword,
        requestContext(req),
      );
      res.status(204).end();
    },
  );

  return router;
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/auth/auth.routes.test.ts ==========

import request from 'supertest';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { buildTestContext } from '../test-utils';
import { STRONG_PASSWORD } from '../testing/auth-context';

const API = '/api/v1/auth';
const json = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' };
const body = (email: string, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  email,
  password: STRONG_PASSWORD,
  displayName: 'Ada Lovelace',
  accountType: 'RESEARCHER',
  ...extra,
});

/** A signed-in browser: cookie jar + CSRF token. */
async function signIn(address = 'ada@example.org') {
  const t = buildTestContext();
  await t.signUp(address); // creates + verifies the account
  const agent = request.agent(t.app);
  const res = await agent.post(`${API}/login`).send({ email: address, password: STRONG_PASSWORD });
  return { ...t, agent, csrf: res.body.csrfToken as string, loginRes: res };
}

describe('registration over HTTP', () => {
  it('answers 202 with the same body for new and existing addresses', async () => {
    const { app } = buildTestContext();
    const first = await request(app).post(`${API}/register`).send(body('ada@example.org'));
    const again = await request(app).post(`${API}/register`).send(body('ada@example.org'));
    expect(first.status).toBe(202);
    expect(again.status).toBe(202);
    expect(again.body).toEqual(first.body);
  });

  it('rejects a client-supplied role (no privilege escalation at sign-up)', async () => {
    const { app, repos } = buildTestContext();
    for (const extra of [
      { roles: ['SUPER_ADMIN'] },
      { role: 'ADMIN' },
      { accountType: 'SUPER_ADMIN' },
    ]) {
      const res = await request(app).post(`${API}/register`).send(body('eve@example.org', extra));
      expect(res.status).toBe(400);
      expect(res.body.error.code).toBe('VALIDATION_ERROR');
    }
    expect(repos.data.users).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('returns field errors without echoing the submitted password', async () => {
    const { app } = buildTestContext();
    const res = await request(app)
      .post(`${API}/register`)
      .send(body('bad', { password: 'short' }));
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect(res.body.error.details.map((d: { path: string }) => d.path)).toEqual(
      expect.arrayContaining(['email', 'password']),
    );
    expect(JSON.stringify(res.body)).not.toContain('short');
  });
});

describe('login, session cookie and /me', () => {
  it('sets an HttpOnly SameSite cookie and never returns the password hash', async () => {
    const { loginRes, agent } = await signIn();
    expect(loginRes.status).toBe(200);
    const cookie = String(loginRes.headers['set-cookie']);
    expect(cookie).toMatch(/ia_session=/);
    expect(cookie).toMatch(/HttpOnly/i);
    expect(cookie).toMatch(/SameSite=Lax/i);
    expect(JSON.stringify(loginRes.body)).not.toMatch(/passwordHash|scrypt\$/);
    const me = await agent.get(`${API}/me`);
    expect(me.status).toBe(200);
    expect(me.body.user.email).toBe('ada@example.org');
    expect(me.body.csrfToken).toBe(loginRes.body.csrfToken);
  });

  it('uses a Secure __Host- cookie in production', async () => {
    const prod = {
      NODE_ENV: 'production',
      API_CORS_ORIGINS: 'https://app.example.org',
      MONGODB_URI: 'mongodb://u:p@db.internal/d',
      REDIS_URL: 'rediss://:p@cache.internal:6379',
      APP_WEB_URL: 'https://app.example.org',
      EMAIL_TRANSPORT: 'disabled',
    };
    const t = buildTestContext({}, prod);
    await t.signUp('ada@example.org');
    const res = await request(t.app)
      .post(`${API}/login`)
      .send({ email: 'ada@example.org', password: STRONG_PASSWORD });
    expect(String(res.headers['set-cookie'])).toMatch(/^__Host-ia_session=.*Secure/i);
  });

  it('gives a generic 401 for a wrong password and an unknown address', async () => {
    const { app, signUp } = buildTestContext();
    await signUp('ada@example.org');
    const wrong = await request(app)
      .post(`${API}/login`)
      .send({ email: 'ada@example.org', password: 'wrong-password-123' });
    const ghost = await request(app)
      .post(`${API}/login`)
      .send({ email: 'ghost@example.org', password: 'wrong-password-123' });
    expect(wrong.status).toBe(401);
    expect(ghost.status).toBe(401);
    expect(wrong.body.error.message).toBe(ghost.body.error.message);
  });

  it('locks out after repeated failures (429)', async () => {
    const { app, signUp } = buildTestContext();
    await signUp('ada@example.org');
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) {
      await request(app)
        .post(`${API}/login`)
        .send({ email: 'ada@example.org', password: 'wrong-password-123' });
    }
    const res = await request(app)
      .post(`${API}/login`)
      .send({ email: 'ada@example.org', password: STRONG_PASSWORD });
    expect(res.status).toBe(429);
    expect(res.body.error.code).toBe('TOO_MANY_ATTEMPTS');
  });

  it('rate limits credential endpoints per IP', async () => {
    const { app } = buildTestContext({}, { AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_MAX: '2' });
    const codes: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
      codes.push(
        (await request(app).post(`${API}/login`).send({ email: 'a@example.org', password: 'x' }))
          .status,
      );
    }
    expect(codes).toEqual([401, 401, 429, 429]);
  });
});

describe('unauthorized access', () => {
  it.each([
    ['GET', '/me'],
    ['POST', '/logout'],
    ['POST', '/change-password'],
  ])('%s %s without a session is 401', async (method, path) => {
    const { app } = buildTestContext();
    const res = await request(app)[method === 'GET' ? 'get' : 'post'](`${API}${path}`).send({});
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    expect(res.body.error.code).toBe('UNAUTHENTICATED');
  });

  it('a forged or garbage cookie is 401', async () => {
    const { app } = buildTestContext();
    const res = await request(app).get(`${API}/me`).set('Cookie', 'ia_session=forged-token-value');
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });

  it('an expired session is 401 and cannot be revived', async () => {
    const { agent, advance } = await signIn();
    expect((await agent.get(`${API}/me`)).status).toBe(200);
    advance(61 * 60_000); // idle timeout in the test setup is 60 minutes
    expect((await agent.get(`${API}/me`)).status).toBe(401);
  });
});

describe('CSRF, origin and content-type guards', () => {
  it('rejects state-changing requests without the CSRF token, accepts them with it', async () => {
    const { agent, csrf } = await signIn();
    const missing = await agent.post(`${API}/logout`).send({});
    expect(missing.status).toBe(403);
    expect(missing.body.error.code).toBe('CSRF_FAILED');
    expect((await agent.post(`${API}/logout`).set('X-CSRF-Token', 'wrong').send({})).status).toBe(
      403,
    );

    const ok = await agent.post(`${API}/logout`).set('X-CSRF-Token', csrf).send({});
    expect(ok.status).toBe(204);
    expect((await agent.get(`${API}/me`)).status).toBe(401); // session revoked server-side
  });

  it('rejects browser requests from a foreign origin', async () => {
    const { app } = buildTestContext();
    const res = await request(app)
      .post(`${API}/login`)
      .set('Origin', 'https://evil.example')
      .send({ email: 'a@example.org', password: 'x' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
    expect(res.body.error.code).toBe('ORIGIN_NOT_ALLOWED');
  });

  it('requires JSON bodies (415 for form posts)', async () => {
    const { app } = buildTestContext();
    const res = await request(app)
      .post(`${API}/login`)
      .type('form')
      .send('email=a@example.org&password=x');
    expect(res.status).toBe(415);
  });
});

describe('verification and password reset over HTTP', () => {
  it('blocks sign-in until the emailed link is used', async () => {
    const t = buildTestContext();
    await request(t.app).post(`${API}/register`).send(body('ada@example.org'));
    const before = await request(t.app)
      .post(`${API}/login`)
      .send({ email: 'ada@example.org', password: STRONG_PASSWORD });
    expect(before.status).toBe(403);
    const token = t.email.tokenFor('verify', 'ada@example.org');
    expect((await request(t.app).post(`${API}/verify-email`).send({ token })).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await request(t.app).post(`${API}/verify-email`).send({ token })).status).toBe(400); // one-time
    const after = await request(t.app)
      .post(`${API}/login`)
      .send({ email: 'ada@example.org', password: STRONG_PASSWORD });
    expect(after.status).toBe(200);
  });

  it('forgot-password answers identically for known and unknown addresses', async () => {
    const t = buildTestContext();
    await t.signUp('ada@example.org');
    const known = await request(t.app)
      .post(`${API}/forgot-password`)
      .send({ email: 'ada@example.org' });
    const unknown = await request(t.app)
      .post(`${API}/forgot-password`)
      .send({ email: 'ghost@example.org' });
    expect(known.status).toBe(202);
    expect(unknown.status).toBe(202);
    expect(unknown.body).toEqual(known.body);
  });

  it('rejects an invalid reset token', async () => {
    const { app } = buildTestContext();
    const res = await request(app)
      .post(`${API}/reset-password`)
      .send({ token: 'x'.repeat(43), password: 'brand-new-password-1' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect(res.body.error.code).toBe('INVALID_OR_EXPIRED_TOKEN');
  });

  it('resets once, ends the old session and lets the new password in', async () => {
    const t = await signIn();
    await request(t.app).post(`${API}/forgot-password`).send({ email: 'ada@example.org' });
    const token = t.email.tokenFor('reset', 'ada@example.org');
    const reset = await request(t.app)
      .post(`${API}/reset-password`)
      .send({ token, password: 'brand-new-password-1' });
    expect(reset.status).toBe(200);
    expect((await t.agent.get(`${API}/me`)).status).toBe(401);
    expect(
      (
        await request(t.app)
          .post(`${API}/reset-password`)
          .send({ token, password: 'another-password-12' })
      ).status,
    ).toBe(400);
    const login = await request(t.app)
      .post(`${API}/login`)
      .send({ email: 'ada@example.org', password: 'brand-new-password-1' });
    expect(login.status).toBe(200);
  });

  it('change-password needs the CSRF token and keeps only the current session', async () => {
    const t = await signIn();
    const payload = { currentPassword: STRONG_PASSWORD, newPassword: 'brand-new-password-1' };
    expect((await t.agent.post(`${API}/change-password`).send(payload)).status).toBe(403);
    expect(
      (await t.agent.post(`${API}/change-password`).set('X-CSRF-Token', t.csrf).send(payload))
        .status,
    ).toBe(204);
    expect((await t.agent.get(`${API}/me`)).status).toBe(200);
  });
});

describe('error responses', () => {
  it('never include a stack trace or internal detail', async () => {
    const { app } = buildTestContext();
    const res = await request(app).post(`${API}/login`).set(json).send('{"email": ');
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect(JSON.stringify(res.body)).not.toMatch(/at .*\(|node_modules|stack/i);
  });
});



========== FILE: apps/api/src/auth/auth.service.test.ts ==========

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { hashToken } from '@impactalign/auth';
import { AuditEvent } from '@impactalign/types';
import { HttpError } from '../common/http-error';
import { buildAuthTestContext, STRONG_PASSWORD } from '../testing/auth-context';

const register = (c: ReturnType<typeof buildAuthTestContext>, email: string) =>
  c.auth.register(
    { email, password: STRONG_PASSWORD, displayName: 'Ada Lovelace', accountType: 'RESEARCHER' },
    c.ctx,
  );

const rejects = async (promise: Promise<unknown>, status: number, code: string) => {
  const err = await promise.then(
    () => null,
    (e: unknown) => e,
  );
  expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(HttpError);
  expect(err).toMatchObject({ status, code });
};

describe('registration', () => {
  it('stores a hash (never the password), gives the chosen role and sends a verification link', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    await register(c, 'ada@example.org');
    const user = c.repos.data.users[0]!;
    expect(user.passwordHash).not.toContain(STRONG_PASSWORD);
    expect(user.roles).toEqual(['RESEARCHER']);
    expect(user.emailVerifiedAt).toBeNull();
    expect(c.email.tokenFor('verify', 'ada@example.org').length).toBeGreaterThan(40);
  });

  it('stores only the hash of the verification token', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    await register(c, 'ada@example.org');
    const token = c.email.tokenFor('verify', 'ada@example.org');
    expect(c.repos.data.tokens.email_verification[0]!.tokenHash).toBe(hashToken(token));
    expect(JSON.stringify(c.repos.data.tokens)).not.toContain(token);
  });

  it('does not reveal whether an email is registered', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    await register(c, 'ada@example.org');
    await expect(register(c, 'ada@example.org')).resolves.toBeUndefined(); // same outcome
    expect(c.repos.data.users).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(c.email.sent.at(-1)?.kind).toBe('exists');
  });
});

describe('email verification', () => {
  it('blocks login until verified, then allows it', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    await register(c, 'ada@example.org');
    await rejects(
      c.auth.login({ email: 'ada@example.org', password: STRONG_PASSWORD }, c.ctx),
      403,
      'EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED',
    );
    await c.auth.verifyEmail(c.email.tokenFor('verify', 'ada@example.org'), c.ctx);
    await expect(
      c.auth.login({ email: 'ada@example.org', password: STRONG_PASSWORD }, c.ctx),
    ).resolves.toBeDefined();
  });

  it('rejects an unknown, reused or expired token', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    await register(c, 'ada@example.org');
    const token = c.email.tokenFor('verify', 'ada@example.org');
    await rejects(c.auth.verifyEmail('x'.repeat(43), c.ctx), 400, 'INVALID_OR_EXPIRED_TOKEN');
    await c.auth.verifyEmail(token, c.ctx);
    await rejects(c.auth.verifyEmail(token, c.ctx), 400, 'INVALID_OR_EXPIRED_TOKEN');

    await register(c, 'bob@example.org');
    const late = c.email.tokenFor('verify', 'bob@example.org');
    c.advance(25 * 60 * 60_000);
    await rejects(c.auth.verifyEmail(late, c.ctx), 400, 'INVALID_OR_EXPIRED_TOKEN');
  });
});

describe('login', () => {
  it('gives the same error for a wrong password and an unknown email', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    await c.signUp('ada@example.org');
    await rejects(
      c.auth.login({ email: 'ada@example.org', password: 'wrong-password-123' }, c.ctx),
      401,
      'INVALID_CREDENTIALS',
    );
    await rejects(
      c.auth.login({ email: 'nobody@example.org', password: 'wrong-password-123' }, c.ctx),
      401,
      'INVALID_CREDENTIALS',
    );
  });

  it('locks the account after repeated failures — also for unknown emails — and unlocks after the window', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    await c.signUp('ada@example.org');
    for (const email of ['ada@example.org', 'ghost@example.org']) {
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1)
        await c.auth.login({ email, password: 'bad-password-1234' }, c.ctx).catch(() => null);
      await rejects(
        c.auth.login({ email, password: STRONG_PASSWORD }, c.ctx),
        429,
        'TOO_MANY_ATTEMPTS',
      );
    }
    c.advance(16 * 60_000);
    await expect(
      c.auth.login({ email: 'ada@example.org', password: STRONG_PASSWORD }, c.ctx),
    ).resolves.toBeDefined();
  });

  it('stores only the session token hash and issues a new session each time (no fixation)', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    const first = await c.signUp('ada@example.org');
    const second = await c.auth.login(
      { email: 'ada@example.org', password: STRONG_PASSWORD },
      c.ctx,
    );
    expect(first.sessionToken).not.toBe(second.sessionToken);
    expect(JSON.stringify(c.repos.data.sessions)).not.toContain(first.sessionToken);
    expect(c.repos.data.sessions[0]!.tokenHash).toBe(hashToken(first.sessionToken));
  });

  it('writes audit events for success and failure', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    await c.signUp('ada@example.org');
    await c.auth
      .login({ email: 'ada@example.org', password: 'wrong-password-123' }, c.ctx)
      .catch(() => null);
    const events = c.repos.data.audit.map((e) => `${e.action}:${e.outcome}`);
    expect(events).toContain(`${AuditEvent.LOGIN_SUCCESS}:success`);
    expect(events).toContain(`${AuditEvent.LOGIN_FAILED}:failure`);
    expect(events).toContain(`${AuditEvent.EMAIL_VERIFIED}:success`);
    expect(JSON.stringify(c.repos.data.audit)).not.toContain(STRONG_PASSWORD);
  });
});

describe('sessions', () => {
  it('authenticates a valid session and rejects garbage', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    const { sessionToken, user } = await c.signUp('ada@example.org');
    expect((await c.auth.authenticate(sessionToken))?.user.id).toBe(user.id);
    expect(await c.auth.authenticate(undefined)).toBeNull();
    expect(await c.auth.authenticate('not-a-real-token')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('expires after the idle timeout and revokes the session', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    const { sessionToken } = await c.signUp('ada@example.org');
    c.advance(61 * 60_000);
    expect(await c.auth.authenticate(sessionToken)).toBeNull();
    expect(c.repos.data.sessions[0]!.revokedAt).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it('expires at the absolute limit even when the user stays active', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    const { sessionToken } = await c.signUp('ada@example.org');
    for (let i = 0; i < 23; i += 1) {
      c.advance(50 * 60_000);
      if (!(await c.auth.authenticate(sessionToken))) break;
    }
    c.advance(60 * 60_000);
    expect(await c.auth.authenticate(sessionToken)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('logout revokes the session', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    const { sessionToken } = await c.signUp('ada@example.org');
    await c.auth.logout((await c.auth.authenticate(sessionToken))!, c.ctx);
    expect(await c.auth.authenticate(sessionToken)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('stops working when the account is disabled', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    const { sessionToken } = await c.signUp('ada@example.org');
    c.repos.data.users[0]!.status = 'disabled';
    expect(await c.auth.authenticate(sessionToken)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('password reset', () => {
  it('rejects invalid tokens', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    await rejects(
      c.auth.resetPassword('x'.repeat(43), 'new-password-12345', c.ctx),
      400,
      'INVALID_OR_EXPIRED_TOKEN',
    );
  });

  it('says nothing about unknown emails', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    await expect(c.auth.forgotPassword('ghost@example.org')).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    expect(c.email.sent).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('works once, ends all sessions, unlocks the account and accepts the new password', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    const { sessionToken } = await c.signUp('ada@example.org');
    await c.auth.forgotPassword('ada@example.org');
    const token = c.email.tokenFor('reset', 'ada@example.org');

    await c.auth.resetPassword(token, 'brand-new-password-1', c.ctx);
    expect(await c.auth.authenticate(sessionToken)).toBeNull();
    await rejects(
      c.auth.resetPassword(token, 'another-password-12', c.ctx),
      400,
      'INVALID_OR_EXPIRED_TOKEN',
    ); // one-time
    await rejects(
      c.auth.login({ email: 'ada@example.org', password: STRONG_PASSWORD }, c.ctx),
      401,
      'INVALID_CREDENTIALS',
    );
    await expect(
      c.auth.login({ email: 'ada@example.org', password: 'brand-new-password-1' }, c.ctx),
    ).resolves.toBeDefined();
    expect(c.repos.data.audit.map((e) => e.action)).toContain(AuditEvent.PASSWORD_RESET);
  });

  it('expires after an hour and a newer request invalidates the older link', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    await c.signUp('ada@example.org');
    await c.auth.forgotPassword('ada@example.org');
    const old = c.email.tokenFor('reset', 'ada@example.org');
    await c.auth.forgotPassword('ada@example.org');
    await rejects(
      c.auth.resetPassword(old, 'brand-new-password-1', c.ctx),
      400,
      'INVALID_OR_EXPIRED_TOKEN',
    );
    const fresh = c.email.tokenFor('reset', 'ada@example.org');
    c.advance(61 * 60_000);
    await rejects(
      c.auth.resetPassword(fresh, 'brand-new-password-1', c.ctx),
      400,
      'INVALID_OR_EXPIRED_TOKEN',
    );
  });
});

describe('change password', () => {
  it('needs the current password, then ends the other sessions but keeps this one', async () => {
    const c = buildAuthTestContext();
    const first = await c.signUp('ada@example.org');
    const second = await c.auth.login(
      { email: 'ada@example.org', password: STRONG_PASSWORD },
      c.ctx,
    );
    const current = (await c.auth.authenticate(second.sessionToken))!;

    await rejects(
      c.auth.changePassword(current, 'wrong-current-password', 'brand-new-password-1', c.ctx),
      400,
      'INVALID_CURRENT_PASSWORD',
    );
    await c.auth.changePassword(current, STRONG_PASSWORD, 'brand-new-password-1', c.ctx);
    expect(await c.auth.authenticate(first.sessionToken)).toBeNull();
    expect(await c.auth.authenticate(second.sessionToken)).not.toBeNull();
    expect(c.repos.data.audit.map((e) => e.action)).toContain(AuditEvent.PASSWORD_CHANGED);
  });
});



========== FILE: apps/api/src/auth/auth.service.ts ==========

import { generateToken, hashToken, type PasswordHasher } from '@impactalign/auth';
import { AuditEvent } from '@impactalign/types';
import type { LoginInput, RegisterInput } from '@impactalign/validation';
import { HttpError } from '../common/http-error';
import {
  DuplicateEmailError,
  type OneTimeTokenKind,
  type Repositories,
  type SessionRecord,
  type UserRecord,
} from '../data/repositories';
import type { AuditService } from './audit.service';
import type { EmailSender } from './email';

export interface RequestContext {
  ipAddress?: string | undefined;
  userAgent?: string | undefined;
  requestId?: string | undefined;
}

export interface AuthServiceOptions {
  sessionIdleMs: number;
  sessionAbsoluteMs: number;
  verificationTtlMs: number;
  resetTtlMs: number;
  maxFailedAttempts: number;
  lockoutWindowMs: number;
  webUrl: string;
}

export interface AuthDeps {
  repos: Repositories;
  hasher: PasswordHasher;
  email: EmailSender;
  audit: AuditService;
  clock: () => Date;
  options: AuthServiceOptions;
}

export interface LoginResult {
  user: UserRecord;
  sessionToken: string;
  csrfToken: string;
  expiresAt: Date;
}
export interface AuthContext {
  user: UserRecord;
  session: SessionRecord;
}

const INVALID_TOKEN = () =>
  new HttpError(400, 'INVALID_OR_EXPIRED_TOKEN', 'This link is invalid or has expired');

export class AuthService {
  private dummyHash: Promise<string> | undefined;

  constructor(private readonly deps: AuthDeps) {}

  /** Same response whether or not the email exists (no account enumeration). */
  async register(input: RegisterInput, ctx: RequestContext): Promise<void> {
    const { repos, email, audit, hasher } = this.deps;
    const passwordHash = await hasher.hash(input.password); // always hash: equal timing either way
    const exists = async () => {
      await email.sendAccountExists({ to: input.email });
      await audit.record({
        action: AuditEvent.USER_REGISTERED,
        outcome: 'failure',
        requestId: ctx.requestId,
        ipAddress: ctx.ipAddress,
        metadata: { reason: 'email_exists' },
      });
    };

    if (await repos.users.findByEmail(input.email)) return exists();
    let user: UserRecord;
    try {
      user = await repos.users.create({
        email: input.email,
        displayName: input.displayName,
        passwordHash,
        roles: [input.accountType],
      });
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof DuplicateEmailError) return exists();
      throw err;
    }
    await this.issueToken(
      'email_verification',
      user,
      this.deps.options.verificationTtlMs,
      'verify-email',
    );
    await audit.record({
      action: AuditEvent.USER_REGISTERED,
      outcome: 'success',
      actorId: user.id,
      requestId: ctx.requestId,
      ipAddress: ctx.ipAddress,
    });
  }

  async verifyEmail(token: string, ctx: RequestContext): Promise<void> {
    const { repos, audit, clock } = this.deps;
    const record = await repos.tokens.consume('email_verification', hashToken(token), clock());
    if (!record) throw INVALID_TOKEN();
    await repos.users.setEmailVerified(record.userId, clock());
    await audit.record({
      action: AuditEvent.EMAIL_VERIFIED,
      outcome: 'success',
      actorId: record.userId,
      requestId: ctx.requestId,
      ipAddress: ctx.ipAddress,
    });
  }

  async login(input: LoginInput, ctx: RequestContext): Promise<LoginResult> {
    const { repos, hasher, clock, options } = this.deps;
    const now = clock();
    const failures = await repos.loginAttempts.countFailedSince(
      input.email,
      new Date(now.getTime() - options.lockoutWindowMs),
    );
    if (failures >= options.maxFailedAttempts) {
      await this.auditLogin('failure', null, ctx, 'locked');
      throw new HttpError(429, 'TOO_MANY_ATTEMPTS', 'Too many failed attempts. Try again later.');
    }

    const user = await repos.users.findByEmail(input.email);
    // Verify against a dummy hash for unknown emails so timing does not reveal which emails exist.
    const passwordOk = await hasher.verify(
      input.password,
      user?.passwordHash ?? (await this.getDummyHash()),
    );
    if (!user || !passwordOk || user.status !== 'active') {
      await repos.loginAttempts.record({
        email: input.email,
        success: false,
        ipAddress: ctx.ipAddress,
        at: now,
      });
      await this.auditLogin('failure', user?.id ?? null, ctx, 'invalid_credentials');
      throw new HttpError(401, 'INVALID_CREDENTIALS', 'Invalid email or password');
    }
    if (!user.emailVerifiedAt) {
      await this.auditLogin('failure', user.id, ctx, 'email_not_verified');
      throw new HttpError(403, 'EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED', 'Verify your email before signing in');
    }

    await repos.loginAttempts.clearFailed(input.email);
    await repos.loginAttempts.record({
      email: input.email,
      success: true,
      ipAddress: ctx.ipAddress,
      at: now,
    });
    if (hasher.needsRehash(user.passwordHash)) {
      await repos.users.setPasswordHash(user.id, await hasher.hash(input.password));
    }
    const session = await this.createSession(user.id, ctx);
    await this.auditLogin('success', user.id, ctx);
    return { user, ...session };
  }

  /** Resolves a session cookie value to the signed-in user, or null when missing/expired/revoked. */
  async authenticate(sessionToken: string | undefined): Promise<AuthContext | null> {
    if (!sessionToken || sessionToken.length > 200) return null;
    const { repos, clock, options } = this.deps;
    const session = await repos.sessions.findByTokenHash(hashToken(sessionToken));
    if (!session || session.revokedAt) return null;

    const now = clock();
    if (now >= session.absoluteExpiresAt || now >= session.idleExpiresAt) {
      await repos.sessions.revoke(session.id, now);
      return null;
    }
    const user = await repos.users.findById(session.userId);
    if (!user || user.status !== 'active') return null;

    if (now.getTime() - session.lastSeenAt.getTime() > 60_000) {
      const idle = new Date(
        Math.min(now.getTime() + options.sessionIdleMs, session.absoluteExpiresAt.getTime()),
      );
      await repos.sessions.touch(session.id, now, idle);
    }
    return { user, session };
  }

  async logout(auth: AuthContext, ctx: RequestContext): Promise<void> {
    await this.deps.repos.sessions.revoke(auth.session.id, this.deps.clock());
    await this.deps.audit.record({
      action: AuditEvent.LOGOUT,
      outcome: 'success',
      actorId: auth.user.id,
      requestId: ctx.requestId,
      ipAddress: ctx.ipAddress,
    });
  }

  /** Always succeeds from the caller's point of view (no account enumeration). */
  async forgotPassword(email: string): Promise<void> {
    const user = await this.deps.repos.users.findByEmail(email);
    if (user)
      await this.issueToken('password_reset', user, this.deps.options.resetTtlMs, 'reset-password');
  }

  async resetPassword(token: string, newPassword: string, ctx: RequestContext): Promise<void> {
    const { repos, hasher, audit, clock } = this.deps;
    const record = await repos.tokens.consume('password_reset', hashToken(token), clock());
    const user = record ? await repos.users.findById(record.userId) : null;
    if (!record || !user) throw INVALID_TOKEN();

    await repos.users.setPasswordHash(user.id, await hasher.hash(newPassword));
    if (!user.emailVerifiedAt) await repos.users.setEmailVerified(user.id, clock()); // proved mailbox control
    await repos.sessions.revokeAllForUser(user.id, clock());
    await repos.loginAttempts.clearFailed(user.email);
    await audit.record({
      action: AuditEvent.PASSWORD_RESET,
      outcome: 'success',
      actorId: user.id,
      requestId: ctx.requestId,
      ipAddress: ctx.ipAddress,
    });
  }

  async changePassword(
    auth: AuthContext,
    currentPassword: string,
    newPassword: string,
    ctx: RequestContext,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const { repos, hasher, audit, clock } = this.deps;
    if (!(await hasher.verify(currentPassword, auth.user.passwordHash))) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'INVALID_CURRENT_PASSWORD', 'Current password is incorrect');
    }
    await repos.users.setPasswordHash(auth.user.id, await hasher.hash(newPassword));
    await repos.sessions.revokeAllForUser(auth.user.id, clock(), auth.session.id);
    await audit.record({
      action: AuditEvent.PASSWORD_CHANGED,
      outcome: 'success',
      actorId: auth.user.id,
      requestId: ctx.requestId,
      ipAddress: ctx.ipAddress,
    });
  }

  private getDummyHash(): Promise<string> {
    this.dummyHash ??= this.deps.hasher.hash(generateToken().token);
    return this.dummyHash;
  }

  private async createSession(userId: string, ctx: RequestContext) {
    const { repos, clock, options } = this.deps;
    const now = clock();
    const { token, hash } = generateToken();
    const absolute = new Date(now.getTime() + options.sessionAbsoluteMs);
    const idle = new Date(Math.min(now.getTime() + options.sessionIdleMs, absolute.getTime()));
    const csrfToken = generateToken().token;
    await repos.sessions.create({
      userId,
      tokenHash: hash,
      csrfToken,
      lastSeenAt: now,
      idleExpiresAt: idle,
      absoluteExpiresAt: absolute,
      ipAddress: ctx.ipAddress,
      userAgent: ctx.userAgent,
    });
    return { sessionToken: token, csrfToken, expiresAt: absolute };
  }

  private async issueToken(
    kind: OneTimeTokenKind,
    user: UserRecord,
    ttlMs: number,
    path: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const { repos, email, clock, options } = this.deps;
    await repos.tokens.invalidateAllForUser(kind, user.id, clock());
    const { token, hash } = generateToken();
    await repos.tokens.create(kind, {
      userId: user.id,
      tokenHash: hash,
      expiresAt: new Date(clock().getTime() + ttlMs),
    });
    const link = `${options.webUrl}/${path}?token=${token}`;
    if (kind === 'email_verification') await email.sendVerification({ to: user.email, link });
    else await email.sendPasswordReset({ to: user.email, link });
  }

  private auditLogin(
    outcome: 'success' | 'failure',
    actorId: string | null,
    ctx: RequestContext,
    reason?: string,
  ) {
    return this.deps.audit.record({
      action: outcome === 'success' ? AuditEvent.LOGIN_SUCCESS : AuditEvent.LOGIN_FAILED,
      outcome,
      actorId,
      requestId: ctx.requestId,
      ipAddress: ctx.ipAddress,
      ...(reason ? { metadata: { reason } } : {}),
    });
  }
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/auth/bootstrap.test.ts ==========

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createScryptHasher } from '@impactalign/auth';
import { createMemoryRepositories } from '../testing/memory-repositories';
import { createSuperAdmin } from './bootstrap';

const hasher = createScryptHasher({ log2N: 4 });

describe('createSuperAdmin', () => {
  it('creates a verified SUPER_ADMIN with a hashed password and an audit entry', async () => {
    const repos = createMemoryRepositories();
    const user = await createSuperAdmin(repos, hasher, {
      email: 'Root@Example.org',
      password: 'correct-horse-battery-9',
    });
    const stored = repos.data.users[0]!;
    expect(user.email).toBe('root@example.org');
    expect(stored.roles).toEqual(['SUPER_ADMIN']);
    expect(stored.emailVerifiedAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect(stored.passwordHash).not.toContain('correct-horse');
    expect(repos.data.audit[0]).toMatchObject({
      action: 'ROLE_CHANGED',
      metadata: { bootstrap: true },
    });
  });

  it('refuses to touch an existing account and rejects weak passwords', async () => {
    const repos = createMemoryRepositories();
    await createSuperAdmin(repos, hasher, {
      email: 'root@example.org',
      password: 'correct-horse-battery-9',
    });
    await expect(
      createSuperAdmin(repos, hasher, {
        email: 'root@example.org',
        password: 'another-strong-pass-1',
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/already exists/);
    await expect(
      createSuperAdmin(repos, hasher, { email: 'x@example.org', password: 'short' }),
    ).rejects.toThrow();
    expect(repos.data.users).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});



========== FILE: apps/api/src/auth/bootstrap.ts ==========

import type { PasswordHasher } from '@impactalign/auth';
import { AuditEvent } from '@impactalign/types';
import { emailSchema, passwordSchema } from '@impactalign/validation';
import type { Repositories, UserRecord } from '../data/repositories';

/**
 * Creates the first SUPER_ADMIN (there is deliberately no HTTP endpoint for this). Refuses to touch an
 * existing account, so it cannot be used to take over or reset someone else's login.
 */
export async function createSuperAdmin(
  repos: Repositories,
  hasher: PasswordHasher,
  input: { email: string; password: string; displayName?: string },
): Promise<UserRecord> {
  const email = emailSchema.parse(input.email);
  const password = passwordSchema.parse(input.password);
  if (await repos.users.findByEmail(email))
    throw new Error('An account with this email already exists');

  const user = await repos.users.create({
    email,
    displayName: input.displayName ?? 'Super Admin',
    passwordHash: await hasher.hash(password),
    roles: ['SUPER_ADMIN'],
  });
  await repos.users.setEmailVerified(user.id, new Date());
  await repos.audit.insert({
    action: AuditEvent.ROLE_CHANGED,
    outcome: 'success',
    actorId: null,
    resourceType: 'user',
    resourceId: user.id,
    metadata: { bootstrap: true, after: ['SUPER_ADMIN'] },
  });
  return { ...user, emailVerifiedAt: new Date() };
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/auth/cookies.ts ==========

import type { Response } from 'express';
import type { ApiEnv } from '@impactalign/config';

export interface CookieSettings {
  name: string;
  secure: boolean;
  sameSite: 'lax' | 'strict' | 'none';
}

/**
 * Session cookie: HttpOnly (no JavaScript access), Secure in production, and in production it uses
 * the __Host- prefix (forces Secure, Path=/, no Domain) so subdomains cannot overwrite it.
 */
export function cookieSettings(env: Pick<ApiEnv, 'NODE_ENV' | 'COOKIE_SAME_SITE'>): CookieSettings {
  const secure = env.NODE_ENV === 'production';
  return {
    name: secure ? '__Host-ia_session' : 'ia_session',
    secure,
    sameSite: env.COOKIE_SAME_SITE,
  };
}

const baseOptions = (s: CookieSettings) =>
  ({ httpOnly: true, secure: s.secure, sameSite: s.sameSite, path: '/' }) as const;

export function setSessionCookie(
  res: Response,
  s: CookieSettings,
  token: string,
  expires: Date,
): void {
  res.cookie(s.name, token, { ...baseOptions(s), expires });
}

export function clearSessionCookie(res: Response, s: CookieSettings): void {
  res.clearCookie(s.name, baseOptions(s));
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/auth/email.ts ==========

import type { AppLogger } from '../common/logger';

export interface EmailSender {
  sendVerification(message: { to: string; link: string }): Promise<void>;
  sendPasswordReset(message: { to: string; link: string }): Promise<void>;
  sendAccountExists(message: { to: string }): Promise<void>;
}

/** Development only: prints emails (with their secret links) to the log. Blocked in production. */
export class ConsoleEmailSender implements EmailSender {
  constructor(private readonly logger: AppLogger) {}
  async sendVerification(message: { to: string; link: string }): Promise<void> {
    this.logger.info({ ...message, kind: 'verify-email' }, 'DEV EMAIL');
  }
  async sendPasswordReset(message: { to: string; link: string }): Promise<void> {
    this.logger.info({ ...message, kind: 'reset-password' }, 'DEV EMAIL');
  }
  async sendAccountExists(message: { to: string }): Promise<void> {
    this.logger.info({ ...message, kind: 'account-exists' }, 'DEV EMAIL');
  }
}

/** Used when EMAIL_TRANSPORT=disabled: sends nothing. */
export class NoopEmailSender implements EmailSender {
  async sendVerification(): Promise<void> {}
  async sendPasswordReset(): Promise<void> {}
  async sendAccountExists(): Promise<void> {}
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/auth/middleware.ts ==========

import type { Request, RequestHandler } from 'express';
import { hasPlatformPermission, safeEqual } from '@impactalign/auth';
import type { Permission } from '@impactalign/types';
import { HttpError } from '../common/http-error';
import type { AuthService, RequestContext } from './auth.service';
import { clearSessionCookie, type CookieSettings } from './cookies';

const SAFE_METHODS = new Set(['GET', 'HEAD', 'OPTIONS']);

export function requestContext(req: Request): RequestContext {
  return {
    ipAddress: req.ip,
    userAgent: req.get('user-agent')?.slice(0, 300),
    requestId: typeof req.id === 'string' ? req.id : undefined,
  };
}

/** Loads the signed-in user (if any) from the session cookie into req.auth. */
export function authenticate(auth: AuthService, cookie: CookieSettings): RequestHandler {
  return async (req, res, next) => {
    const token: unknown = req.cookies?.[cookie.name];
    if (typeof token === 'string') {
      const context = await auth.authenticate(token);
      if (context) req.auth = context;
      else clearSessionCookie(res, cookie); // stale cookie: drop it
    }
    next();
  };
}

export const requireAuth: RequestHandler = (req, _res, next) =>
  req.auth ? next() : next(new HttpError(401, 'UNAUTHENTICATED', 'Sign in required'));

/** Platform-level permission check (server-side; never trust the client's idea of its role). */
export function requirePermission(permission: Permission): RequestHandler {
  return (req, _res, next) => {
    if (!req.auth) return next(new HttpError(401, 'UNAUTHENTICATED', 'Sign in required'));
    if (!hasPlatformPermission(req.auth.user.roles, permission)) {
      return next(new HttpError(403, 'FORBIDDEN', 'You do not have permission to do this'));
    }
    next();
  };
}

/**
 * CSRF protection for cookie sessions: every state-changing request from a signed-in user must send
 * the session's CSRF token in X-CSRF-Token (a cross-site page cannot read or set it).
 */
export const csrfGuard: RequestHandler = (req, _res, next) => {
  if (SAFE_METHODS.has(req.method) || !req.auth) return next();
  const sent = req.get('x-csrf-token');
  if (!sent || !safeEqual(sent, req.auth.session.csrfToken)) {
    return next(new HttpError(403, 'CSRF_FAILED', 'Missing or invalid CSRF token'));
  }
  next();
};

/** Rejects browser requests from origins that are not on the allow-list. */
export function originGuard(allowedOrigins: ReadonlySet<string>): RequestHandler {
  return (req, _res, next) => {
    const origin = req.get('origin');
    if (!SAFE_METHODS.has(req.method) && origin && !allowedOrigins.has(origin)) {
      return next(new HttpError(403, 'ORIGIN_NOT_ALLOWED', 'Origin not allowed'));
    }
    next();
  };
}

/** Bodies must be JSON: blocks form-style cross-site posts (which cannot send JSON without CORS). */
export const requireJson: RequestHandler = (req, _res, next) =>
  req.is('application/json') === false
    ? next(new HttpError(415, 'UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE', 'Send the body as application/json'))
    : next();

import type { PublicUser } from '@impactalign/types';
import type { UserRecord } from '../data/repositories';

/** The only way a user leaves the API: no password hash, no internal fields. */
export function toPublicUser(user: UserRecord): PublicUser {
  return {
    id: user.id,
    email: user.email,
    displayName: user.displayName,
    roles: user.roles,
    emailVerified: user.emailVerifiedAt !== null,
    createdAt: user.createdAt.toISOString(),
  };
