========== FILE: apps/api/src/common/error-handler.ts ==========

import type { ErrorRequestHandler, RequestHandler } from 'express';
import type { ApiErrorBody, ApiErrorDetail } from '@impactalign/types';
import { HttpError } from './http-error';

function requestIdOf(req: { id?: unknown }): string {
  return typeof req.id === 'string' ? req.id : 'unknown';
}

function send(
  res: Parameters<RequestHandler>[1],
  req: Parameters<RequestHandler>[0],
  status: number,
  code: string,
  message: string,
  details?: ApiErrorDetail[],
): void {
  const body: ApiErrorBody = {
    error: { code, message, requestId: requestIdOf(req), ...(details ? { details } : {}) },
  };
  res.status(status).json(body);
}

export const notFoundHandler: RequestHandler = (req, res) => {
  send(res, req, 404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Route not found');
};

/**
 * Final error handler. Known client errors get a stable code; anything else is a 500 whose
 * details are logged server-side only — stack traces and messages never reach the client.
 */
export const errorHandler: ErrorRequestHandler = (err: unknown, req, res, _next) => {
  if (res.headersSent) return;

  if (err instanceof HttpError) {
    send(res, req, err.status, err.code, err.message, err.details);
    return;
  }

  const type = (err as { type?: string } | null)?.type;
  if (type === 'entity.too.large') {
    send(res, req, 413, 'PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE', 'Request body is too large');
    return;
  }
  if (type === 'entity.parse.failed') {
    send(res, req, 400, 'INVALID_JSON', 'Request body is not valid JSON');
    return;
  }

  req.log.error({ err }, 'Unhandled error');
  send(res, req, 500, 'INTERNAL_ERROR', 'An unexpected error occurred');
};



========== FILE: apps/api/src/common/http-error.ts ==========

import type { ApiErrorDetail } from '@impactalign/types';

/** Error that is safe to show to API clients (message is public). */
export class HttpError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;
  readonly code: string;
  readonly details: ApiErrorDetail[] | undefined;

  constructor(status: number, code: string, message: string, details?: ApiErrorDetail[]) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'HttpError';
    this.status = status;
    this.code = code;
    this.details = details;
  }

  static notFound(message = 'Resource not found'): HttpError {
    return new HttpError(404, 'NOT_FOUND', message);
  }
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/common/logger.ts ==========

import pino, { type Logger } from 'pino';
import type { ApiEnv } from '@impactalign/config';

export type AppLogger = Logger;

/** Structured JSON logger. Credentials and cookies are redacted from every log line. */
export function createLogger(env: Pick<ApiEnv, 'LOG_LEVEL' | 'NODE_ENV'>): AppLogger {
  return pino({
    level: env.NODE_ENV === 'test' ? 'silent' : env.LOG_LEVEL,
    base: { service: 'impactalign-api' },
    redact: {
      paths: [
        'req.headers.authorization',
        'req.headers.cookie',
        'res.headers["set-cookie"]',
        '*.password',
        '*.token',
        '*.secret',
      ],
      censor: '[REDACTED]',
    },
  });
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/common/rate-limit.ts ==========

import { rateLimit } from 'express-rate-limit';
import type { RequestHandler } from 'express';
import type { ApiErrorBody } from '@impactalign/types';

/** Per-IP limiter returning the standard error envelope. In-memory: per instance (see docs). */
export function createRateLimiter(options: { windowMs: number; limit: number }): RequestHandler {
  return rateLimit({
    windowMs: options.windowMs,
    limit: options.limit,
    standardHeaders: 'draft-8',
    legacyHeaders: false,
    handler: (req, res) => {
      const body: ApiErrorBody = {
        error: {
          code: 'RATE_LIMITED',
          message: 'Too many requests',
          requestId: typeof req.id === 'string' ? req.id : 'unknown',
        },
      };
      res.status(429).json(body);
    },
  });
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/common/validate.ts ==========

import type { RequestHandler } from 'express';
import { HttpError } from './http-error';

/** Anything with Zod's safeParse shape (keeps this file free of a direct zod dependency). */
export interface BodySchema<T> {
  safeParse(
    data: unknown,
  ):
    | { success: true; data: T }
    | {
        success: false;
        error: { issues: { path: PropertyKey[]; message: string }[] };
      };
}

export function validateBody<T>(schema: BodySchema<T>): RequestHandler {
  return (req, _res, next) => {
    const result = schema.safeParse(req.body);

    if (result.success === false) {
      const details = result.error.issues.map((issue) => ({
        path: issue.path.map(String).join('.'),
        message: issue.message,
      }));

      return next(
        new HttpError(400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Invalid request', details),
      );
    }

    req.body = result.data;
    next();
  };
}

/** Route parameter must be a valid id; anything else is reported as "not found" (no probing). */
export function validateIdParam(
  name: string,
  isValid: (value: string) => boolean,
): RequestHandler {
  return (req, _res, next) => {
    const value = req.params[name];
    return typeof value === 'string' && isValid(value)
      ? next()
      : next(HttpError.notFound());
  };
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/common/with-timeout.ts ==========

/** Rejects if `promise` does not settle within `ms`. The timer never keeps the process alive. */
export async function withTimeout<T>(promise: Promise<T>, ms: number, label: string): Promise<T> {
  let timer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined;
  const timeout = new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
    timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`${label} timed out after ${ms}ms`)), ms);
    timer.unref();
  });
  try {
    return await Promise.race([promise, timeout]);
  } finally {
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/config/env.ts ==========

import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { parseApiEnv, type ApiEnv } from '@impactalign/config';

/**
 * Loads a local .env file for development/test only. In production the platform
 * (container orchestrator / secret manager) must inject real environment variables.
 */
function loadLocalEnvFile(): void {
  if (process.env['NODE_ENV'] === 'production') return;
  for (const candidate of [
    path.resolve(process.cwd(), '.env'),
    path.resolve(process.cwd(), '../../.env'),
  ]) {
    if (existsSync(candidate)) {
      process.loadEnvFile(candidate);
      return;
    }
  }
}

export function loadEnv(source: Record<string, string | undefined> = process.env): ApiEnv {
  if (source === process.env) loadLocalEnvFile();
  return parseApiEnv(source);
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/data/mongo-repositories.test.ts ==========

import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { EmailVerification, LoginAttempt, Session, User } from '@impactalign/database';
import { DuplicateEmailError } from './repositories';
import { createMongoRepositories } from './mongo-repositories';

afterEach(() => vi.restoreAllMocks());

const resolved = (value: unknown) => (() => Promise.resolve(value)) as never;
const hasTrustedOperator = (value: unknown): boolean =>
  typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(value).length > 0;

describe('mongo repositories (query contracts; the live database is exercised by the smoke test)', () => {
  it('does not query when an id is not a valid ObjectId', async () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(User, 'findOne');
    const repos = createMongoRepositories();
    expect(await repos.users.findById('not-an-id')).toBeNull();
    expect(await repos.users.findById('123456789012')).toBeNull(); // 12 chars: valid for Mongoose, not for us
    expect(await repos.sessions.revokeAllForUser('nope', new Date())).toBe(0);
    expect(spy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('maps a duplicate-key error to DuplicateEmailError', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(User, 'create').mockImplementation((() => Promise.reject({ code: 11000 })) as never);
    await expect(
      createMongoRepositories().users.create({
        email: 'a@b.co',
        displayName: 'Ab',
        passwordHash: 'h',
        roles: ['PUBLIC_USER'],
      }),
    ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(DuplicateEmailError);
  });

  it('consumes a one-time token atomically, with trusted operators and a null usedAt guard', async () => {
    const spy = vi
      .spyOn(EmailVerification, 'findOneAndUpdate')
      .mockImplementation((() => ({ lean: () => Promise.resolve(null) })) as never);
    const result = await createMongoRepositories().tokens.consume(
      'email_verification',
      'hash',
      new Date(),
    );
    expect(result).toBeNull();
    const filter = spy.mock.calls[0]![0] as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
    expect(filter['usedAt']).toBeNull();
    expect(filter['tokenHash']).toBe('hash');
    expect(hasTrustedOperator(filter['expiresAt'])).toBe(true);
  });

  it('counts only failed attempts inside the window, with a trusted operator', async () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(LoginAttempt, 'countDocuments').mockImplementation(resolved(3));
    expect(
      await createMongoRepositories().loginAttempts.countFailedSince('a@b.co', new Date()),
    ).toBe(3);
    const filter = spy.mock.calls[0]![0] as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
    expect(filter['success']).toBe(false);
    expect(hasTrustedOperator(filter['createdAt'])).toBe(true);
  });

  it('revokes every other session but keeps the excepted one (trusted $ne)', async () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(Session, 'updateMany').mockImplementation(resolved({ modifiedCount: 2 }));
    const keep = new Types.ObjectId().toHexString();
    const user = new Types.ObjectId().toHexString();
    expect(await createMongoRepositories().sessions.revokeAllForUser(user, new Date(), keep)).toBe(
      2,
    );
    const filter = spy.mock.calls[0]![0] as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
    expect(filter['revokedAt']).toBeNull();
    expect(hasTrustedOperator(filter['_id'])).toBe(true);
  });
});



========== FILE: apps/api/src/data/mongo-repositories.ts ==========

import { Types, trusted } from 'mongoose';
import type { OrganizationRole, PlatformRole } from '@impactalign/types';
import {
  AuditLog,
  EmailVerification,
  LoginAttempt,
  Organization,
  OrganizationMember,
  PasswordResetToken,
  Session,
  User,
  SDG,
  SDGTarget,
  SDGIndicator,
} from '@impactalign/database';
import {
  DuplicateEmailError,
  type OneTimeTokenKind,
  type MemberRecord,
  type OneTimeTokenRecord,
  type OrganizationRecord,
  type Repositories,
  type SessionRecord,
  type UserRecord,
  type SdgRecord,
  type SdgTargetRecord,
  type SdgIndicatorRecord,

} from './repositories';

/** Strict check: 24 hex chars that round-trip (rejects 12-character strings Mongoose would accept). */
const isId = (id: string): boolean =>
  Types.ObjectId.isValid(id) && new Types.ObjectId(id).toHexString() === id;
const optionalId = (id: string | null | undefined) =>
  id && isId(id) ? new Types.ObjectId(id) : null;

interface LeanUser {
  _id: Types.ObjectId;
  email: string;
  displayName: string;
  passwordHash: string;
  roles: string[];
  status: string;
  emailVerifiedAt?: Date | null;
  createdAt: Date;
}
interface LeanSession {
  _id: Types.ObjectId;
  userId: Types.ObjectId;
  tokenHash: string;
  csrfToken: string;
  lastSeenAt: Date;
  idleExpiresAt: Date;
  absoluteExpiresAt: Date;
  revokedAt?: Date | null;
  ipAddress?: string | null;
  userAgent?: string | null;
}
interface LeanOrganization {
  _id: Types.ObjectId;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  createdAt: Date;
}
interface LeanMember {
  organizationId: Types.ObjectId;
  userId: Types.ObjectId;
  role: string;
}
interface LeanToken {
  _id: Types.ObjectId;
  userId: Types.ObjectId;
  tokenHash: string;
  expiresAt: Date;
  usedAt?: Date | null;
}

interface LeanSdg {
  _id: Types.ObjectId;
  number: number;
  code: string;
  title?: string | null;
  description?: string | null;
  officialUrl?: string | null;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  createdAt: Date;
  updatedAt: Date;
}

interface LeanSdgTarget {
  _id: Types.ObjectId;
  sdgId: Types.ObjectId;
  code: string;
  title?: string | null;
  description?: string | null;
  officialUrl?: string | null;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  createdAt: Date;
  updatedAt: Date;
}

interface LeanSdgIndicator {
  _id: Types.ObjectId;
  sdgId: Types.ObjectId;
  targetId?: Types.ObjectId | null;
  code: string;
  title?: string | null;
  description?: string | null;
  officialUrl?: string | null;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  createdAt: Date;
  updatedAt: Date;
}

const toUser = (d: LeanUser): UserRecord => ({
  id: d._id.toHexString(),
  email: d.email,
  displayName: d.displayName,
  passwordHash: d.passwordHash,
  roles: d.roles as PlatformRole[],
  status: d.status === 'disabled' ? 'disabled' : 'active',
  emailVerifiedAt: d.emailVerifiedAt ?? null,
  createdAt: d.createdAt,
});

const toSession = (d: LeanSession): SessionRecord => ({
  id: d._id.toHexString(),
  userId: d.userId.toHexString(),
  tokenHash: d.tokenHash,
  csrfToken: d.csrfToken,
  lastSeenAt: d.lastSeenAt,
  idleExpiresAt: d.idleExpiresAt,
  absoluteExpiresAt: d.absoluteExpiresAt,
  revokedAt: d.revokedAt ?? null,
  ipAddress: d.ipAddress ?? undefined,
  userAgent: d.userAgent ?? undefined,
});

const toOrganization = (d: LeanOrganization): OrganizationRecord => ({
  id: d._id.toHexString(),
  name: d.name,
  slug: d.slug,
  createdAt: d.createdAt,
});

const toSdg = (d: LeanSdg): SdgRecord => ({
  id: d._id.toHexString(),
  number: d.number,
  code: d.code,
  title: d.title ?? null,
  description: d.description ?? null,
  officialUrl: d.officialUrl ?? null,
  metadata: d.metadata ?? null,
  createdAt: d.createdAt,
  updatedAt: d.updatedAt,
});

const toSdgTarget = (d: LeanSdgTarget): SdgTargetRecord => ({
  id: d._id.toHexString(),
  sdgId: d.sdgId.toHexString(),
  code: d.code,
  title: d.title ?? null,
  description: d.description ?? null,
  officialUrl: d.officialUrl ?? null,
  metadata: d.metadata ?? null,
  createdAt: d.createdAt,
  updatedAt: d.updatedAt,
});

const toSdgIndicator = (d: LeanSdgIndicator): SdgIndicatorRecord => ({
  id: d._id.toHexString(),
  sdgId: d.sdgId.toHexString(),
  targetId: d.targetId?.toHexString() ?? null,
  code: d.code,
  title: d.title ?? null,
  description: d.description ?? null,
  officialUrl: d.officialUrl ?? null,
  metadata: d.metadata ?? null,
  createdAt: d.createdAt,
  updatedAt: d.updatedAt,
});

const tokenModel = (kind: OneTimeTokenKind) =>
  (kind === 'email_verification'
    ? EmailVerification
    : PasswordResetToken) as typeof EmailVerification;

const isDuplicateKey = (err: unknown): boolean => (err as { code?: number } | null)?.code === 11000;

/**
 * MongoDB implementation of the repositories. Note: the connection uses sanitizeFilter, so every
 * filter that legitimately needs an operator ($gt, $ne, ...) is wrapped in trusted().
 */
export function createMongoRepositories(): Repositories {
  return {
    users: {
      async findByEmail(email) {
        const doc = await User.findOne({ email, deletedAt: null })
          .select('+passwordHash')
          .lean<LeanUser>();
        return doc ? toUser(doc) : null;
      },
      async findById(id) {
        if (!isId(id)) return null;
        const doc = await User.findOne({ _id: id, deletedAt: null })
          .select('+passwordHash')
          .lean<LeanUser>();
        return doc ? toUser(doc) : null;
      },
      async create(data) {
        try {
          const doc = await User.create({
            email: data.email,
            displayName: data.displayName,
            passwordHash: data.passwordHash,
            roles: data.roles,
          });
          return toUser(doc.toObject() as unknown as LeanUser);
        } catch (err) {
          if (isDuplicateKey(err)) throw new DuplicateEmailError();
          throw err;
        }
      },
      async setEmailVerified(id, at) {
        if (isId(id)) await User.updateOne({ _id: id }, { $set: { emailVerifiedAt: at } });
      },
      async setPasswordHash(id, passwordHash) {
        if (isId(id)) await User.updateOne({ _id: id }, { $set: { passwordHash } });
      },
      async setDisplayName(id, displayName) {
        if (isId(id)) await User.updateOne({ _id: id }, { $set: { displayName } });
      },
      async setRoles(id, roles) {
        if (isId(id)) await User.updateOne({ _id: id }, { $set: { roles } });
      },
    },

    sessions: {
      async create(data) {
        const doc = await Session.create({
          ...data,
          userId: new Types.ObjectId(data.userId),
          revokedAt: null,
        });
        return toSession(doc.toObject() as unknown as LeanSession);
      },
      async findByTokenHash(tokenHash) {
        const doc = await Session.findOne({ tokenHash }).lean<LeanSession>();
        return doc ? toSession(doc) : null;
      },
      async touch(id, lastSeenAt, idleExpiresAt) {
        if (isId(id)) await Session.updateOne({ _id: id }, { $set: { lastSeenAt, idleExpiresAt } });
      },
      async revoke(id, at) {
        if (isId(id))
          await Session.updateOne({ _id: id, revokedAt: null }, { $set: { revokedAt: at } });
      },
      async revokeAllForUser(userId, at, exceptId) {
        if (!isId(userId)) return 0;
        const filter = {
          userId,
          revokedAt: null,
          ...(exceptId && isId(exceptId)
            ? { _id: trusted({ $ne: new Types.ObjectId(exceptId) }) }
            : {}),
        };
        const result = await Session.updateMany(filter, { $set: { revokedAt: at } });
        return result.modifiedCount;
      },
    },

    tokens: {
      async create(kind, data) {
        await tokenModel(kind).create({ ...data, userId: new Types.ObjectId(data.userId) });
      },
      /** Atomic: only one caller can flip usedAt from null, so a link works exactly once. */
      async consume(kind, tokenHash, now): Promise<OneTimeTokenRecord | null> {
        const doc = await tokenModel(kind)
          .findOneAndUpdate(
            { tokenHash, usedAt: null, expiresAt: trusted({ $gt: now }) },
            { $set: { usedAt: now } },
          )
          .lean<LeanToken>();
        if (!doc) return null;
        return {
          id: doc._id.toHexString(),
          userId: doc.userId.toHexString(),
          tokenHash: doc.tokenHash,
          expiresAt: doc.expiresAt,
          usedAt: now,
        };
      },
      async invalidateAllForUser(kind, userId, now) {
        if (isId(userId)) {
          await tokenModel(kind).updateMany({ userId, usedAt: null }, { $set: { usedAt: now } });
        }
      },
    },

    loginAttempts: {
      async record({ email, success, ipAddress, at }) {
        await LoginAttempt.create({ email, success, ipAddress, createdAt: at });
      },
      async countFailedSince(email, since) {
        return LoginAttempt.countDocuments({
          email,
          success: false,
          createdAt: trusted({ $gte: since }),
        });
      },
      async clearFailed(email) {
        await LoginAttempt.deleteMany({ email, success: false });
      },
    },

    audit: {
      async insert(entry) {
        await AuditLog.create({
          action: entry.action,
          outcome: entry.outcome,
          actorId: optionalId(entry.actorId),
          organizationId: optionalId(entry.organizationId),
          resourceType: entry.resourceType,
          resourceId: entry.resourceId,
          requestId: entry.requestId,
          ipAddress: entry.ipAddress,
          metadata: entry.metadata,
        });
      },
    },

    organizations: {
      async create(data) {
        const doc = await Organization.create({
          name: data.name,
          slug: data.slug,
          createdById: new Types.ObjectId(data.createdById),
        });
        return toOrganization(doc.toObject() as unknown as LeanOrganization);
      },
      async rename(id, name) {
        if (isId(id)) await Organization.updateOne({ _id: id }, { $set: { name } });
      },
      async remove(id) {
        if (!isId(id)) return;
        await OrganizationMember.deleteMany({ organizationId: id });
        await Organization.deleteOne({ _id: id });
      },
      async addMember(organizationId, userId, role) {
        await OrganizationMember.create({
          organizationId: new Types.ObjectId(organizationId),
          userId: new Types.ObjectId(userId),
          role,
        });
      },
      async findById(id) {
        if (!isId(id)) return null;
        const doc = await Organization.findOne({
          _id: id,
          deletedAt: null,
        }).lean<LeanOrganization>();
        return doc ? toOrganization(doc) : null;
      },
      async findMembership(organizationId, userId) {
        if (!isId(organizationId) || !isId(userId)) return null;
        const doc = await OrganizationMember.findOne({ organizationId, userId }).lean<LeanMember>();
        return doc ? { organizationId, userId, role: doc.role as OrganizationRole } : null;
      },
      async listForUser(userId) {
        if (!isId(userId)) return [];
        const members = await OrganizationMember.find({ userId }).lean<LeanMember[]>();
        if (members.length === 0) return [];
        const orgs = await Organization.find({
          _id: trusted({ $in: members.map((m) => m.organizationId) }),
          deletedAt: null,
        }).lean<LeanOrganization[]>();
        return members.flatMap((m) => {
          const org = orgs.find((o) => o._id.equals(m.organizationId));
          return org
            ? [{ organization: toOrganization(org), role: m.role as OrganizationRole }]
            : [];
        });
      },
      async listMembers(organizationId): Promise<MemberRecord[]> {
        if (!isId(organizationId)) return [];
        const members = await OrganizationMember.find({ organizationId }).lean<LeanMember[]>();
        const users = await User.find({ _id: trusted({ $in: members.map((m) => m.userId) }) })
          .select('displayName')
          .lean<{ _id: Types.ObjectId; displayName: string }[]>();
        return members.map((m) => ({
          userId: m.userId.toHexString(),
          displayName: users.find((u) => u._id.equals(m.userId))?.displayName ?? 'Unknown',
          role: m.role as OrganizationRole,
        }));
      },
    },
    sdgs: {
      async listSDGs() {
        const docs = await SDG.find({ deletedAt: null })
          .sort({ number: 1 })
          .lean<LeanSdg[]>();

        return docs.map(toSdg);
      },

      async findSDGByNumber(number) {
        const doc = await SDG.findOne({
          number,
          deletedAt: null,
        }).lean<LeanSdg>();

        return doc ? toSdg(doc) : null;
      },

      async listTargetsBySdgId(sdgId) {
        if (!isId(sdgId)) return [];

        const docs = await SDGTarget.find({
          sdgId: new Types.ObjectId(sdgId),
          deletedAt: null,
        })
          .sort({ code: 1 })
          .lean<LeanSdgTarget[]>();

        return docs.map(toSdgTarget);
      },

      async listIndicatorsBySdgId(sdgId) {
        if (!isId(sdgId)) return [];

        const docs = await SDGIndicator.find({
          sdgId: new Types.ObjectId(sdgId),
          deletedAt: null,
        })
          .sort({ code: 1 })
          .lean<LeanSdgIndicator[]>();

        return docs.map(toSdgIndicator);
      },
    },
  };
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/data/mongo-sdg-repository.test.ts ==========

import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { SDG, SDGIndicator, SDGTarget } from '@impactalign/database';
import { createMongoRepositories } from './mongo-repositories';

afterEach(() => vi.restoreAllMocks());

/** Chainable, awaitable stand-in for a Mongoose query (.sort().lean() etc. all return itself). */
const query = (value: unknown): never => {
  const q: unknown = new Proxy(() => undefined, {
    get: (_target, prop) =>
      prop === 'then'
        ? (resolve: (v: unknown) => unknown, reject: (e: unknown) => unknown) =>
            Promise.resolve(value).then(resolve, reject)
        : () => q,
  });
  return q as never;
};

const firstFilter = (spy: { mock: { calls: unknown[][] } }) =>
  spy.mock.calls[0]![0] as Record<string, unknown>;

describe('mongo SDG repository: soft-deleted rows are never returned', () => {
  it('listSDGs filters deletedAt: null', async () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(SDG, 'find').mockImplementation((() => query([])) as never);
    await createMongoRepositories().sdgs.listSDGs();
    expect(firstFilter(spy)['deletedAt']).toBeNull();
  });

  it('findSDGByNumber filters deletedAt: null', async () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(SDG, 'findOne').mockImplementation((() => query(null)) as never);
    expect(await createMongoRepositories().sdgs.findSDGByNumber(1)).toBeNull();
    const filter = firstFilter(spy);
    expect(filter['number']).toBe(1);
    expect(filter['deletedAt']).toBeNull();
  });

  it('listTargetsBySdgId filters deletedAt: null', async () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(SDGTarget, 'find').mockImplementation((() => query([])) as never);
    await createMongoRepositories().sdgs.listTargetsBySdgId(new Types.ObjectId().toHexString());
    expect(firstFilter(spy)['deletedAt']).toBeNull();
  });

  it('listIndicatorsBySdgId filters deletedAt: null', async () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(SDGIndicator, 'find').mockImplementation((() => query([])) as never);
    await createMongoRepositories().sdgs.listIndicatorsBySdgId(new Types.ObjectId().toHexString());
    expect(firstFilter(spy)['deletedAt']).toBeNull();
  });
});

import type { AuditEvent, OrganizationRole, PlatformRole } from '@impactalign/types';

export class DuplicateEmailError extends Error {
  constructor() {
    super('Email already registered');
    this.name = 'DuplicateEmailError';
  }
}

export interface UserRecord {

  email: string;
  displayName: string;
  passwordHash: string;

  createdAt: Date;
}

  passwordHash: string;
  roles: PlatformRole[];
}

========== FILE: apps/api/src/health/health.router.ts ==========

import { Router } from 'express';
import type { HealthCheckResult } from '@impactalign/types';
import type { HealthService } from './health.service';

const statusCodeFor = (result: HealthCheckResult): number => (result.status === 'ok' ? 200 : 503);

/** GET /health, /health/database, /health/redis — unauthenticated and rate-limit exempt. */
export function createHealthRouter(service: HealthService): Router {
  const router = Router();

  router.use((_req, res, next) => {
    res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-store');
    next();
  });

  router.get('/', (_req, res) => {
    res.json(service.liveness());
  });

  router.get('/database', async (_req, res) => {
    const result = await service.checkDatabase();
    res.status(statusCodeFor(result)).json(result);
  });

  router.get('/redis', async (_req, res) => {
    const result = await service.checkRedis();
    res.status(statusCodeFor(result)).json(result);
  });

  return router;
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/health/health.service.ts ==========

import type { HealthCheckResult, LivenessResult } from '@impactalign/types';
import type { AppLogger } from '../common/logger';
import { withTimeout } from '../common/with-timeout';

export interface HealthProbes {
  pingDatabase(): Promise<void>;
  pingRedis(): Promise<void>;
}

export interface HealthServiceOptions {
  readonly serviceName: string;
  readonly version: string;
  readonly timeoutMs: number;
  readonly logger: AppLogger;
}

export class HealthService {
  private readonly startedAt = Date.now();

  constructor(
    private readonly probes: HealthProbes,
    private readonly options: HealthServiceOptions,
  ) {}

  /** Liveness: the process is up and able to serve requests. Touches no dependencies. */
  liveness(): LivenessResult {
    return {
      status: 'ok',
      check: 'liveness',
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      service: this.options.serviceName,
      version: this.options.version,
      uptimeSeconds: Math.floor((Date.now() - this.startedAt) / 1000),
    };
  }

  checkDatabase(): Promise<HealthCheckResult> {
    return this.probe('database', () => this.probes.pingDatabase());
  }

  checkRedis(): Promise<HealthCheckResult> {
    return this.probe('redis', () => this.probes.pingRedis());
  }

  private async probe(
    check: 'database' | 'redis',
    run: () => Promise<void>,
  ): Promise<HealthCheckResult> {
    const started = performance.now();
    try {
      await withTimeout(run(), this.options.timeoutMs, `${check} health probe`);
      return {
        status: 'ok',
        check,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        latencyMs: Math.round(performance.now() - started),
      };
    } catch (err) {
      // Details go to the server log only; the response never exposes hostnames or errors.
      this.options.logger.warn({ err, check }, 'Health probe failed');
      return { status: 'down', check, timestamp: new Date().toISOString() };
    }
  }
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/health/health.test.ts ==========

import request from 'supertest';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { healthCheckResultSchema, livenessResultSchema } from '@impactalign/validation';
import { buildTestApp } from '../test-utils';

describe('GET /health', () => {
  it('returns liveness without touching dependencies', async () => {
    const app = buildTestApp({
      pingDatabase: () => Promise.reject(new Error('db down')),
      pingRedis: () => Promise.reject(new Error('redis down')),
    });
    const res = await request(app).get('/health');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(livenessResultSchema.parse(res.body).status).toBe('ok');
    expect(res.headers['cache-control']).toBe('no-store');
  });
});

describe.each([
  ['/health/database', 'database', 'pingDatabase'],
  ['/health/redis', 'redis', 'pingRedis'],
] as const)('GET %s', (path, check, probeName) => {
  it('returns 200 with latency when the dependency responds', async () => {
    const res = await request(buildTestApp()).get(path);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    const body = healthCheckResultSchema.parse(res.body);
    expect(body).toMatchObject({ status: 'ok', check });
    expect(body.latencyMs).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
  });

  it('returns 503 and leaks no error details when the dependency fails', async () => {
    const app = buildTestApp({
      [probeName]: () =>
        Promise.reject(new Error('connect ECONNREFUSED 10.0.0.5:27017 secret-host')),
    });
    const res = await request(app).get(path);
    expect(res.status).toBe(503);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({ status: 'down', check });
    expect(JSON.stringify(res.body)).not.toMatch(/ECONNREFUSED|10\.0\.0\.5|secret-host/);
  });

  it('returns 503 when the dependency hangs past the timeout', async () => {
    const app = buildTestApp({ [probeName]: () => new Promise<void>(() => undefined) });
    const res = await request(app).get(path);
    expect(res.status).toBe(503);
    expect(res.body.status).toBe('down');
  });
});



========== FILE: apps/api/src/infrastructure/mongo.ts ==========

import { connectMongo } from '@impactalign/database';
import type { AppLogger } from '../common/logger';

/**
 * Connects to MongoDB in the background and keeps retrying until it succeeds, so the API
 * (and its /health endpoints) stays up and reports `down` instead of crash-looping.
 * Returns a function that stops further retries.
 */
export function connectMongoWithRetry(uri: string, logger: AppLogger, retryMs = 5_000): () => void {
  let stopped = false;
  let timer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined;

  const attempt = async (): Promise<void> => {
    try {
      await connectMongo({ uri });
      logger.info('MongoDB connection ready');
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error({ err }, 'MongoDB connection failed; retrying');
      if (!stopped) {
        timer = setTimeout(() => void attempt(), retryMs);
        timer.unref();
      }
    }
  };
  void attempt();

  return () => {
    stopped = true;
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
  };
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/infrastructure/redis.ts ==========

import { Redis } from 'ioredis';
import type { AppLogger } from '../common/logger';

/**
 * Shared Redis client. It reconnects automatically and never crashes the process when
 * Redis is down; commands fail fast (no offline queue) so health checks report accurately.
 * Connection errors are logged once per outage (not on every retry) to keep logs readable.
 */
export function createRedisClient(url: string, logger: AppLogger): Redis {
  const client = new Redis(url, {
    enableOfflineQueue: false,
    maxRetriesPerRequest: 1,
    connectTimeout: 2_000,
    retryStrategy: (attempt) => Math.min(attempt * 200, 5_000),
  });

  let failing = false;
  client.on('error', (err: NodeJS.ErrnoException) => {
    if (failing) return;
    failing = true;
    logger.warn(
      { code: err.code, message: err.message || 'connection failed' },
      'Redis unavailable (retrying in the background; further errors are not logged until it recovers)',
    );
  });
  client.on('ready', () => {
    failing = false;
    logger.info('Redis connection ready');
  });
  return client;
}

export async function pingRedis(client: Redis): Promise<void> {
  if (client.status !== 'ready') throw new Error(`Redis not ready (status: ${client.status})`);
  const reply = await client.ping();
  if (reply !== 'PONG') throw new Error('Unexpected Redis PING reply');
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/main.ts ==========

import { disconnectMongo, pingMongo } from '@impactalign/database';
import { createApp } from './app';
import { ConsoleEmailSender, NoopEmailSender } from './auth/email';
import { createLogger } from './common/logger';
import { loadEnv } from './config/env';
import { createMongoRepositories } from './data/mongo-repositories';
import { HealthService } from './health/health.service';
import { connectMongoWithRetry } from './infrastructure/mongo';
import { createRedisClient, pingRedis } from './infrastructure/redis';
import { createServices } from './services';

const SERVICE_VERSION = process.env['npm_package_version'] ?? '0.1.0';

function main(): void {
  const env = loadEnv();
  const logger = createLogger(env);

  const stopMongoRetry = connectMongoWithRetry(env.MONGODB_URI, logger);
  const redis = createRedisClient(env.REDIS_URL, logger);

  const healthService = new HealthService(
    { pingDatabase: pingMongo, pingRedis: () => pingRedis(redis) },
    {
      serviceName: 'impactalign-api',
      version: SERVICE_VERSION,
      timeoutMs: env.HEALTH_CHECK_TIMEOUT_MS,
      logger,
    },
  );

  const services = createServices({
    env,
    logger,
    repos: createMongoRepositories(),
    email:
      env.EMAIL_TRANSPORT === 'console' ? new ConsoleEmailSender(logger) : new NoopEmailSender(),
  });

  const app = createApp({ env, logger, healthService, services });
  // const server = app.listen(env.API_PORT, env.API_HOST, () => {
  //   logger.info({ port: env.API_PORT, env: env.NODE_ENV }, 'API listening');
  // });
  const port = Number(process.env.PORT) || env.API_PORT;

const server = app.listen(port, env.API_HOST, () => {
  logger.info({ port, env: env.NODE_ENV }, 'API listening');
});

  let shuttingDown = false;
  const shutdown = (signal: string): void => {
    if (shuttingDown) return;
    shuttingDown = true;
    logger.info({ signal }, 'Shutting down');
    stopMongoRetry();
    const force = setTimeout(() => process.exit(1), 10_000);
    force.unref();
    server.close(() => {
      void Promise.allSettled([disconnectMongo(), redis.quit()]).then(() => process.exit(0));
    });
  };
  process.on('SIGTERM', () => shutdown('SIGTERM'));
  process.on('SIGINT', () => shutdown('SIGINT'));
}

try {
  main();
} catch (err) {
  // Env validation errors list variable names only — never values.
  console.error(err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);
  process.exit(1);
}

const healthResponse = (description: string) => ({
  description,
  content: { 'application/json': { schema: { $ref: '#/components/schemas/HealthCheckResult' } } },
});

export const openApiDocument = {
  openapi: '3.1.0',
  info: {
    title: 'ImpactAlign SDG API',
    version: '0.1.0',
    description: 'Phase 1 foundation: health endpoints only.',
  },
  servers: [{ url: 'http://localhost:4000' }],
  tags: [{ name: 'Health', description: 'Liveness and dependency checks (unauthenticated)' }],
  paths: {
    '/health': {
      get: {
        tags: ['Health'],
        summary: 'Liveness',
        responses: {
          '200': {
            description: 'Service is running',
            content: {
              'application/json': { schema: { $ref: '#/components/schemas/LivenessResult' } },
            },
          },
        },
      },
    },
    '/health/database': {
      get: {
        tags: ['Health'],
        summary: 'MongoDB connectivity',
        responses: {
          '200': healthResponse('MongoDB reachable'),
          '503': healthResponse('MongoDB unreachable'),
        },
      },
    },
    '/health/redis': {
      get: {
        tags: ['Health'],
        summary: 'Redis connectivity',
        responses: {
          '200': healthResponse('Redis reachable'),
          '503': healthResponse('Redis unreachable'),
        },
      },
    },
  },
  components: {
    schemas: {
      HealthCheckResult: {
        type: 'object',
        required: ['status', 'check', 'timestamp'],
        properties: {
          status: { type: 'string', enum: ['ok', 'down'] },
          check: { type: 'string', enum: ['liveness', 'database', 'redis'] },
          timestamp: { type: 'string', format: 'date-time' },
          latencyMs: { type: 'number', minimum: 0 },
        },
      },
      LivenessResult: {
        allOf: [
          { $ref: '#/components/schemas/HealthCheckResult' },
          {
            type: 'object',
            required: ['service', 'version', 'uptimeSeconds'],
            properties: {
              service: { type: 'string' },
              version: { type: 'string' },
              uptimeSeconds: { type: 'number', minimum: 0 },
            },
          },
        ],
      },
      ApiError: {
        type: 'object',
        required: ['error'],
        properties: {
          error: {
            type: 'object',
            required: ['code', 'message', 'requestId'],
            properties: {
              code: { type: 'string' },
              message: { type: 'string' },
              requestId: { type: 'string' },
            },
          },
        },
      },
    },
  },
} as const;



========== FILE: apps/api/src/organizations/organizations.router.ts ==========

import { Router } from 'express';
import { createOrganizationSchema } from '@impactalign/validation';
import { Permission } from '@impactalign/types';
import { requestContext, requirePermission, requireAuth } from '../auth/middleware';
import { validateBody } from '../common/validate';
import type { OrganizationsService } from './organizations.service';

export function createOrganizationsRouter(organizations: OrganizationsService): Router {
  const router = Router();
  router.use(requireAuth);

  router.post(
    '/',
    requirePermission(Permission.ORG_CREATE),
    validateBody(createOrganizationSchema),
    async (req, res) => {
      res.status(201).json({
        organization: await organizations.create(
          req.auth!.user,
          req.body.name,
          requestContext(req),
        ),
      });
    },
  );

  router.get('/', async (req, res) => {
    res.json({ organizations: await organizations.listMine(req.auth!.user) });
  });

  router.get('/:orgId', async (req, res) => {
    res.json({
      organization: await organizations.get(req.auth!.user, String(req.params['orgId'])),
    });
  });

  router.get('/:orgId/members', async (req, res) => {
    res.json({
      members: await organizations.listMembers(req.auth!.user, String(req.params['orgId'])),
    });
  });

  router.patch('/:orgId', validateBody(createOrganizationSchema), async (req, res) => {
    res.json({
      organization: await organizations.rename(
        req.auth!.user,
        String(req.params['orgId']),
        req.body.name,
      ),
    });
  });

  return router;
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/organizations/organizations.service.ts ==========

import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { hasOrganizationPermission } from '@impactalign/auth';
import {
  AuditEvent,
  Permission,
  type OrganizationMemberView,
  type OrganizationRole,
  type PublicOrganization,
} from '@impactalign/types';
import type { AuditService } from '../auth/audit.service';
import type { RequestContext } from '../auth/auth.service';
import { HttpError } from '../common/http-error';
import type { OrganizationRecord, Repositories, UserRecord } from '../data/repositories';

const ID_PATTERN = /^[a-f\d]{24}$/i;

export const slugify = (name: string): string =>
  name
    .toLowerCase()
    .normalize('NFKD')
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '')
    .slice(0, 40) || 'organization';

const view = (org: OrganizationRecord, role: OrganizationRole): PublicOrganization => ({
  id: org.id,
  name: org.name,
  slug: org.slug,
  createdAt: org.createdAt.toISOString(),
  role,
});

/**
 * Organization isolation: membership is the ONLY way to reach an organization's data. A non-member
 * gets the same 404 as for an organization that does not exist, so nothing can be probed, and
 * platform roles (even SUPER_ADMIN) grant no access through these routes.
 */
export class OrganizationsService {
  constructor(private readonly deps: { repos: Repositories; audit: AuditService }) {}

  async create(actor: UserRecord, name: string, ctx: RequestContext): Promise<PublicOrganization> {
    const { repos, audit } = this.deps;
    const org = await repos.organizations.create({
      name,
      slug: `${slugify(name)}-${randomBytes(3).toString('hex')}`,
      createdById: actor.id,
    });
    try {
      await repos.organizations.addMember(org.id, actor.id, 'ORGANIZATION_ADMIN');
    } catch (err) {
      await repos.organizations.remove(org.id); // no organization without an admin
      throw err;
    }
    await audit.record({
      action: AuditEvent.ORGANIZATION_CREATED,
      outcome: 'success',
      actorId: actor.id,
      organizationId: org.id,
      resourceType: 'organization',
      resourceId: org.id,
      requestId: ctx.requestId,
      ipAddress: ctx.ipAddress,
    });
    return view(org, 'ORGANIZATION_ADMIN');
  }

  async listMine(actor: UserRecord): Promise<PublicOrganization[]> {
    const rows = await this.deps.repos.organizations.listForUser(actor.id);
    return rows.map((row) => view(row.organization, row.role));
  }

  async get(actor: UserRecord, orgId: string): Promise<PublicOrganization> {
    const { org, role } = await this.access(actor, orgId, Permission.ORG_READ);
    return view(org, role);
  }

  /** Display names and roles only: never email addresses. */
  async listMembers(actor: UserRecord, orgId: string): Promise<OrganizationMemberView[]> {
    await this.access(actor, orgId, Permission.ORG_READ);
    return this.deps.repos.organizations.listMembers(orgId);
  }

  async rename(actor: UserRecord, orgId: string, name: string): Promise<PublicOrganization> {
    const { org, role } = await this.access(actor, orgId, Permission.ORG_UPDATE);
    await this.deps.repos.organizations.rename(org.id, name);
    return view({ ...org, name }, role);
  }

  private async access(actor: UserRecord, orgId: string, permission: Permission) {
    const notFound = () => HttpError.notFound('Organization not found');
    if (!ID_PATTERN.test(orgId)) throw notFound();
    const membership = await this.deps.repos.organizations.findMembership(orgId, actor.id);
    if (!membership) throw notFound();
    const org = await this.deps.repos.organizations.findById(orgId);
    if (!org) throw notFound();
    if (!hasOrganizationPermission(membership.role, permission)) {
      throw new HttpError(403, 'FORBIDDEN', 'You do not have permission to do this');
    }
    return { org, role: membership.role };
  }
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/scripts/create-super-admin.ts ==========

import { createScryptHasher } from '@impactalign/auth';
import { connectMongo, disconnectMongo } from '@impactalign/database';
import { createSuperAdmin } from '../auth/bootstrap';
import { loadEnv } from '../config/env';
import { createMongoRepositories } from '../data/mongo-repositories';

async function run(): Promise<void> {
  const email = process.env['SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL'];
  const password = process.env['SUPER_ADMIN_PASSWORD'];
  if (!email || !password) throw new Error('Set SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL and SUPER_ADMIN_PASSWORD first');

  const env = loadEnv();
  await connectMongo({ uri: env.MONGODB_URI });
  try {
    const user = await createSuperAdmin(createMongoRepositories(), createScryptHasher(), {
      email,
      password,
    });
    process.stdout.write(`SUPER_ADMIN created: ${user.email}\n`);
  } finally {
    await disconnectMongo();
  }
}

run().catch((err: unknown) => {
  console.error(err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);
  process.exit(1);
});



========== FILE: apps/api/src/sdgs/sdgs.router.ts ==========

import { Router, type Request, type RequestHandler, type Response } from 'express';
import type { SdgDto, SdgIndicatorDto, SdgTargetDto } from '@impactalign/types';
import { sdgNumberParamSchema } from '@impactalign/validation';
import type { SdgIndicatorRecord, SdgRecord, SdgTargetRecord } from '../data/repositories';
import type { SdgsService } from './sdgs.service';

const toSdg = (r: SdgRecord): SdgDto => ({
  id: r.id,
  number: r.number,
  code: r.code,
  title: r.title,
  description: r.description,
  officialUrl: r.officialUrl,
});

const toTarget = (r: SdgTargetRecord): SdgTargetDto => ({
  id: r.id,
  sdgId: r.sdgId,
  code: r.code,
  title: r.title,
  description: r.description,
  officialUrl: r.officialUrl,
});

const toIndicator = (r: SdgIndicatorRecord): SdgIndicatorDto => ({
  id: r.id,
  sdgId: r.sdgId,
  targetId: r.targetId,
  code: r.code,
  title: r.title,
  description: r.description,
  officialUrl: r.officialUrl,
});

const route =
  (fn: (req: Request, res: Response) => Promise<void>): RequestHandler =>
  (req, res, next) => {
    fn(req, res).catch(next);
  };

/** Validates :id and loads the SDG; sends 400/404 itself and returns null when it cannot proceed. */
async function resolveSdg(
  service: SdgsService,
  req: Request,
  res: Response,
): Promise<SdgRecord | null> {
  const params = sdgNumberParamSchema.safeParse(req.params);
  if (!params.success) {
    res.status(400).json({
      error: {
        code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR',
        message: 'Invalid request',
        details: params.error.issues.map((i) => ({ path: i.path.join('.'), message: i.message })),
      },
    });
    return null;
  }
  const sdg = await service.findSDGByNumber(params.data.id);
  if (!sdg) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: { code: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'SDG not found' } });
    return null;
  }
  return sdg;
}

/** Read-only SDG catalogue. Mounted at /api/v1/sdgs. */
export function createSdgsRouter(service: SdgsService): Router {
  const router = Router();

  router.get(
    '/',
    route(async (_req, res) => {
      const rows = await service.listSDGs();
      res.json({ data: rows.map(toSdg) });
    }),
  );

  router.get(
    '/:id',
    route(async (req, res) => {
      const sdg = await resolveSdg(service, req, res);
      if (sdg) res.json({ data: toSdg(sdg) });
    }),
  );

  router.get(
    '/:id/targets',
    route(async (req, res) => {
      const sdg = await resolveSdg(service, req, res);
      if (!sdg) return;
      const rows = await service.listTargetsBySdgId(sdg.id);
      res.json({ data: rows.map(toTarget) });
    }),
  );

  router.get(
    '/:id/indicators',
    route(async (req, res) => {
      const sdg = await resolveSdg(service, req, res);
      if (!sdg) return;
      const rows = await service.listIndicatorsBySdgId(sdg.id);
      res.json({ data: rows.map(toIndicator) });
    }),
  );

  return router;
}


========== FILE: apps/api/src/sdgs/sdgs.routes.test.ts ==========

import express from 'express';
import request from 'supertest';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { SdgIndicatorRecord, SdgRecord, SdgRepository, SdgTargetRecord } from '../data/repositories';
import { createSdgsRouter } from './sdgs.router';
import { SdgsService } from './sdgs.service';

const now = new Date('2026-01-01T00:00:00Z');
const base = { title: null, description: null, officialUrl: null, metadata: null, createdAt: now, updatedAt: now };

const sdgs: SdgRecord[] = Array.from({ length: 17 }, (_, i) => ({
  ...base,
  id: `sdg-${i + 1}`,
  number: i + 1,
  code: `SDG-${String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`,
}));
const targets: SdgTargetRecord[] = [{ ...base, id: 't1', sdgId: 'sdg-1', code: '1.1' }];
const indicators: SdgIndicatorRecord[] = [
  { ...base, id: 'i1', sdgId: 'sdg-1', targetId: 't1', code: '1.1.1' },
];

const repo: SdgRepository = {
  listSDGs: async () => sdgs,
  findSDGByNumber: async (n) => sdgs.find((s) => s.number === n) ?? null,
  listTargetsBySdgId: async (id) => targets.filter((t) => t.sdgId === id),
  listIndicatorsBySdgId: async (id) => indicators.filter((i) => i.sdgId === id),
};

const app = express();
app.use('/api/v1/sdgs', createSdgsRouter(new SdgsService(repo)));

describe('SDG routes', () => {
  it('lists all 17 SDGs with null placeholders', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/v1/sdgs');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.data).toHaveLength(17);
    expect(res.body.data[0]).toMatchObject({ number: 1, code: 'SDG-01', title: null });
  });

  it('does not leak internal fields', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/v1/sdgs/1');
    expect(res.body.data).not.toHaveProperty('metadata');
    expect(res.body.data).not.toHaveProperty('createdAt');
  });

  it('returns one SDG by number', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/v1/sdgs/17');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.data.number).toBe(17);
  });

  it.each(['0', '18', 'abc', '01'])('returns 400 for id %s', async (id) => {
    const res = await request(app).get(`/api/v1/sdgs/${id}`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect(res.body.error.code).toBe('VALIDATION_ERROR');
  });

  it('returns targets and indicators of an SDG', async () => {
    const t = await request(app).get('/api/v1/sdgs/1/targets');
    expect(t.status).toBe(200);
    expect(t.body.data).toHaveLength(1);
    const i = await request(app).get('/api/v1/sdgs/1/indicators');
    expect(i.body.data[0]).toMatchObject({ code: '1.1.1', targetId: 't1' });
  });

  it('returns empty lists for an SDG without targets yet', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/v1/sdgs/2/targets');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.data).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('is read-only', async () => {
    for (const method of ['post', 'put', 'patch', 'delete'] as const) {
      const res = await request(app)[method]('/api/v1/sdgs/1');
      expect(res.status).toBe(404);
    }
  });
});

========== FILE: apps/api/src/services.ts ==========

import { createScryptHasher, type PasswordHasher } from '@impactalign/auth';
import type { ApiEnv } from '@impactalign/config';
import { AuditService } from './auth/audit.service';
import { AuthService, type AuthServiceOptions } from './auth/auth.service';
import { cookieSettings, type CookieSettings } from './auth/cookies';
import type { EmailSender } from './auth/email';
import type { AppLogger } from './common/logger';
import type { Repositories } from './data/repositories';
import { OrganizationsService } from './organizations/organizations.service';
import { UsersService } from './users/users.service';
import { SdgsService } from './sdgs/sdgs.service';

export interface Services {
  auth: AuthService;
  audit: AuditService;
  cookie: CookieSettings;
  users: UsersService;
  organizations: OrganizationsService;
  sdgs: SdgsService;
}

export function authOptionsFromEnv(env: ApiEnv): AuthServiceOptions {
  return {
    sessionIdleMs: env.SESSION_IDLE_MINUTES * 60_000,
    sessionAbsoluteMs: env.SESSION_ABSOLUTE_HOURS * 3_600_000,
    verificationTtlMs: 24 * 3_600_000,
    resetTtlMs: 60 * 60_000,
    maxFailedAttempts: env.AUTH_MAX_FAILED_ATTEMPTS,
    lockoutWindowMs: env.AUTH_LOCKOUT_MINUTES * 60_000,
    webUrl: env.APP_WEB_URL.replace(/\/$/, ''),
  };
}

export interface ServiceDeps {
  env: ApiEnv;
  logger: AppLogger;
  repos: Repositories;
  email: EmailSender;
  hasher?: PasswordHasher;
  clock?: () => Date;
}

/** Wires repositories, email and policy into the application services. */
export function createServices(deps: ServiceDeps): Services {
  const audit = new AuditService(deps.repos.audit, deps.logger);
  const auth = new AuthService({
    repos: deps.repos,
    hasher: deps.hasher ?? createScryptHasher(),
    email: deps.email,
    audit,
    clock: deps.clock ?? (() => new Date()),
    options: authOptionsFromEnv(deps.env),
  });
  return {
    auth,
    audit,
    cookie: cookieSettings(deps.env),
    users: new UsersService({ repos: deps.repos, audit }),
    organizations: new OrganizationsService({ repos: deps.repos, audit }),
    sdgs: new SdgsService(deps.repos.sdgs),
  };
}

========== FILE: apps/api/src/testing/auth-context.ts ==========

import { createScryptHasher } from '@impactalign/auth';
import { AuditService } from '../auth/audit.service';
import { AuthService, type AuthServiceOptions } from '../auth/auth.service';
import type { EmailSender } from '../auth/email';
import { createLogger } from '../common/logger';
import { OrganizationsService } from '../organizations/organizations.service';
import { UsersService } from '../users/users.service';
import { createMemoryRepositories } from './memory-repositories';

export interface SentEmail {
  kind: 'verify' | 'reset' | 'exists';
  to: string;
  link?: string;
}

export class RecordingEmailSender implements EmailSender {
  readonly sent: SentEmail[] = [];
  async sendVerification(m: { to: string; link: string }) {
    this.sent.push({ kind: 'verify', ...m });
  }
  async sendPasswordReset(m: { to: string; link: string }) {
    this.sent.push({ kind: 'reset', ...m });
  }
  async sendAccountExists(m: { to: string }) {
    this.sent.push({ kind: 'exists', ...m });
  }
  /** Token from the most recent email of this kind sent to this address. */
  tokenFor(kind: SentEmail['kind'], to: string): string {
    const mail = [...this.sent].reverse().find((e) => e.kind === kind && e.to === to);
    const token = mail?.link ? new URL(mail.link).searchParams.get('token') : null;
    if (!token) throw new Error(`No ${kind} email for ${to}`);
    return token;
  }
}

export const STRONG_PASSWORD = 'correct-horse-battery-9';

const defaultOptions: AuthServiceOptions = {
  sessionIdleMs: 60 * 60_000,
  sessionAbsoluteMs: 24 * 60 * 60_000,
  verificationTtlMs: 24 * 60 * 60_000,
  resetTtlMs: 60 * 60_000,
  maxFailedAttempts: 3,
  lockoutWindowMs: 15 * 60_000,
  webUrl: 'http://localhost:3000',
};

/** Fully wired AuthService on in-memory data, with a controllable clock and cheap password hashing. */
export function buildAuthTestContext(overrides: Partial<AuthServiceOptions> = {}) {
  const repos = createMemoryRepositories();
  const email = new RecordingEmailSender();
  const logger = createLogger({ NODE_ENV: 'test', LOG_LEVEL: 'silent' });
  // Start at the real current time so cookie jars (which use the real clock) keep the cookies.
  const clock = { now: new Date() };
  const hasher = createScryptHasher({ log2N: 4 });
  const audit = new AuditService(repos.audit, logger);
  const auth = new AuthService({
    repos,
    hasher,
    email,
    audit,
    clock: () => clock.now,
    options: { ...defaultOptions, ...overrides },
  });
  const advance = (ms: number) => {
    clock.now = new Date(clock.now.getTime() + ms);
  };
  const ctx = { ipAddress: '203.0.113.7', requestId: 'req-test-1' };

  /** Registers, verifies and returns a signed-in user. */
  async function signUp(address: string, password = STRONG_PASSWORD) {
    await auth.register(
      { email: address, password, displayName: 'Test User', accountType: 'PROFESSIONAL' },
      ctx,
    );
    await auth.verifyEmail(email.tokenFor('verify', address), ctx);
    return auth.login({ email: address, password }, ctx);
  }
  const users = new UsersService({ repos, audit });
  const organizations = new OrganizationsService({ repos, audit });
  return { auth, audit, users, organizations, repos, email, hasher, clock, advance, ctx, signUp };
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/testing/memory-repositories.ts ==========

import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import {
  DuplicateEmailError,
  type AuditEntry,
  type MembershipRecord,
  type OrganizationRecord,
  type OneTimeTokenKind,
  type OneTimeTokenRecord,
  type Repositories,
  type SessionRecord,
  type UserRecord,
  type SdgRecord,
type SdgTargetRecord,
type SdgIndicatorRecord,
} from '../data/repositories';

/** In-memory repositories for tests. They mimic the atomic behaviour the real adapters must have. */
export interface MemoryRepositories extends Repositories {
  data: {
    users: UserRecord[];
    sessions: SessionRecord[];
    tokens: Record<OneTimeTokenKind, OneTimeTokenRecord[]>;
    attempts: { email: string; success: boolean; at: Date }[];
    audit: AuditEntry[];
    orgs: OrganizationRecord[];
    members: MembershipRecord[];
    sdgs: SdgRecord[];
sdgTargets: SdgTargetRecord[];
sdgIndicators: SdgIndicatorRecord[];
  };
}

/** 24 hex characters, like a MongoDB ObjectId, so id validation behaves as in production. */
const newId = () => randomBytes(12).toString('hex');

export function createMemoryRepositories(): MemoryRepositories {
  const data: MemoryRepositories['data'] = {
    users: [],
    sessions: [],
    tokens: { email_verification: [], password_reset: [] },
    attempts: [],
    audit: [],
    orgs: [],
    members: [],
    sdgs: [],
    sdgTargets: [],
    sdgIndicators: [],

  };
  const user = (id: string) => data.users.find((u) => u.id === id);
  const copy = (u: UserRecord): UserRecord => ({ ...u, roles: [...u.roles] });

  return {
    data,
    users: {
      async findByEmail(email) {
        const found = data.users.find((u) => u.email === email);
        return found ? copy(found) : null;
      },
      async findById(id) {
        const found = user(id);
        return found ? copy(found) : null;
      },
      async create(input) {
        if (data.users.some((u) => u.email === input.email)) throw new DuplicateEmailError();
        const record: UserRecord = {
          id: newId(),
          ...input,
          status: 'active',
          emailVerifiedAt: null,
          createdAt: new Date(),
        };
        data.users.push(record);
        return copy(record);
      },
      async setEmailVerified(id, at) {
        const u = user(id);
        if (u) u.emailVerifiedAt = at;
      },
      async setPasswordHash(id, passwordHash) {
        const u = user(id);
        if (u) u.passwordHash = passwordHash;
      },
      async setDisplayName(id, displayName) {
        const u = user(id);
        if (u) u.displayName = displayName;
      },
      async setRoles(id, roles) {
        const u = user(id);
        if (u) u.roles = roles;
      },
    },
    sessions: {
      async create(input) {
        const record: SessionRecord = { id: newId(), revokedAt: null, ...input };
        data.sessions.push(record);
        return record;
      },
      async findByTokenHash(tokenHash) {
        return data.sessions.find((s) => s.tokenHash === tokenHash) ?? null;
      },
      async touch(id, lastSeenAt, idleExpiresAt) {
        const s = data.sessions.find((x) => x.id === id);
        if (s) Object.assign(s, { lastSeenAt, idleExpiresAt });
      },
      async revoke(id, at) {
        const s = data.sessions.find((x) => x.id === id);
        if (s && !s.revokedAt) s.revokedAt = at;
      },
      async revokeAllForUser(userId, at, exceptId) {
        const active = data.sessions.filter(
          (s) => s.userId === userId && !s.revokedAt && s.id !== exceptId,
        );
        active.forEach((s) => (s.revokedAt = at));
        return active.length;
      },
    },
    tokens: {
      async create(kind, input) {
        data.tokens[kind].push({ id: newId(), usedAt: null, ...input });
      },
      async consume(kind, tokenHash, now) {
        const t = data.tokens[kind].find(
          (x) => x.tokenHash === tokenHash && !x.usedAt && x.expiresAt > now,
        );
        if (!t) return null;
        t.usedAt = now;
        return { ...t };
      },
      async invalidateAllForUser(kind, userId, now) {
        data.tokens[kind]
          .filter((t) => t.userId === userId && !t.usedAt)
          .forEach((t) => (t.usedAt = now));
      },
    },
    loginAttempts: {
      async record({ email, success, at }) {
        data.attempts.push({ email, success, at });
      },
      async countFailedSince(email, since) {
        return data.attempts.filter((a) => a.email === email && !a.success && a.at >= since).length;
      },
      async clearFailed(email) {
        data.attempts = data.attempts.filter((a) => !(a.email === email && !a.success));
      },
    },
    audit: {
      async insert(entry) {
        data.audit.push(entry);
      },
    },
    organizations: {
      async create(input) {
        const record: OrganizationRecord = {
          id: newId(),
          name: input.name,
          slug: input.slug,
          createdAt: new Date(),
        };
        data.orgs.push(record);
        return record;
      },
      async rename(id, name) {
        const o = data.orgs.find((x) => x.id === id);
        if (o) o.name = name;
      },
      async remove(id) {
        data.orgs = data.orgs.filter((o) => o.id !== id);
        data.members = data.members.filter((m) => m.organizationId !== id);
      },
      async addMember(organizationId, userId, role) {
        if (data.members.some((m) => m.organizationId === organizationId && m.userId === userId)) {
          throw new Error('Already a member');
        }
        data.members.push({ organizationId, userId, role });
      },
      async findById(id) {
        return data.orgs.find((o) => o.id === id) ?? null;
      },
      async findMembership(organizationId, userId) {
        return (
          data.members.find((m) => m.organizationId === organizationId && m.userId === userId) ??
          null
        );
      },
      async listForUser(userId) {
        return data.members
          .filter((m) => m.userId === userId)
          .flatMap((m) => {
            const organization = data.orgs.find((o) => o.id === m.organizationId);
            return organization ? [{ organization, role: m.role }] : [];
          });
      },
      async listMembers(organizationId) {
        return data.members
          .filter((m) => m.organizationId === organizationId)
          .map((m) => ({
            userId: m.userId,
            displayName: user(m.userId)?.displayName ?? 'Unknown',
            role: m.role,
          }));
      },
    },

    sdgs: {
      async listSDGs() {
        return [...data.sdgs].sort((a, b) => a.number - b.number);
      },

      async findSDGByNumber(number) {
        return data.sdgs.find((sdg) => sdg.number === number) ?? null;
      },

      async listTargetsBySdgId(sdgId) {
        return data.sdgTargets
          .filter((target) => target.sdgId === sdgId)
          .sort((a, b) => a.code.localeCompare(b.code));
      },

      async listIndicatorsBySdgId(sdgId) {
        return data.sdgIndicators
          .filter((indicator) => indicator.sdgId === sdgId)
          .sort((a, b) => a.code.localeCompare(b.code));
      },
    },
  };
}

      auth?: AuthContext;
    }
  }
}
