========== FILE: apps/api/src/users/access-control.routes.test.ts ==========

import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import request from 'supertest';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { PlatformRole } from '@impactalign/types';
import { buildTestContext } from '../test-utils';
import { STRONG_PASSWORD } from '../testing/auth-context';

type Ctx = ReturnType<typeof buildTestContext>;

/** Creates a verified user with the given platform roles and returns a signed-in "browser". */
async function person(t: Ctx, name: string, roles: PlatformRole[] = ['PROFESSIONAL']) {
  const email = `${name}@example.org`;
  await t.signUp(email);
  const record = t.repos.data.users.find((u) => u.email === email)!;
  record.roles = roles; // set directly: the HTTP API (rightly) offers no way to pick staff roles at sign-up
  const agent = request.agent(t.app);
  const res = await agent.post('/api/v1/auth/login').send({ email, password: STRONG_PASSWORD });
  return { id: record.id, email, agent, csrf: res.body.csrfToken as string };
}
type Person = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof person>>;
const put = (p: Person, url: string, body: unknown) =>
  p.agent
    .put(url)
    .set('X-CSRF-Token', p.csrf)
    .send(body as object);
const post = (p: Person, url: string, body: unknown) =>
  p.agent
    .post(url)
    .set('X-CSRF-Token', p.csrf)
    .send(body as object);
const patch = (p: Person, url: string, body: unknown) =>
  p.agent
    .patch(url)
    .set('X-CSRF-Token', p.csrf)
    .send(body as object);
const fakeId = () => randomBytes(12).toString('hex');

describe('unauthorized access', () => {
  it.each([
    ['get', `/api/v1/users/${'a'.repeat(24)}`],
    ['put', `/api/v1/users/${'a'.repeat(24)}/roles`],
    ['patch', '/api/v1/users/me'],
    ['get', '/api/v1/organizations'],
    ['post', '/api/v1/organizations'],
    ['get', `/api/v1/organizations/${'a'.repeat(24)}`],
  ] as const)('%s %s without a session is 401', async (method, url) => {
    const { app } = buildTestContext();
    const res = await request(app)[method](url).send({});
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });
});

describe('cross-user access', () => {
  it('lets you read yourself but not another user', async () => {
    const t = buildTestContext();
    const ada = await person(t, 'ada');
    const bob = await person(t, 'bob');
    expect((await ada.agent.get(`/api/v1/users/${ada.id}`)).status).toBe(200);
    const other = await ada.agent.get(`/api/v1/users/${bob.id}`);
    expect(other.status).toBe(403);
    expect(JSON.stringify(other.body)).not.toContain('bob@example.org');
  });

  it('answers 403 for real and unknown ids alike (no probing for accounts)', async () => {
    const t = buildTestContext();
    const ada = await person(t, 'ada');
    const bob = await person(t, 'bob');
    const real = await ada.agent.get(`/api/v1/users/${bob.id}`);
    const unknown = await ada.agent.get(`/api/v1/users/${fakeId()}`);
    expect(unknown.status).toBe(real.status);
  });

  it('lets staff with USER_READ read others, and a malformed id is a 404', async () => {
    const t = buildTestContext();
    const admin = await person(t, 'admin', ['ADMIN']);
    const bob = await person(t, 'bob');
    expect((await admin.agent.get(`/api/v1/users/${bob.id}`)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await admin.agent.get('/api/v1/users/not-an-id')).status).toBe(404);
  });

  it('only changes your own display name — extra fields such as roles or id are rejected', async () => {
    const t = buildTestContext();
    const ada = await person(t, 'ada');
    const ok = await patch(ada, '/api/v1/users/me', { displayName: 'Ada L.' });
    expect(ok.status).toBe(200);
    expect(ok.body.user.displayName).toBe('Ada L.');
    for (const extra of [
      { roles: ['SUPER_ADMIN'] },
      { id: fakeId() },
      { email: 'x@example.org' },
    ]) {
      expect((await patch(ada, '/api/v1/users/me', { displayName: 'Ada', ...extra })).status).toBe(
        400,
      );
    }
    expect(t.repos.data.users.find((u) => u.id === ada.id)!.roles).toEqual(['PROFESSIONAL']);
  });
});

describe('privilege escalation', () => {
  it('stops an ordinary user from assigning roles to anyone, including themselves', async () => {
    const t = buildTestContext();
    const eve = await person(t, 'eve');
    const bob = await person(t, 'bob');
    expect(
      (await put(eve, `/api/v1/users/${eve.id}/roles`, { roles: ['SUPER_ADMIN'] })).status,
    ).toBe(403);
    expect((await put(eve, `/api/v1/users/${bob.id}/roles`, { roles: ['ADMIN'] })).status).toBe(
      403,
    );
    expect(t.repos.data.users.find((u) => u.id === eve.id)!.roles).toEqual(['PROFESSIONAL']);
  });

  it('requires the CSRF token for role changes', async () => {
    const t = buildTestContext();
    const admin = await person(t, 'admin', ['ADMIN']);
    const bob = await person(t, 'bob');
    const res = await admin.agent
      .put(`/api/v1/users/${bob.id}/roles`)
      .send({ roles: ['ASSESSOR'] });
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
    expect(res.body.error.code).toBe('CSRF_FAILED');
  });

  it('lets an ADMIN assign ordinary roles, records before/after, and applies them at once', async () => {
    const t = buildTestContext();
    const admin = await person(t, 'admin', ['ADMIN']);
    const bob = await person(t, 'bob');
    const res = await put(admin, `/api/v1/users/${bob.id}/roles`, { roles: ['ASSESSOR'] });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.user.roles).toEqual(['ASSESSOR']);
    expect((await bob.agent.get('/api/v1/auth/me')).body.user.roles).toEqual(['ASSESSOR']); // live session sees it
    const entry = t.repos.data.audit.find(
      (e) => e.action === 'ROLE_CHANGED' && e.outcome === 'success',
    )!;
    expect(entry.actorId).toBe(admin.id);
    expect(entry.metadata).toMatchObject({ before: ['PROFESSIONAL'], after: ['ASSESSOR'] });
  });

  it('stops an ADMIN from creating staff, editing staff, or changing their own roles', async () => {
    const t = buildTestContext();
    const admin = await person(t, 'admin', ['ADMIN']);
    const root = await person(t, 'root', ['SUPER_ADMIN']);
    const bob = await person(t, 'bob');
    for (const roles of [['ADMIN'], ['SUPER_ADMIN'], ['ASSESSOR', 'ADMIN']]) {
      expect((await put(admin, `/api/v1/users/${bob.id}/roles`, { roles })).status).toBe(403);
    }
    expect(
      (await put(admin, `/api/v1/users/${root.id}/roles`, { roles: ['PUBLIC_USER'] })).status,
    ).toBe(403);
    expect(
      (await put(admin, `/api/v1/users/${admin.id}/roles`, { roles: ['SUPER_ADMIN'] })).status,
    ).toBe(403);
    expect(t.repos.data.users.find((u) => u.id === bob.id)!.roles).toEqual(['PROFESSIONAL']);
    expect(
      t.repos.data.audit.filter((e) => e.action === 'ROLE_CHANGED' && e.outcome === 'failure')
        .length,
    ).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('lets a SUPER_ADMIN grant ADMIN, but nobody can change their own roles', async () => {
    const t = buildTestContext();
    const root = await person(t, 'root', ['SUPER_ADMIN']);
    const bob = await person(t, 'bob');
    expect((await put(root, `/api/v1/users/${bob.id}/roles`, { roles: ['ADMIN'] })).status).toBe(
      200,
    );
    expect(
      (await put(root, `/api/v1/users/${root.id}/roles`, { roles: ['PUBLIC_USER'] })).status,
    ).toBe(403);
  });

  it('rejects unknown roles and organization roles as platform roles', async () => {
    const t = buildTestContext();
    const root = await person(t, 'root', ['SUPER_ADMIN']);
    const bob = await person(t, 'bob');
    for (const roles of [['GOD_MODE'], ['ORGANIZATION_ADMIN'], []]) {
      expect((await put(root, `/api/v1/users/${bob.id}/roles`, { roles })).status).toBe(400);
    }
  });
});

describe('cross-organization access', () => {
  async function twoOrganizations() {
    const t = buildTestContext();
    const ada = await person(t, 'ada');
    const bob = await person(t, 'bob');

    const firstOrgResponse = await post(ada, '/api/v1/organizations', {
      name: 'Acme Research',
    });
    // console.log('ORG CREATE DEBUG:', firstOrgResponse.status, firstOrgResponse.body);

    const org1 = firstOrgResponse.body.organization;
    const secondOrgResponse = await post(bob, '/api/v1/organizations', {
      name: 'Beta Labs',
    });
    const org2 = secondOrgResponse.body.organization;

    return { t, ada, bob, org1, org2 };
  }

  it('makes the creator an ORGANIZATION_ADMIN and lists only your own organizations', async () => {
    const { ada, org1 } = await twoOrganizations();
    expect(org1.role).toBe('ORGANIZATION_ADMIN');
    const mine = await ada.agent.get('/api/v1/organizations');
    expect(mine.body.organizations.map((o: { id: string }) => o.id)).toEqual([org1.id]);
  });

  it('hides another organization completely: read, members and rename are all 404', async () => {
    const { ada, org2 } = await twoOrganizations();
    expect((await ada.agent.get(`/api/v1/organizations/${org2.id}`)).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await ada.agent.get(`/api/v1/organizations/${org2.id}/members`)).status).toBe(404);
    expect(
      (await patch(ada, `/api/v1/organizations/${org2.id}`, { name: 'Hijacked' })).status,
    ).toBe(404);
  });

  it('gives a non-member the same response as for an organization that does not exist', async () => {
    const { ada, org2 } = await twoOrganizations();
    const foreign = await ada.agent.get(`/api/v1/organizations/${org2.id}`);
    const missing = await ada.agent.get(`/api/v1/organizations/${fakeId()}`);
    const malformed = await ada.agent.get('/api/v1/organizations/not-an-id');
    expect(foreign.status).toBe(404);
    expect(missing.status).toBe(404);
    expect(malformed.status).toBe(404);
    expect(foreign.body.error.code).toBe(missing.body.error.code);
  });

  it('gives platform staff, even a SUPER_ADMIN, no access to organization data', async () => {
    const { t, org1 } = await twoOrganizations();
    for (const [name, roles] of [
      ['admin', ['ADMIN']],
      ['root', ['SUPER_ADMIN']],
    ] as const) {
      const staff = await person(t, name, [...roles]);
      expect((await staff.agent.get(`/api/v1/organizations/${org1.id}`)).status).toBe(404);
      expect((await staff.agent.get(`/api/v1/organizations/${org1.id}/members`)).status).toBe(404);
    }
  });

  it('separates organization admins from members inside one organization', async () => {
    const { t, ada, org1 } = await twoOrganizations();
    const carol = await person(t, 'carol', ['PUBLIC_USER']);
    await t.repos.organizations.addMember(org1.id, carol.id, 'ORGANIZATION_MEMBER');

    const read = await carol.agent.get(`/api/v1/organizations/${org1.id}`);
    expect(read.status).toBe(200);
    expect(read.body.organization.role).toBe('ORGANIZATION_MEMBER');
    expect(
      (await patch(carol, `/api/v1/organizations/${org1.id}`, { name: 'Mine now' })).status,
    ).toBe(403);
    expect(
      (await patch(ada, `/api/v1/organizations/${org1.id}`, { name: 'Acme Labs' })).body
        .organization.name,
    ).toBe('Acme Labs');
  });

  it('lists members by name and role only, never email addresses', async () => {
    const { t, ada, org1 } = await twoOrganizations();
    const carol = await person(t, 'carol');
    await t.repos.organizations.addMember(org1.id, carol.id, 'ORGANIZATION_MEMBER');
    const res = await ada.agent.get(`/api/v1/organizations/${org1.id}/members`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.members).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(JSON.stringify(res.body)).not.toMatch(/@example\.org/);
  });

  it('stops users without ORG_CREATE (PUBLIC_USER) from creating organizations', async () => {
    const t = buildTestContext();
    const pat = await person(t, 'pat', ['PUBLIC_USER']);
    expect((await post(pat, '/api/v1/organizations', { name: 'Nope Inc' })).status).toBe(403);
    expect(t.repos.data.orgs).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('writes an audit event when an organization is created', async () => {
    const { t, ada, org1 } = await twoOrganizations();
    const entry = t.repos.data.audit.find(
      (e) => e.action === 'ORGANIZATION_CREATED' && e.organizationId === org1.id,
    )!;
    expect(entry.actorId).toBe(ada.id);
  });
});



========== FILE: apps/api/src/users/users.router.ts ==========

import { Router } from 'express';
import { assignRolesSchema, objectIdSchema, updateProfileSchema } from '@impactalign/validation';
import { Permission } from '@impactalign/types';
import { requestContext, requirePermission, requireAuth } from '../auth/middleware';
import { toPublicUser } from '../auth/public-user';
import { validateBody, validateIdParam } from '../common/validate';
import type { UsersService } from './users.service';

const isId = (value: string) => objectIdSchema.safeParse(value).success;

export function createUsersRouter(users: UsersService): Router {
  const router = Router();

  router.patch('/me', requireAuth, validateBody(updateProfileSchema), async (req, res) => {
    const updated = await users.updateOwnProfile(req.auth!.user, req.body.displayName);
    res.json({ user: toPublicUser(updated) });
  });

  router.get('/:id', requireAuth, validateIdParam('id', isId), async (req, res) => {
    const user = await users.getUser(req.auth!.user, String(req.params['id']));
    res.json({ user: toPublicUser(user) });
  });

  router.put(
    '/:id/roles',
    requirePermission(Permission.USER_ROLE_ASSIGN),
    validateIdParam('id', isId),
    validateBody(assignRolesSchema),
    async (req, res) => {
      const updated = await users.assignRoles(
        req.auth!.user,
        String(req.params['id']),
        req.body.roles,
        requestContext(req),
      );
      res.json({ user: toPublicUser(updated) });
    },
  );

  return router;
}



========== FILE: apps/api/src/users/users.service.ts ==========

import { canAssignRoles, hasPlatformPermission } from '@impactalign/auth';
import { AuditEvent, Permission, type PlatformRole } from '@impactalign/types';
import type { AuditService } from '../auth/audit.service';
import type { RequestContext } from '../auth/auth.service';
import { HttpError } from '../common/http-error';
import type { Repositories, UserRecord } from '../data/repositories';

const forbidden = () => new HttpError(403, 'FORBIDDEN', 'You do not have permission to do this');

export class UsersService {
  constructor(private readonly deps: { repos: Repositories; audit: AuditService }) {}

  /** Your own profile, or anyone's when you hold USER_READ. Others get 403 (no existence probing). */
  async getUser(actor: UserRecord, targetId: string): Promise<UserRecord> {
    if (actor.id !== targetId && !hasPlatformPermission(actor.roles, Permission.USER_READ))
      throw forbidden();
    const user = await this.deps.repos.users.findById(targetId);
    if (!user) throw HttpError.notFound('User not found');
    return user;
  }

  /** Only the display name can be changed here; roles and email are never client-editable. */
  async updateOwnProfile(actor: UserRecord, displayName: string): Promise<UserRecord> {
    await this.deps.repos.users.setDisplayName(actor.id, displayName);
    return { ...actor, displayName };
  }

  async assignRoles(
    actor: UserRecord,
    targetId: string,
    requested: readonly PlatformRole[],
    ctx: RequestContext,
  ): Promise<UserRecord> {
    const { repos, audit } = this.deps;
    const target = await repos.users.findById(targetId);
    if (!target) throw HttpError.notFound('User not found');

    const roles = [...new Set(requested)];
    const before = [...target.roles]; // capture before anything changes
    const decision = canAssignRoles({
      actorId: actor.id,
      actorRoles: actor.roles,
      targetId: target.id,
      targetRoles: target.roles,
      newRoles: roles,
    });
    const base = {
      action: AuditEvent.ROLE_CHANGED,
      actorId: actor.id,
      resourceType: 'user',
      resourceId: target.id,
      requestId: ctx.requestId,
      ipAddress: ctx.ipAddress,
    };
    if (decision.ok === false) {
      await audit.record({
        ...base,
        outcome: 'failure',
        metadata: { reason: decision.reason, requested: roles },
      });
      throw forbidden(); // the reason is logged, not revealed
    }
    await repos.users.setRoles(target.id, roles);
    await audit.record({ ...base, outcome: 'success', metadata: { before, after: roles } });
    return { ...target, roles };
  }
}

========== FILE: apps/web/README.md ==========

# @impactalign/web

Public website (Next.js App Router). Marketing routes live in `app/(marketing)`; the authenticated dashboard
will be a separate route group with its own layout. Per-request nonce CSP is set in `proxy.ts`, which is why
pages render dynamically. All pages besides the home page are labelled placeholders.

  robots: { index: false, follow: false },

    <AuthCard
      title="Reset your password"
      description="Enter your email and we will send you a link if an account exists."

  );
}

========== FILE: apps/web/app/(auth)/login/page.tsx ==========

import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { AuthCard } from '@/components/auth/auth-card';
import { LoginForm } from '@/components/auth/login-form';
import { getSession } from '@/lib/auth/session';
import { safeNextPath } from '@/lib/auth/next-path';

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: 'Sign in', robots: { index: false, follow: false } };

export default async function Page({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ next?: string; reauth?: string }>;
}) {
  const { next, reauth } = await searchParams;
  // Already signed in: skip the form. Never when we were just sent here by a protected page (reauth),
  // otherwise a disagreement about the session would bounce between /login and /dashboard forever.
  if (!reauth && (await getSession())) redirect(safeNextPath(next));
  return (
    <AuthCard
      title="Sign in"
      description="Welcome back to ImpactAlign SDG."
      footer={{ text: 'New here?', linkLabel: 'Create an account', href: '/register' }}
    >
      <LoginForm next={next} />
    </AuthCard>
  );
}

========== FILE: apps/web/app/(auth)/reset-password/page.tsx ==========

import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { AuthCard } from '@/components/auth/auth-card';
import { ResetPasswordForm } from '@/components/auth/reset-password-form';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'Set new password',
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
};

export default async function Page({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ token?: string }>;
}) {
  const { token } = await searchParams;
  return (
    <AuthCard
      title="Set a new password"
      footer={{ text: 'Need a new link?', linkLabel: 'Request one', href: '/forgot-password' }}
    >
      {token ? (
        <ResetPasswordForm token={token} />
      ) : (
        <p role="alert">
          This link is incomplete. Open the link from your email again, or request a new one.
        </p>
      )}
    </AuthCard>
  );
}

};

export default async function Page({
  searchParams,

}) {

import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Container } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { PageHero } from '@/components/marketing/page-hero';
import { CtaBanner } from '@/components/marketing/cta-banner';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'Accreditation Journey',
  description:
    'Learn how ImpactAlign plans to support SDG alignment, evidence submission, human assessment and continuous improvement.',
  alternates: {
    canonical: '/accreditation',
  },
};

const stages = [
  {
    number: '01',
    title: 'Align with the SDGs',
    description:
      'Identify relevant Sustainable Development Goals, targets and indicators for your activities, programmes or organization.',
  },
  {
    number: '02',
    title: 'Collect and submit evidence',
    description:
      'Organize supporting documents, measurements and other evidence that explain the outcomes of your work.',
  },
  {
    number: '03',
    title: 'Review and validate',
    description:
      'A human-led assessment process can examine submitted evidence, identify gaps and document findings. AI may assist with analysis, but never makes the final decision.',
  },
  {
    number: '04',
    title: 'Improve and track progress',
    description:
      'Use assessment feedback to address gaps, strengthen evidence and improve how impact is measured and reported.',
  },
] as const;

export default function AccreditationPage() {
  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        eyebrow="Assessment and recognition"
        title="A clearer journey from SDG alignment to verified impact"
        intro="ImpactAlign is being developed to help organizations connect their sustainability work to SDG targets, organize evidence and support a transparent, human-led assessment process."
        crumbs={[{ label: 'Home', href: '/' }, { label: 'Accreditation' }]}
      />

      <Container className="py-14 md:py-20">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-gold-700">
            The accreditation journey
          </p>

          <h2 className="mt-3 font-serif text-3xl font-bold text-navy-900 md:text-4xl">
            Four stages toward stronger impact evidence
          </h2>

          <p className="mt-4 leading-7 text-slate-600">
            The following describes the intended approach. Availability of assessment services,
            accreditation decisions and formal recognition depends on the platform&apos;s
            implementation and any applicable independent assessment arrangements.
          </p>
        </div>

        <ol className="mx-auto mt-10 grid max-w-5xl gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
          {stages.map((stage) => (
            <li
              key={stage.number}
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm"
            >
              <span className="text-sm font-bold tracking-widest text-gold-700">
                STAGE {stage.number}
              </span>

              <h3 className="mt-3 font-serif text-xl font-bold text-navy-900">{stage.title}</h3>

              <p className="mt-3 leading-7 text-slate-600">{stage.description}</p>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>

        <section className="mx-auto mt-12 max-w-3xl rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-6 md:p-8">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-navy-900">
            Human decisions. Responsible AI.
          </h2>

          <p className="mt-3 leading-7 text-slate-600">
            AI-assisted tools may help organize information, summarize evidence and identify
            potential gaps. They do not replace qualified human judgment or independently award
            accreditation. Assessment criteria, reviewer responsibilities and decision records
            should be transparent.
          </p>
        </section>

        <p className="mx-auto mt-8 max-w-3xl">
          <Link
            href="/accreditation/faqs"
            className="font-semibold text-navy-900 underline underline-offset-4 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-teal-600"
          >
            Read the accreditation FAQs →
          </Link>
        </p>
        {/* <p
          role="note"
          className="mx-auto mt-8 max-w-3xl rounded-md border border-gold-500 bg-gold-50 p-4 text-sm leading-6 text-navy-900"
        >
          Development status: This page describes the intended journey. It does not represent a
          guarantee of accreditation, certification or recognition by an external accreditation
          body.
        </p> */}
      </Container>

      <CtaBanner />
    </>
  );
}

========== FILE: apps/web/app/(marketing)/pages.test.tsx ==========

// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { cleanup, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { AI_GENERATED_LABEL } from '@impactalign/ai';
import { legalPages } from '@impactalign/config';
import { LegalPlaceholder } from '@/components/marketing/legal-placeholder';
import { SectionPage } from '@/components/marketing/section-page';
import { sections } from '@/lib/section-content';
import HomePage from './page';

afterEach(cleanup);

describe('Home page', () => {
  it('has exactly one h1 and the primary call to action', () => {
    render(<HomePage />);
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('link', { name: 'JOIN IMPACTALIGN' }).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('labels every section landmark with a heading that exists', () => {
    const { container } = render(<HomePage />);
    const sectionsWithLabel = container.querySelectorAll('section[aria-labelledby]');
    expect(sectionsWithLabel.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(10);
    sectionsWithLabel.forEach((section) => {
      const id = section.getAttribute('aria-labelledby') ?? '';
      expect(container.querySelector(`#${id}`)?.textContent).toBeTruthy();
    });
  });

  it('shows all five methodology steps', () => {
    render(<HomePage />);
    for (const step of ['Diagnose', 'Align', 'Measure', 'Validate', 'Improve']) {
      expect(screen.getAllByRole('heading', { name: step }).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });

  it('previews 17 goals by number only, without hard-coded official titles', () => {
    render(<HomePage />);
    const goals = screen.getAllByRole('link', { name: /^Sustainable Development Goal \d+$/ });
    expect(goals).toHaveLength(17);
    expect(screen.queryByText(/no poverty|zero hunger/i)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('is honest: dashboard preview is marked as sample and the AI rule is shown', () => {
    render(<HomePage />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Sample layout — no real data')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText(AI_GENERATED_LABEL)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getAllByText('Planned').length).toBe(6);
  });
});

describe('Section pages', () => {
  it.each(sections.map((s) => s.slug))(
    '/%s renders one h1, breadcrumbs and a placeholder note',
    (slug) => {
      render(<SectionPage slug={slug} />);
      expect(screen.getAllByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveLength(1);
      const crumbs = screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Breadcrumb' });
      expect(within(crumbs).getByRole('link', { name: 'Home' }).getAttribute('href')).toBe('/');
      expect(screen.getByRole('note').textContent).toMatch(/under development/i);
    },
  );

  it('lists the planned topics for a section', () => {
    render(<SectionPage slug="about" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Governance' })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Partners & Collaborations' })).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

describe('Legal pages', () => {
  it.each(legalPages.map((p) => p.slug))('/%s makes no legal claims', (slug) => {
    render(<LegalPlaceholder slug={slug} />);
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(screen.getByRole('note').textContent).toMatch(/placeholder/i);
  });
});

========== FILE: apps/web/app/dashboard/layout.tsx ==========

import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { DashboardShell } from '@/components/dashboard/dashboard-shell';
import { getSession } from '@/lib/auth/session';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: { default: 'Dashboard', template: '%s | Dashboard' },
  robots: { index: false, follow: false, nocache: true },
};

/** Every dashboard page requires a session that the API confirms; otherwise: sign in first. */
export default async function DashboardLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const session = await getSession();
  if (!session) redirect('/login?next=/dashboard');
  return <DashboardShell user={session.user}>{children}</DashboardShell>;
}

========== FILE: apps/web/app/dashboard/page.tsx ==========

import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Card } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { dashboardNav } from '@impactalign/config';
import { dashboardMetrics } from '@/lib/home-content';

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: 'Dashboard' };

const quickLinks = dashboardNav.filter((item) =>
  [
    '/dashboard/projects',
    '/dashboard/uploads/new',
    '/dashboard/submissions',
    '/dashboard/accreditation',
  ].includes(item.href),
);

export default function DashboardHomePage() {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <header>
        <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl font-bold text-navy-900">Dashboard</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-navy-800">Your impact alignment at a glance.</p>
      </header>

      <section aria-labelledby="overview-heading">
        <h2 id="overview-heading" className="sr-only">
          Overview
        </h2>
        <ul className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
          {dashboardMetrics.map((metric) => (
            <li key={metric}>
              <Card className="h-full">
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-navy-800">{metric}</p>
                <p
                  aria-label="No value yet"
                  className="mt-3 font-serif text-4xl font-bold text-navy-300"
                >
                  —
                </p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-navy-700">No data yet</p>
              </Card>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="quick-heading">
        <h2 id="quick-heading" className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-navy-900">
          Quick links
        </h2>
        <ul className="mt-3 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {quickLinks.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.href}>
              <Link
                href={item.href}
                className="block rounded-lg border border-navy-100 bg-white p-4 font-semibold text-navy-900 hover:bg-navy-50 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-teal-600"
              >
                {item.label} →
              </Link>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

========== FILE: apps/web/app/robots.ts ==========

import type { MetadataRoute } from 'next';
import { siteUrl } from '@/lib/site-url';

export default function robots(): MetadataRoute.Robots {
  return {
    rules: [
      {
        userAgent: '*',
        allow: '/',
        disallow: [
          '/dashboard',
          '/api',
          '/login',
          '/register',
          '/verify-email',
          '/forgot-password',
          '/reset-password',
        ],
      },
    ],
    sitemap: new URL('/sitemap.xml', siteUrl).toString(),
  };
}



========== FILE: apps/web/app/sitemap.ts ==========

import type { MetadataRoute } from 'next';
import { joinNav, legalPages, publicNav } from '@impactalign/config';
import { siteUrl } from '@/lib/site-url';
import { allTopicPaths } from '@/lib/topics';

export default function sitemap(): MetadataRoute.Sitemap {
  const paths = [
    ...publicNav.map((i) => i.href),
    joinNav.href,
    ...legalPages.map((p) => `/${p.slug}`),
    ...allTopicPaths,
  ];
  return paths.map((path) => ({ url: new URL(path, siteUrl).toString() }));
}

import Link from 'next/link';
import { Card } from '@impactalign/ui';

export interface AuthCardProps {
  readonly title: string;
  readonly description?: string;
  readonly children: React.ReactNode;
  readonly footer?: { readonly text: string; readonly linkLabel: string; readonly href: string };
}

export function AuthCard({ title, description, children, footer }: AuthCardProps) {
  return (
    <Card className="p-6 sm:p-8">
      <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl font-bold text-navy-900">{title}</h1>
      {description && <p className="mt-2 text-navy-800">{description}</p>}
      <div className="mt-6">{children}</div>
      {footer && (
        <p className="mt-6 text-sm text-navy-800">
          {footer.text}{' '}
          <Link
            href={footer.href}
            className="font-semibold text-navy-900 underline underline-offset-4"

          </Link>

========== FILE: apps/web/components/auth/auth-forms.test.tsx ==========

// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { setCsrfToken } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { ForgotPasswordForm } from './forgot-password-form';
import { LoginForm } from './login-form';
import { RegisterForm } from './register-form';
import { ResetPasswordForm } from './reset-password-form';
import { VerifyEmailForm } from './verify-email-form';

const router = { replace: vi.fn(), refresh: vi.fn(), push: vi.fn() };
vi.mock('next/navigation', () => ({ useRouter: () => router }));

const fetchMock = vi.fn();
const reply = (status: number, body: unknown) =>
  fetchMock.mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: status < 400, status, json: () => Promise.resolve(body) });
const apiError = (code: string, message = 'server message') => ({
  error: { code, message, requestId: 'r' },
});
const type = (label: RegExp | string, value: string) =>
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(label), { target: { value } });
const submit = (name: RegExp) => fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name }));
const sentBody = (call = 0) => JSON.parse(fetchMock.mock.calls[call]![1].body as string);

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
});
afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  fetchMock.mockReset();
  Object.values(router).forEach((fn) => fn.mockReset());
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
  setCsrfToken(null);
});

describe('RegisterForm', () => {
  it('validates on the client first: no request, accessible errors tied to the fields', async () => {
    render(<RegisterForm />);
    type('Email', 'not-an-email');
    type('Password', 'short');
    submit(/create account/i);
    expect(
      await screen.findByText(/at least 12 characters/i, { selector: 'p[id$="-error"]' }),
    ).toBeTruthy();
    const password = screen.getByLabelText('Password');
    expect(password.getAttribute('aria-invalid')).toBe('true');
    expect(
      document.getElementById(password.getAttribute('aria-describedby')!.split(' ').pop()!),
    ).toBeTruthy();
    expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('submits only the allowed fields, defaults to the lowest-privilege account type, then asks to check email', async () => {
    reply(202, { message: 'If this address can be used, a verification email has been sent.' });
    render(<RegisterForm />);
    type('Full name', 'Ada Lovelace');
    type('Email', 'Ada@Example.org');
    type('Password', 'correct-horse-battery-9');
    submit(/create account/i);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('status')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(fetchMock.mock.calls[0]![0]).toBe('/api/v1/auth/register');
    expect(sentBody()).toEqual({
      email: 'ada@example.org',
      password: 'correct-horse-battery-9',
      displayName: 'Ada Lovelace',
      accountType: 'PUBLIC_USER',
    });
  });

  it('offers only self-registration account types (no staff roles)', () => {
    render(<RegisterForm />);
    const options = [...(screen.getByLabelText(/joining as/i) as HTMLSelectElement).options].map(
      (o) => o.value,
    );
    expect(options).toEqual(['PROFESSIONAL', 'RESEARCHER', 'ACADEMIC', 'PARTNER', 'PUBLIC_USER']);
  });
});

describe('LoginForm', () => {
  it('signs in and goes to a safe next page', async () => {
    reply(200, { user: { id: '1' }, csrfToken: 'csrf-1' });
    render(<LoginForm next="/dashboard/projects" />);
    type('Email', 'ada@example.org');
    type('Password', 'correct-horse-battery-9');
    submit(/sign in/i);
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.replace).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/dashboard/projects'));
    expect(router.refresh).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('ignores an off-site next link', async () => {
    reply(200, { user: { id: '1' }, csrfToken: 'csrf-1' });
    render(<LoginForm next="https://evil.example" />);
    type('Email', 'ada@example.org');
    type('Password', 'x');
    submit(/sign in/i);
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.replace).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/dashboard'));
  });

  it.each([
    ['INVALID_CREDENTIALS', 401, /invalid email or password/i],
    ['EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED', 403, /verify your email/i],
    ['TOO_MANY_ATTEMPTS', 429, /too many attempts/i],
  ])('shows a friendly alert for %s and does not navigate', async (code, status, text) => {
    reply(status, apiError(code));
    render(<LoginForm />);
    type('Email', 'ada@example.org');
    type('Password', 'whatever-password-1');
    submit(/sign in/i);
    expect((await screen.findByRole('alert')).textContent).toMatch(text);
    expect(router.replace).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(screen.queryByText('server message')).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('ForgotPasswordForm', () => {
  it('shows the same neutral confirmation the API returns', async () => {
    reply(202, { message: 'If an account exists for this address, a reset link has been sent.' });
    render(<ForgotPasswordForm />);
    type('Email', 'ghost@example.org');
    submit(/send reset link/i);
    expect((await screen.findByRole('status')).textContent).toMatch(/if an account exists/i);
  });
});

describe('ResetPasswordForm', () => {
  const token = 'x'.repeat(43);
  it('checks the two passwords match before calling the API', async () => {
    render(<ResetPasswordForm token={token} />);
    type('New password', 'brand-new-password-1');
    type('Confirm new password', 'different-password-22');
    submit(/set new password/i);
    expect(await screen.findByText(/do not match/i)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('sends the token and new password, then links to sign in', async () => {
    reply(200, { message: 'Password updated. Sign in with your new password.' });
    render(<ResetPasswordForm token={token} />);
    type('New password', 'brand-new-password-1');
    type('Confirm new password', 'brand-new-password-1');
    submit(/set new password/i);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('link', { name: /go to sign in/i })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(sentBody()).toEqual({ token, password: 'brand-new-password-1' });
  });

  it('explains an expired link', async () => {
    reply(400, apiError('INVALID_OR_EXPIRED_TOKEN'));
    render(<ResetPasswordForm token={token} />);
    type('New password', 'brand-new-password-1');
    type('Confirm new password', 'brand-new-password-1');
    submit(/set new password/i);
    expect((await screen.findByRole('alert')).textContent).toMatch(/invalid or has expired/i);
  });
});

describe('VerifyEmailForm', () => {
  const token = 'y'.repeat(43);
  it('does nothing on load and verifies only when the button is pressed', async () => {
    reply(200, { message: 'Email verified. You can now sign in.' });
    render(<VerifyEmailForm token={token} />);
    expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    submit(/verify my email/i);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('link', { name: /go to sign in/i })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(sentBody()).toEqual({ token });
  });
});



========== FILE: apps/web/components/auth/forgot-password-form.tsx ==========

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { forgotPasswordSchema } from '@impactalign/validation';
import { authApi, describeError } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { fieldErrors } from '@/lib/auth/form-errors';
import { FormMessage } from './form-message';
import { TextField } from './text-field';

export function ForgotPasswordForm() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [done, setDone] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  async function onSubmit(event: React.FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setFormError(null);
    const parsed = forgotPasswordSchema.safeParse({ email });
    if (!parsed.success) {
      setErrors(fieldErrors(parsed.error.issues));
      return;
    }
    setErrors({});
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      setDone((await authApi.forgotPassword(parsed.data.email)).message);
    } catch (error) {
      setFormError(describeError(error));
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  if (done) return <FormMessage kind="success">{done}</FormMessage>;

  return (
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} noValidate className="space-y-4" aria-busy={submitting}>
      {formError && <FormMessage kind="error">{formError}</FormMessage>}
      <TextField
        label="Email"
        name="email"
        type="email"
        autoComplete="email"
        value={email}
        onChange={setEmail}
        error={errors['email']}
        disabled={submitting}
      />
      <Button type="submit" size="lg" className="w-full" disabled={submitting}>
        {submitting ? 'Sending…' : 'Send reset link'}
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}



========== FILE: apps/web/components/auth/form-message.tsx ==========

import { cn } from '@impactalign/ui';

/** Error (role=alert, announced immediately) or success (role=status) message above a form. */
export function FormMessage({
  kind,
  children,
}: {
  readonly kind: 'error' | 'success';
  readonly children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <p
      role={kind === 'error' ? 'alert' : 'status'}
      className={cn(
        'rounded-md border p-3 text-sm',
        kind === 'error'
          ? 'border-red-700 bg-red-50 text-red-900'
          : 'border-teal-700 bg-teal-50 text-teal-900',
      )}
    >
      {children}
    </p>
  );
}



========== FILE: apps/web/components/auth/login-form.tsx ==========

'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { loginSchema } from '@impactalign/validation';
import { authApi, describeError } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { fieldErrors } from '@/lib/auth/form-errors';
import { safeNextPath } from '@/lib/auth/next-path';
import { FormMessage } from './form-message';
import { TextField } from './text-field';

export function LoginForm({ next }: { readonly next?: string | undefined }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  async function onSubmit(event: React.FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setFormError(null);
    const parsed = loginSchema.safeParse({ email, password });
    if (!parsed.success) {
      setErrors(fieldErrors(parsed.error.issues));
      return;
    }
    setErrors({});
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await authApi.login(parsed.data.email, parsed.data.password);
      router.replace(safeNextPath(next));
      router.refresh();
    } catch (error) {
      setFormError(describeError(error));
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} noValidate className="space-y-4" aria-busy={submitting}>
      {formError && <FormMessage kind="error">{formError}</FormMessage>}
      <TextField
        label="Email"
        name="email"
        type="email"
        autoComplete="email"
        value={email}
        onChange={setEmail}
        error={errors['email']}
        disabled={submitting}
      />
      <TextField
        label="Password"
        name="password"
        type="password"
        autoComplete="current-password"
        value={password}
        onChange={setPassword}
        error={errors['password']}
        disabled={submitting}
      />
      <p className="text-sm">
        <Link
          href="/forgot-password"
          className="font-semibold text-navy-900 underline underline-offset-4"
        >
          Forgot your password?
        </Link>
      </p>
      <Button type="submit" size="lg" className="w-full" disabled={submitting}>
        {submitting ? 'Signing in…' : 'Sign in'}
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}



========== FILE: apps/web/components/auth/register-form.tsx ==========

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { SELF_REGISTRATION_ROLES } from '@impactalign/types';
import { registerSchema } from '@impactalign/validation';
import { authApi, describeError } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { fieldErrors } from '@/lib/auth/form-errors';
import { FormMessage } from './form-message';
import { TextField } from './text-field';

const ACCOUNT_LABELS: Record<(typeof SELF_REGISTRATION_ROLES)[number], string> = {
  PROFESSIONAL: 'Professional',
  RESEARCHER: 'Researcher',
  ACADEMIC: 'Academic',
  PARTNER: 'Partner',
  PUBLIC_USER: 'Public user',
};

export function RegisterForm() {
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [accountType, setAccountType] = useState<string>('PUBLIC_USER');
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [done, setDone] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  async function onSubmit(event: React.FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setFormError(null);
    const parsed = registerSchema.safeParse({ email, password, displayName, accountType });
    if (!parsed.success) {
      setErrors(fieldErrors(parsed.error.issues));
      return;
    }
    setErrors({});
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const result = await authApi.register(parsed.data);
      setDone(result.message);
    } catch (error) {
      setFormError(describeError(error));
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  if (done) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <FormMessage kind="success">{done}</FormMessage>
        <p className="text-sm text-navy-800">
          Open the email we sent and select the verification link. The link works once and expires
          after 24 hours.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} noValidate className="space-y-4" aria-busy={submitting}>
      {formError && <FormMessage kind="error">{formError}</FormMessage>}
      <TextField
        label="Full name"
        name="displayName"
        autoComplete="name"
        value={displayName}
        onChange={setDisplayName}
        error={errors['displayName']}
        disabled={submitting}
      />
      <TextField
        label="Email"
        name="email"
        type="email"
        autoComplete="email"
        value={email}
        onChange={setEmail}
        error={errors['email']}
        disabled={submitting}
      />
      <TextField
        label="Password"
        name="password"
        type="password"
        autoComplete="new-password"
        hint="At least 12 characters. A few random words make a strong, memorable password."
        value={password}
        onChange={setPassword}
        error={errors['password']}
        disabled={submitting}
      />
      <div>
        <label htmlFor="accountType" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-navy-900">
          I am joining as
        </label>
        <select
          id="accountType"
          name="accountType"
          value={accountType}
          disabled={submitting}
          onChange={(event) => setAccountType(event.target.value)}
          className="mt-1 block h-11 w-full rounded-md border border-navy-300 bg-white px-3 text-navy-950 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-teal-600"
        >
          {SELF_REGISTRATION_ROLES.map((role) => (
            <option key={role} value={role}>
              {ACCOUNT_LABELS[role]}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>
      <Button type="submit" size="lg" className="w-full" disabled={submitting}>
        {submitting ? 'Creating account…' : 'Create account'}
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}



========== FILE: apps/web/components/auth/reset-password-form.tsx ==========

'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { resetPasswordSchema } from '@impactalign/validation';
import { authApi, describeError } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { fieldErrors } from '@/lib/auth/form-errors';
import { FormMessage } from './form-message';
import { TextField } from './text-field';

export function ResetPasswordForm({ token }: { readonly token: string }) {
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState('');
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [done, setDone] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  async function onSubmit(event: React.FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setFormError(null);
    const parsed = resetPasswordSchema.safeParse({ token, password });
    const next = parsed.success ? {} : fieldErrors(parsed.error.issues);
    if (password !== confirm) next['confirm'] = 'The passwords do not match';
    if (!parsed.success || Object.keys(next).length > 0) {
      setErrors(next);
      return;
    }
    setErrors({});
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      setDone((await authApi.resetPassword(token, parsed.data.password)).message);
    } catch (error) {
      setFormError(describeError(error));
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  if (done) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <FormMessage kind="success">{done}</FormMessage>
        <Button asChild size="lg" className="w-full">
          <Link href="/login">Go to sign in</Link>
        </Button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} noValidate className="space-y-4" aria-busy={submitting}>
      {formError && <FormMessage kind="error">{formError}</FormMessage>}
      <TextField
        label="New password"
        name="password"
        type="password"
        autoComplete="new-password"
        hint="At least 12 characters."
        value={password}
        onChange={setPassword}
        error={errors['password']}
        disabled={submitting}
      />
      <TextField
        label="Confirm new password"
        name="confirm"
        type="password"
        autoComplete="new-password"
        value={confirm}
        onChange={setConfirm}
        error={errors['confirm']}
        disabled={submitting}
      />
      <Button type="submit" size="lg" className="w-full" disabled={submitting}>
        {submitting ? 'Saving…' : 'Set new password'}
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}



========== FILE: apps/web/components/auth/text-field.tsx ==========

import { useId } from 'react';
import { cn } from '@impactalign/ui';

export interface TextFieldProps {
  readonly label: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly value: string;
  readonly onChange: (value: string) => void;
  readonly type?: 'text' | 'email' | 'password';
  readonly error?: string | undefined;
  readonly hint?: string;
  readonly autoComplete?: string;
  readonly disabled?: boolean;
}

/** Labelled input with its hint and error wired up through aria-describedby. */
export function TextField({
  label,
  name,
  value,
  onChange,
  type = 'text',
  error,
  hint,
  autoComplete,
  disabled,
}: TextFieldProps) {
  const id = useId();
  const hintId = `${id}-hint`;
  const errorId = `${id}-error`;
  const describedBy =
    [hint ? hintId : '', error ? errorId : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' ') || undefined;
  return (
    <div>
      <label htmlFor={id} className="block text-sm font-semibold text-navy-900">
        {label}
      </label>
      <input
        id={id}
        name={name}
        type={type}
        value={value}
        disabled={disabled}
        autoComplete={autoComplete}
        aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
        aria-describedby={describedBy}
        onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}
        className={cn(
          'mt-1 block h-11 w-full rounded-md border bg-white px-3 text-navy-950 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-teal-600 disabled:opacity-60',
          error ? 'border-red-700' : 'border-navy-300',
        )}
      />
      {hint && (
        <p id={hintId} className="mt-1 text-sm text-navy-700">
          {hint}
        </p>
      )}
      {error && (
        <p id={errorId} className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-red-700">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}



========== FILE: apps/web/components/auth/verify-email-form.tsx ==========

'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { authApi, describeError } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { FormMessage } from './form-message';

/** Verification needs an explicit click (not on page load) so link-preview bots cannot use up the token. */
export function VerifyEmailForm({ token }: { readonly token: string }) {
  const [state, setState] = useState<'idle' | 'working' | 'done'>('idle');
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function verify() {
    setState('working');
    setError(null);
    try {
      setMessage((await authApi.verifyEmail(token)).message);
      setState('done');
    } catch (err) {
      setError(describeError(err));
      setState('idle');
    }
  }

  if (state === 'done') {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <FormMessage kind="success">{message}</FormMessage>
        <Button asChild size="lg" className="w-full">
          <Link href="/login">Go to sign in</Link>
        </Button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {error && <FormMessage kind="error">{error}</FormMessage>}
      <Button
        type="button"
        size="lg"
        className="w-full"
        disabled={state === 'working'}
        onClick={verify}
      >
        {state === 'working' ? 'Verifying…' : 'Verify my email'}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}



========== FILE: apps/web/components/dashboard/dashboard-nav.tsx ==========

'use client';

import { cn } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { dashboardNav } from '@impactalign/config';
import { NavLink } from '@/components/navigation/nav-link';

/** Sidebar link list. `/dashboard` matches exactly so it is not "current" on every sub-page. */
export function DashboardNavList({ className }: { readonly className?: string }) {
  return (
    <ul className={cn('flex flex-col gap-1', className)}>
      {dashboardNav.map((item) => (
        <li key={item.href}>
          <NavLink href={item.href} exact={item.href === '/dashboard'} className="block">
            {item.label}
          </NavLink>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}

import { Badge, Card, EmptyState } from '@impactalign/ui';
import {
  evidenceStatuses,
  getDashboardContent,
  getDashboardTitle,
  plannedFileTypes,
} from '@/lib/dashboard-content';

/** Generic dashboard screen: title, honest empty state and the capabilities planned for it. */
export function DashboardPage({ href }: { readonly href: string }) {
  const title = getDashboardTitle(href);
  const content = getDashboardContent(href);
  const showStatuses = href === '/dashboard/submissions';
  const showFileTypes = href === '/dashboard/uploads/new';

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <header>
        <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl font-bold text-navy-900">{title}</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-navy-800">{content.description}</p>
      </header>

      <EmptyState
        title={content.emptyTitle}
        description="This feature is built in a later phase."
      />

      <Card>
        <h2 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-navy-900">Planned capabilities</h2>
        <ul className="mt-3 list-disc space-y-1 pl-5 text-navy-800">
          {content.capabilities.map((capability) => (
            <li key={capability}>{capability}</li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </Card>

      {showStatuses && (
        <Card>
          <h2 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-navy-900">Evidence statuses</h2>
          <ul className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {evidenceStatuses.map((status) => (
              <li key={status}>
                <Badge>{status}</Badge>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </Card>
      )}

      {showFileTypes && (
        <Card>
          <h2 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-navy-900">Planned file types</h2>
          <ul className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {plannedFileTypes.map((type) => (
              <li key={type}>
                <Badge tone="teal">{type}</Badge>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </Card>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

========== FILE: apps/web/components/dashboard/dashboard.test.tsx ==========

// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { dashboardNav } from '@impactalign/config';
import DashboardHomePage from '@/app/dashboard/page';
import { setCsrfToken } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { DashboardPage } from './dashboard-page';
import { DashboardShell } from './dashboard-shell';

const router = { replace: vi.fn(), refresh: vi.fn() };
vi.mock('next/navigation', () => ({
  usePathname: () => '/dashboard/projects',
  useRouter: () => router,
}));

const fetchMock = vi.fn();
beforeEach(() => {
  vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
});
afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  fetchMock.mockReset();
  router.replace.mockReset();
  router.refresh.mockReset();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
  setCsrfToken(null);
});

const user = { displayName: 'Ada Lovelace' };

describe('DashboardShell', () => {
  it('shows who is signed in, a sign-out button and labelled landmarks', () => {
    render(
      <DashboardShell user={user}>
        <p>child</p>
      </DashboardShell>,
    );
    expect(screen.getByText('Ada Lovelace')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /sign out/i })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('note')[0]?.textContent).toMatch(/still being built/i);
    expect(screen.getByRole('main')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Dashboard' })).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('lists every dashboard link and marks only the current page', () => {
    render(<DashboardShell user={user} />);
    const nav = screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Dashboard' });
    expect(within(nav).getAllByRole('link')).toHaveLength(dashboardNav.length);
    expect(within(nav).getByRole('link', { name: 'Projects' }).getAttribute('aria-current')).toBe(
      'page',
    );
    expect(
      within(nav).getByRole('link', { name: 'Dashboard' }).getAttribute('aria-current'),
    ).toBeNull();
  });

  it('signs out with a fresh CSRF token, then leaves for the sign-in page', async () => {
    fetchMock
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        json: () => Promise.resolve({ user: {}, csrfToken: 'csrf-9' }),
      })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({
        ok: true,
        status: 204,
        json: () => Promise.reject(new Error('empty')),
      });
    render(<DashboardShell user={user} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /sign out/i }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.replace).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/login'));
    expect(fetchMock.mock.calls[1]![0]).toBe('/api/v1/auth/logout');
    expect(fetchMock.mock.calls[1]![1].headers['X-CSRF-Token']).toBe('csrf-9');
  });

  it('still leaves the dashboard when the session is already gone', async () => {
    fetchMock.mockResolvedValue({
      ok: false,
      status: 401,
      json: () =>
        Promise.resolve({ error: { code: 'UNAUTHENTICATED', message: 'x', requestId: 'r' } }),
    });
    render(<DashboardShell user={user} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /sign out/i }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.replace).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/login'));
  });
});

describe('Dashboard pages', () => {
  it('home shows the five metrics as empty, never as fake numbers', () => {
    render(<DashboardHomePage />);
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(screen.getAllByLabelText('No value yet')).toHaveLength(5);
  });

  it.each(dashboardNav.filter((n) => n.href !== '/dashboard').map((n) => n.href))(
    '%s renders one h1 and an honest empty state',
    (href) => {
      render(<DashboardPage href={href} />);
      expect(screen.getAllByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(screen.getByText('This feature is built in a later phase.')).toBeTruthy();
    },
  );

  it('shows the evidence status legend on submissions and file types on uploads', () => {
    render(<DashboardPage href="/dashboard/submissions" />);
    expect(screen.getByText('CLARIFICATION_REQUIRED')).toBeTruthy();
    cleanup();
    render(<DashboardPage href="/dashboard/uploads/new" />);
    expect(screen.getByText('XLSX')).toBeTruthy();
  });
});



========== FILE: apps/web/components/dashboard/logout-button.tsx ==========

'use client';

import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { authApi } from '@/lib/api/client';

export function LogoutButton() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [working, setWorking] = useState(false);

  async function logout() {
    setWorking(true);
    try {
      await authApi.me(); // after a page load the CSRF token is gone from memory; fetch it again
      await authApi.logout();
    } catch {
      // Already signed out, or the server is unreachable: either way, leave the dashboard.
    } finally {
      router.replace('/login');
      router.refresh();
    }
  }

  return (
    <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={logout} disabled={working}>
      {working ? 'Signing out…' : 'Sign out'}
    </Button>
  );
}

========== FILE: apps/web/components/dashboard/profile-form.tsx ==========

'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { PublicUser } from '@impactalign/types';
import { Button } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { ApiError, authApi, describeError } from '@impactalign/api-client';

export function ProfileForm() {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<PublicUser | null>(null);
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    authApi.me().then((session) => {
      if (active) { setUser(session.user); setDisplayName(session.user.displayName); }
    }).catch((cause: unknown) => {
      if (active) setError(describeError(cause));
    }).finally(() => { if (active) setLoading(false); });
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, []);

  async function save(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault(); setError(null); setMessage(null);
    const name = displayName.trim();
    if (name.length < 2 || name.length > 80) { setError('Display name must be between 2 and 80 characters.'); return; }
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const result = await authApi.updateProfile(name);
      setUser(result.user); setDisplayName(result.user.displayName); setMessage('Display name updated.');
    } catch (cause) {
      setError(cause instanceof ApiError && cause.code === 'VALIDATION_ERROR' ? 'Please enter a valid display name.' : describeError(cause));
    } finally { setSaving(false); }
  }

  if (loading) return <p role="status">Loading profile…</p>;
  if (!user) return <p role="alert">{error ?? 'Unable to load your profile.'}</p>;

  return <div className="max-w-2xl space-y-8">
    <header><h1 className="font-serif text-3xl font-bold text-navy-900">Your profile</h1><p className="mt-2 text-navy-800">View your account details and update your display name.</p></header>
    <dl className="grid gap-4 rounded-xl border border-navy-100 bg-white p-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
      <div><dt className="text-sm font-semibold text-navy-700">Name</dt><dd className="mt-1 break-words">{user.displayName}</dd></div>
      <div><dt className="text-sm font-semibold text-navy-700">Email</dt><dd className="mt-1 break-words">{user.email}</dd></div>
      <div><dt className="text-sm font-semibold text-navy-700">Roles</dt><dd className="mt-1">{user.roles.join(', ')}</dd></div>
      <div><dt className="text-sm font-semibold text-navy-700">Email verified</dt><dd className="mt-1">{user.emailVerified ? 'Yes' : 'No'}</dd></div>
    </dl>
    <form onSubmit={save} className="space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-navy-100 bg-white p-5">
      <h2 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-navy-900">Edit display name</h2>
      {error && <p role="alert" className="rounded-md border border-red-700 bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-900">{error}</p>}
      {message && <p role="status" className="rounded-md border border-teal-700 bg-teal-50 p-3 text-sm text-teal-900">{message}</p>}
      <div className="space-y-1"><label htmlFor="displayName" className="text-sm font-semibold">Display name</label><input id="displayName" name="displayName" maxLength={80} required value={displayName} onChange={(event) => setDisplayName(event.target.value)} disabled={saving} className="w-full rounded-md border border-navy-200 bg-white px-3 py-2" /></div>
      <Button type="submit" disabled={saving}>{saving ? 'Saving…' : 'Save changes'}</Button>
    </form>
  </div>;
}

========== FILE: apps/web/components/marketing/faq-list.test.tsx ==========

import { cleanup, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { faqGroups } from '@/lib/faq-content';
import { FaqList } from './faq-list';

afterEach(cleanup);

describe('FaqList', () => {
  it('renders one collapsed item per question', () => {
    const { container } = render(<FaqList groups={faqGroups} />);
    const details = Array.from(container.querySelectorAll('details'));
    expect(details).toHaveLength(47);
    expect(details.every((item) => !item.open)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('has a labelled topic navigation and one heading per group', () => {
    render(<FaqList groups={faqGroups} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'FAQ topics' })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('heading', { level: 2 })).toHaveLength(faqGroups.length);
  });
});


========== FILE: apps/web/components/marketing/faq-list.tsx ==========

import type { FaqGroup } from '@/lib/faq-content';

export function FaqList({ groups }: { readonly groups: readonly FaqGroup[] }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <nav aria-label="FAQ topics">
        <ul className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {groups.map((group) => (
            <li key={group.id}>
              <a
                href={`#${group.id}`}
                className="block rounded-full border border-navy-100 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-navy-900 hover:bg-navy-50 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-teal-600"
              >
                {group.title}
              </a>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </nav>

      {groups.map((group) => (
        <section
          key={group.id}
          id={group.id}
          aria-labelledby={`${group.id}-heading`}
          className="mt-10 scroll-mt-24"
        >
          <h2
            id={`${group.id}-heading`}
            className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-navy-900"
          >
            {group.title}
          </h2>
          <div className="mt-4 divide-y divide-navy-100 rounded-lg border border-navy-100 bg-white">
            {group.items.map((item) => (
              <details key={item.number} className="p-4">
                <summary className="cursor-pointer font-semibold text-navy-900 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-teal-600">
                  <span className="mr-2 text-gold-700">{item.number}.</span>
                  {item.question}
                </summary>
                <div className="mt-3 space-y-3 leading-7 text-navy-800">
                  {item.answer.map((block) =>
                    typeof block === 'string' ? (
                      <p key={block}>{block}</p>
                    ) : (
                      <ul key={block.join('|')} className="list-disc space-y-1 pl-6">
                        {block.map((entry) => (
                          <li key={entry}>{entry}</li>
                        ))}
                      </ul>
                    ),
                  )}
                </div>
              </details>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

========== FILE: apps/web/components/marketing/item-grid.tsx ==========

import Link from 'next/link';
import { Badge, Card, cn } from '@impactalign/ui';
import type { TitledItem } from '@/lib/home-content';

export interface ItemGridProps {
  readonly items: readonly (TitledItem & { readonly status?: string; readonly href?: string })[];
  readonly columns?: 2 | 3;
  readonly tone?: 'light' | 'muted';
}

const columnClasses = {
  2: 'sm:grid-cols-2',
  3: 'sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3',
} as const;

/** Responsive grid of title + description cards (used for values, audiences, features, topics). */
export function ItemGrid({ items, columns = 3, tone = 'light' }: ItemGridProps) {
  return (
    <ul className={cn('grid gap-5', columnClasses[columns])}>
      {items.map((item) => (
        <li key={item.title}>
          <Card tone={tone} interactive={Boolean(item.href)} className="relative h-full">
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
              <h3 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-navy-900">
                {item.href ? (
                  <Link
                    href={item.href}
                    className="after:absolute after:inset-0 focus:outline-none"
                  >
                    {item.title}
                  </Link>
                ) : (
                  item.title
                )}
              </h3>
              {item.status && <Badge tone="gold">{item.status}</Badge>}
            </div>
            <p className="mt-2 text-navy-800">{item.description}</p>
          </Card>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}

import { Container } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { Breadcrumbs, type Crumb } from '@/components/navigation/breadcrumbs';

export interface PageHeroProps {
  readonly title: string;
  readonly eyebrow?: string;
  readonly intro?: string;
  readonly crumbs: readonly Crumb[];
}

/** Banner for inner pages: breadcrumbs, eyebrow, the page's single h1 and an intro. */
export function PageHero({ title, eyebrow, intro, crumbs }: PageHeroProps) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-navy-950 bg-[radial-gradient(40rem_20rem_at_90%_-20%,rgba(201,162,39,0.18),transparent)] text-white">
      <Container className="py-12 md:py-16">
        <Breadcrumbs items={crumbs} />

          <p className="mt-6 text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-gold-400">

          </p>
        )}

        {intro && <p className="mt-4 max-w-3xl text-lg text-navy-100">{intro}</p>}
      </Container>
    </div>
  );
}

========== FILE: apps/web/components/marketing/section-page.tsx ==========

import { Container } from '@impactalign/ui';
import { getSection } from '@/lib/section-content';
import { topicHref } from '@/lib/topics';
import { ItemGrid } from './item-grid';
import { PageHero } from './page-hero';
import { CtaBanner } from './cta-banner';

/** Public section page: hero + the topics the section covers. Shows a placeholder note until the section is published. */
export function SectionPage({ slug }: { readonly slug: string }) {
  const section = getSection(slug);
  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        title={section.title}
        eyebrow={section.eyebrow}
        intro={section.intro}
        crumbs={[{ label: 'Home', href: '/' }, { label: section.title }]}
      />
      <Container className="py-14 md:py-16">
        {section.topics.length > 0 && (
          <>
            <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-navy-900">Topics in this section</h2>
            <div className="mt-6">
              <ItemGrid
                items={section.topics.map((topic) => ({
                  title: topic.title,
                  description: topic.description,
                  href: topicHref(section, topic),
                }))}
                tone="muted"
              />
            </div>
          </>
        )}
        {!section.published && (
          <p
            role="note"
            className="mt-10 max-w-2xl rounded-md border border-gold-500 bg-gold-50 p-4 text-sm text-navy-900"
          >
            Note: This section is under development.
          </p>
        )}
      </Container>
      {slug !== 'join-impactalign' && <CtaBanner />}
    </>
  );
}


========== FILE: apps/web/components/marketing/section.tsx ==========

import { Container, cn } from '@impactalign/ui';

export interface SectionProps {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly title: string;
  readonly eyebrow?: string;
  readonly description?: string;
  readonly tone?: 'light' | 'muted' | 'dark';
  readonly children: React.ReactNode;
}

const toneClasses = {
  light: 'bg-white text-navy-950',
  muted: 'bg-navy-50 text-navy-950',
  dark: 'bg-navy-950 text-white',
} as const;

/** Standard page section: labelled landmark with eyebrow, heading and optional description. */
export function Section({
  id,
  title,
  eyebrow,
  description,
  tone = 'light',
  children,
}: SectionProps) {
  const dark = tone === 'dark';
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby={`${id}-heading`} className={cn('py-16 md:py-20', toneClasses[tone])}>
      <Container>
        <header className="max-w-3xl">
          {eyebrow && (
            <p
              className={cn(
                'text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest',
                dark ? 'text-gold-400' : 'text-teal-700',
              )}
            >
              {eyebrow}
            </p>
          )}
          <h2 id={`${id}-heading`} className="mt-2 font-serif text-3xl font-bold md:text-4xl">
            {title}
          </h2>
          {description && (
            <p className={cn('mt-4 text-lg', dark ? 'text-navy-100' : 'text-navy-800')}>
              {description}
            </p>
          )}
        </header>
        <div className="mt-10">{children}</div>
      </Container>
    </section>
  );
}



========== FILE: apps/web/components/marketing/topic-page.test.tsx ==========

import { cleanup, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { SectionContent } from '@/lib/section-content';
import { TopicPage } from './topic-page';

afterEach(cleanup);

const section: SectionContent = {
  slug: 'about',
  title: 'About',
  eyebrow: 'About',
  intro: 'Intro',
  topics: [],
};

describe('TopicPage', () => {
  it('shows the under-development note when the topic has no body', () => {
    render(<TopicPage section={section} topic={{ title: 'Vision', description: 'Short' }} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('note').textContent).toMatch(/under development/i);
  });

  it('shows real copy and no note when the topic has a body', () => {
    render(
      <TopicPage
        section={section}
        topic={{
          title: 'Vision',
          description: 'Short',
          body: ['First paragraph.', 'Second paragraph.'],
        }}
      />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByText('First paragraph.')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText('Second paragraph.')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('note')).toBeNull();
  });
});


========== FILE: apps/web/components/marketing/topic-page.tsx ==========

import Link from 'next/link';
import { Container } from '@impactalign/ui';
import type { SectionContent, TopicItem } from '@/lib/section-content';
import { slugify, topicHref } from '@/lib/topics';
import { PageHero } from './page-hero';

export interface TopicPageProps {
  readonly section: SectionContent;
  readonly topic: TopicItem;
}

/** Sub-page for one topic of a public section. Shows real copy when present, else a labelled placeholder. */
export function TopicPage({ section, topic }: TopicPageProps) {
  const siblings = section.topics.filter((t) => slugify(t.title) !== slugify(topic.title));
  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        title={topic.title}
        eyebrow={section.title}
        intro={topic.description}
        crumbs={[
          { label: 'Home', href: '/' },
          { label: section.title, href: `/${section.slug}` },
          { label: topic.title },
        ]}
      />
      <Container className="py-12 md:py-16">
        {topic.body ? (
          <div className="max-w-3xl space-y-4 leading-7 text-navy-800">
            {topic.body.map((paragraph) => (
              <p key={paragraph}>{paragraph}</p>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <p
            role="note"
            className="max-w-2xl rounded-md border border-gold-500 bg-gold-50 p-4 text-sm text-navy-900"
          >
            Note: This page is under development.
          </p>
        )}
        {siblings.length > 0 && (
          <nav aria-label={`More in ${section.title}`} className="mt-10">
            <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-navy-900">More in {section.title}</h2>
            <ul className="mt-4 grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
              {siblings.map((sibling) => (
                <li key={sibling.title}>
                  <Link
                    href={topicHref(section, sibling)}
                    className="block rounded-lg border border-navy-100 bg-white px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-navy-900 hover:bg-navy-50 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-teal-600"
                  >
                    {sibling.title}
                  </Link>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </nav>
        )}
      </Container>
    </>
  );
}
