========== FILE: apps/web/components/navigation/mobile-menu.tsx ==========

'use client';

import { useEffect, useId, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation';
import { Button } from '@impactalign/ui';
import type { NavItem } from '@impactalign/config';
import { NavLink } from './nav-link';

export interface MobileMenuProps {
  readonly items: readonly NavItem[];
  readonly cta: NavItem;
}

/** Disclosure-pattern menu for small screens: button + list, closes on Escape and navigation. */
export function MobileMenu({ items, cta }: MobileMenuProps) {
  // The menu is open only for the path it was opened on, so navigating closes it without an effect.
  const pathname = usePathname() ?? '';
  const [openPath, setOpenPath] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const open = openPath === pathname;
  const setOpen = (next: boolean) => setOpenPath(next ? pathname : null);
  const panelId = useId();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') setOpenPath(null);
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="lg:hidden">
      <Button
        variant="outline"
        size="sm"
        aria-expanded={open}
        aria-controls={panelId}
        onClick={() => setOpen(!open)}
      >
        {open ? 'Close menu' : 'Menu'}
      </Button>
      <nav
        id={panelId}
        aria-label="Mobile"
        hidden={!open}
        className="absolute inset-x-0 top-full border-b border-navy-100 bg-white px-4 pb-4 shadow-lg"
      >
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1 pt-2">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.href}>
              <NavLink href={item.href} className="block py-3 text-base">
                {item.label}
              </NavLink>
            </li>
          ))}
          <li className="pt-2">
            <Button asChild variant="accent" className="w-full">
              <Link href={cta.href}>{cta.label}</Link>
            </Button>
          </li>
        </ul>
      </nav>
    </div>
  );
}

========== FILE: apps/web/lib/auth/auth-lib.test.ts ==========

/** First message per top-level field from a Zod-style issue list. */
export function fieldErrors(
  issues: readonly { path: PropertyKey[]; message: string }[],
): Record<string, string> {
  const errors: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const issue of issues) {
    const key = String(issue.path[0] ?? 'form');
    errors[key] ??= issue.message;
  }
  return errors;
}



========== FILE: apps/web/lib/auth/next-path.ts ==========

/**
 * Where to go after signing in. Only same-site paths are allowed, so a crafted ?next=https://evil.example
 * link cannot turn the login page into an open redirect.
 */
export function safeNextPath(value: string | null | undefined, fallback = '/dashboard'): string {
  if (!value || !value.startsWith('/') || value.startsWith('//') || value.includes('\\'))
    return fallback;
  for (const char of value) {
    if (char.charCodeAt(0) < 32 || char.charCodeAt(0) === 127) return fallback; // control characters
  }
  return value;
}



========== FILE: apps/web/lib/auth/session.test.ts ==========

import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

const cookieString = vi.fn();
vi.mock('next/headers', () => ({ cookies: async () => ({ toString: cookieString }) }));

import { getSession } from './session';

const fetchMock = vi.fn();
beforeEach(() => {
  vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
  cookieString.mockReturnValue('ia_session=abc123');
});
afterEach(() => {
  fetchMock.mockReset();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
});

describe('getSession (server-side)', () => {
  it('returns null without calling the API when there is no cookie', async () => {
    cookieString.mockReturnValue('');
    expect(await getSession()).toBeNull();
    expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('forwards the cookies to the API and returns the signed-in user', async () => {
    const body = { user: { id: '1', displayName: 'Ada' }, csrfToken: 'c' };
    fetchMock.mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve(body) });
    expect(await getSession()).toEqual(body);
    const [url, init] = fetchMock.mock.calls[0]!;
    expect(url).toBe('http://localhost:4000/api/v1/auth/me');
    expect(init.headers.cookie).toBe('ia_session=abc123');
    expect(init.cache).toBe('no-store');
  });

  it('treats a rejected session as signed out', async () => {
    fetchMock.mockResolvedValue({ ok: false, status: 401, json: () => Promise.resolve({}) });
    expect(await getSession()).toBeNull();
  });

  it('fails closed (null, no throw) when the API is unreachable', async () => {
    fetchMock.mockRejectedValue(new TypeError('fetch failed'));
    await expect(getSession()).resolves.toBeNull();
  });
});



========== FILE: apps/web/lib/auth/session.ts ==========

import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import type { AuthResponse } from '@impactalign/types';

/** Where the Express API lives (server-side only). Same value the /api/v1 rewrite uses. */
const apiUrl = () => (process.env.API_INTERNAL_URL ?? 'http://localhost:4000').replace(/\/$/, '');

/**
 * The signed-in user for this request, or null. Runs on the server: it forwards the browser's cookies
 * to the API, which is the only place that decides whether a session is valid. Never throws, so an
 * unreachable API simply means "not signed in" (fail closed).
 */
export async function getSession(): Promise<AuthResponse | null> {
  const cookieHeader = (await cookies()).toString();
  if (!cookieHeader) return null;
  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${apiUrl()}/api/v1/auth/me`, {
      headers: { cookie: cookieHeader, accept: 'application/json' },
      cache: 'no-store',
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(4_000),
    });
    return response.ok ? ((await response.json()) as AuthResponse) : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function getDashboardTitle(href: string): string {
  const item = dashboardNav.find((n) => n.href === href);
  if (!item) throw new Error(`Unknown dashboard route: ${href}`);
  return item.label;
}

export function getDashboardContent(href: string): DashboardPageContent {
  const content = dashboardContent[href];
  if (!content) throw new Error(`No dashboard content for: ${href}`);
  return content;
}

export function dashboardMetadata(href: string): Metadata {
  return { title: getDashboardTitle(href) };
}

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { FAQ_COUNT, faqGroups } from './faq-content';

const items = faqGroups.flatMap((group) => group.items);

describe('faq content', () => {
  it('holds all 47 questions, numbered 1 to 47 in order', () => {
    expect(FAQ_COUNT).toBe(47);
    expect(items.map((item) => item.number)).toEqual(Array.from({ length: 47 }, (_, n) => n + 1));
  });

  it('has unique group ids and no empty questions or answers', () => {
    const ids = faqGroups.map((group) => group.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
    for (const item of items) {
      expect(item.question.trim()).not.toBe('');
      expect(item.answer.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });
});
/** Accreditation FAQ copy (source: File-3, Frequently Asked Questions - Accreditation). */
/** A string is one paragraph; an array of strings is a bullet list. */
export type FaqBlock = string | readonly string[];

export interface FaqItem {
  readonly number: number;
  readonly question: string;
  readonly answer: readonly FaqBlock[];
}

export interface FaqGroup {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly title: string;
  readonly items: readonly FaqItem[];
}

const q = (number: number, question: string, ...answer: FaqBlock[]): FaqItem => ({
  number,
  question,
  answer,
});

========== FILE: apps/web/lib/legal.ts ==========

import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { legalPages } from '@impactalign/config';

export function getLegalPage(slug: string) {
  const page = legalPages.find((p) => p.slug === slug);
  if (!page) throw new Error(`Unknown legal page: ${slug}`);
  return page;
}

export function legalMetadata(slug: string): Metadata {
  const { title } = getLegalPage(slug);
  return { title, alternates: { canonical: `/${slug}` } };
}

  readonly published?: boolean;
}

  body ? { title, description, body } : { title, description };

========== FILE: apps/web/lib/section-metadata.ts ==========

import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { getSection } from './section-content';

export function sectionMetadata(slug: string): Metadata {
  const { title, intro } = getSection(slug);
  return {
    title,
    description: intro,
    alternates: { canonical: `/${slug}` },
    ...(slug === 'login' ? { robots: { index: false, follow: false } } : {}),
  };
}

========== FILE: apps/web/lib/topics.test.ts ==========

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { dashboardNav } from '@impactalign/config';
import { dashboardContent } from './dashboard-content';
import { sections } from './section-content';
import { allTopicPaths, findTopic, slugify, topicHref } from './topics';

describe('slugify', () => {
  it('produces clean URL slugs', () => {
    expect(slugify('Vision & Mission')).toBe('vision-and-mission');
    expect(slugify('What is ImpactAlign?')).toBe('what-is-impactalign');
    expect(slugify('Events & Webinars')).toBe('events-and-webinars');
  });
});

describe('topics', () => {
  it('has unique slugs within every section', () => {
    for (const section of sections) {
      const slugs = section.topics.map((t) => slugify(t.title));
      expect(new Set(slugs).size, section.slug).toBe(slugs.length);
    }
  });

  it('resolves every listed topic and rejects unknown ones', () => {
    expect(allTopicPaths.length).toBeGreaterThan(40);
    for (const section of sections) {
      for (const topic of section.topics) {
        const [, s, t] = topicHref(section, topic).split('/');
        expect(findTopic(s ?? '', t ?? '')?.topic.title).toBe(topic.title);
      }
    }
    expect(findTopic('about', 'does-not-exist')).toBeNull();
    expect(findTopic('nope', 'governance')).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('dashboard content', () => {
  it('has content for every dashboard route except the overview', () => {
    for (const item of dashboardNav.filter((n) => n.href !== '/dashboard')) {
      expect(dashboardContent[item.href], item.href).toBeDefined();
    }
  });
});



========== FILE: apps/web/lib/topics.ts ==========

import type { TitledItem } from './home-content';
import { sections, type SectionContent, type TopicItem } from './section-content';

/** URL slug for a topic title: "Vision & Mission" -> "vision-and-mission". */
export function slugify(title: string): string {
  return title
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/&/g, ' and ')
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '');
}

export const topicHref = (section: SectionContent, topic: TitledItem): string =>
  `/${section.slug}/${slugify(topic.title)}`;

export function findTopic(
  sectionSlug: string,
  topicSlug: string,
): { section: SectionContent; topic: TopicItem } | null {
  const section = sections.find((s) => s.slug === sectionSlug);
  const topic = section?.topics.find((t) => slugify(t.title) === topicSlug);
  return section && topic ? { section, topic } : null;
}

/** Every public topic route (used by the sitemap). */
export const allTopicPaths: readonly string[] = sections.flatMap((section) =>
  section.topics.map((topic) => topicHref(section, topic)),
);

========== FILE: apps/web/next.config.ts ==========

import path from 'node:path';
import type { NextConfig } from 'next';
import { staticSecurityHeaders } from '@impactalign/config';

/** Where the Express API lives (server-side only). The browser never calls it directly. */
const apiInternalUrl = (process.env.API_INTERNAL_URL ?? 'http://localhost:4000').replace(/\/$/, '');

const nextConfig: NextConfig = {
  reactStrictMode: true,
  poweredByHeader: false,
  output: 'standalone',
  // Monorepo: trace dependencies from the repository root so standalone builds include packages/*.
  outputFileTracingRoot: path.join(__dirname, '../../'),
  async rewrites() {
    // Same-origin API access: cookies stay first-party and no CORS or cross-site cookie setup is needed.
    return [{ source: '/api/v1/:path*', destination: `${apiInternalUrl}/api/v1/:path*` }];
  },
  async redirects() {
    return [
      {
        source: '/join-impactalign/accreditation',
        destination: '/accreditation',
        permanent: false,
      },
    ];
  },
  async headers() {
    return [
      {
        source: '/:path*',
        headers: staticSecurityHeaders.map(({ key, value }) => ({ key, value })),
      },
    ];
  },
};

export default nextConfig;

========== FILE: apps/web/proxy.ts ==========

import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server';
import { buildContentSecurityPolicy, generateNonce } from '@impactalign/config';

/** Sets a per-request, nonce-based Content-Security-Policy (requires dynamic rendering). */
export function proxy(request: NextRequest): NextResponse {
  const nonce = generateNonce();
  const csp = buildContentSecurityPolicy({
    nonce,
    isDev: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development',
    connectSrc: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ? [process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL] : [],
  });

  const requestHeaders = new Headers(request.headers);
  requestHeaders.set('x-nonce', nonce);
  requestHeaders.set('Content-Security-Policy', csp);

  const response = NextResponse.next({ request: { headers: requestHeaders } });
  response.headers.set('Content-Security-Policy', csp);
  return response;
}

export const config = {
  matcher: [
    {
      source: '/((?!_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico).*)',
      missing: [
        { type: 'header', key: 'next-router-prefetch' },
        { type: 'header', key: 'purpose', value: 'prefetch' },
      ],
    },
  ],
};

========== FILE: docs/architecture/database.md ==========

# Database architecture: SDG catalogue and frameworks (Phase 4a)

MongoDB via Mongoose (`packages/database`). The API reads data only through repository
interfaces (`apps/api/src/data/repositories.ts`), implemented for Mongo and in memory.

## Collections
| Model | Purpose | Key indexes |
|---|---|---|
| SDG | The 17 goals | unique `number`, unique `code` |
| SDGTarget | Targets of an SDG | `sdgId`, unique `(sdgId, code)` |
| SDGIndicator | Indicators of an SDG, optionally of a target | `sdgId`, `targetId`, unique `(sdgId, code)` |
| Framework | Assessment framework | unique `slug` |
| FrameworkVersion | One published version of a framework | `frameworkId`, unique `(frameworkId, version)` |
| Criterion | Criterion in one framework version; optional links to SDG, target, indicator | `frameworkVersionId`, unique `(frameworkVersionId, code)`, `sdgId`, `targetId`, `indicatorId` |
| EvidenceRequirement | Evidence needed for a criterion | `criterionId`, unique `(criterionId, code)` |

## Versioning
Criteria and evidence requirements belong to a `FrameworkVersion`, never directly to a
`Framework`. A new version is a new document, so submissions keep pointing at the version
they were assessed against.

## Soft delete
Every model has `deletedAt` (null = active). There is no automatic query filter: repository
implementations must include `deletedAt: null` in every read and set `deletedAt` instead of
deleting.

## SDG data policy
Official SDG data is never invented. `seedSDGs()` inserts only `number` and `code` for goals
1-17; `title`, `description`, `officialUrl` and `metadata` stay `null`. It uses `$setOnInsert`,
so re-running never overwrites curated data. Official titles, targets and indicators must be
imported from a verified official source.

## API (read-only, `/api/v1/sdgs`)
- `GET /` list of SDGs
- `GET /:id` one SDG (`:id` = number 1-17)
- `GET /:id/targets`
- `GET /:id/indicators`

Responses are `{ data }`. `:id` is validated with Zod (`sdgNumberParamSchema`): 400 for
invalid ids, 404 for unknown SDGs. Internal fields (`metadata`, timestamps) are not exposed.


========== FILE: docs/architecture/folder-structure.md ==========

# Folder Structure and Architectural Rules

```
impactalign/
├── apps/
│   ├── web/        Next.js public site (App Router; marketing routes in the (marketing) group)
│   ├── api/        Express REST API (src/: app.ts, main.ts, common/, config/, health/, infrastructure/, openapi/)
│   └── admin/      Next.js admin console (separate app, separate origin)
├── packages/
│   ├── config/     Env schemas, site/brand config, CSP + security headers
│   ├── types/      Shared TypeScript contracts
│   ├── validation/ Shared Zod schemas
│   ├── database/   Mongoose connection + models
│   ├── auth/       (placeholder) authentication & authorization
│   ├── ai/         (interfaces) AI provider abstraction
│   └── ui/         Shared accessible React primitives
├── docs/           architecture · api · security · privacy · product · decisions
├── infrastructure/ docker (dev stores + Dockerfiles) · nginx (template) · deployment (notes)
├── scripts/        run-workspaces.mjs · check-env.mjs · clean.mjs
└── .github/        CI workflow, Dependabot
```

## Dependency direction

```
types ← validation ← (api, web, admin)
config ← (api, web, admin)
database ← api only
ui ← web, admin
```

Rules:

1. **Apps never import from other apps.** Shared code goes in a package.
2. **`database` is imported by `api` only.** Web and admin reach data exclusively through the API.
3. **Business logic lives in the API** (and shared pure logic in packages). Web/admin render and call the
   API; they never re-implement authorization or validation rules — they may reuse the same Zod schema
   from `packages/validation` for form UX, but the server always re-validates.
4. **Shared contracts live in `packages/types`;** schemas that must match them use
   `satisfies z.ZodType<...>` so the two cannot drift.
5. **Public marketing and authenticated areas are separate route groups/layouts;** admin is a separate app.
6. **Packages build to `dist/` (CommonJS + `.d.ts`)** and are consumed through their `main`/`types`. Build
   order is enforced by `scripts/run-workspaces.mjs` (config → types → validation → database → auth → ai → ui).
7. **No secrets in code.** Configuration enters through environment variables validated by `packages/config`.
8. **Components stay small and reusable;** no monolithic page components.

## Where things go (Phase 2 will add the empty feature folders)

| Concern                 | Location                                             |
| ----------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- |
| New REST feature        | `apps/api/src/<feature>/` (router, service, schemas) |
| Mongoose model          | `packages/database/src/models/`                      |
| Request/response type   | `packages/types/src/`                                |
| Input validation schema | `packages/validation/src/`                           |
| Reusable UI primitive   | `packages/ui/src/components/`                        |
| Page-specific component | `apps/<app>/components/<area>/`                      |



========== FILE: docs/architecture/system-architecture.md ==========



========== FILE: docs/decisions/ADR-001-initial-architecture.md ==========

# ADR-001: Initial architecture

- **Status:** Accepted
- **Date:** 2026-10-08

## Context

ImpactAlign SDG needs a maintainable, secure, internationally extensible platform built incrementally. The
original brief proposed Next.js + NestJS + PostgreSQL/Prisma (+ pgvector). The project owner then specified the
backend as **Node.js, Express.js and MongoDB**.

## Decision

- **Monorepo with npm workspaces** (`apps/*`, `packages/*`); no extra orchestrator yet.
- **Frontend:** Next.js 16 (App Router), React 19, TypeScript, Tailwind CSS 4, shadcn/ui-style components.
- **Backend:** Node.js 22, Express 5, TypeScript, REST + OpenAPI; **MongoDB via Mongoose**; Redis (ioredis).
- **Validation:** Zod everywhere; shared schemas in `packages/validation`, shared types in `packages/types`.
- **Packages compile to CommonJS + `.d.ts`** and are consumed from `dist/` so the Express API and Next apps
  share them without per-app transpilation.
- **Security by default:** nonce-based CSP, helmet, CORS allow-list, rate limiting, env validation that fails fast.
- **Testing:** Vitest (+ Supertest for the API, Testing Library for React).

## Deviations from the original brief

| Brief               | Chosen                                                            | Reason                                                                                                    |
| ------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| NestJS              | Express 5                                                         | Owner-specified backend                                                                                   |
| PostgreSQL + Prisma | MongoDB + Mongoose                                                | Owner-specified backend                                                                                   |
| pgvector            | **Undecided**                                                     | Needs an alternative for semantic search (e.g. Atlas Vector Search or a vector store) before the AI phase |
| shadcn/ui CLI       | Hand-written shadcn-style primitives (`components.json` included) | The CLI needs registry access; the pattern (Radix Slot + CVA + `cn`) is identical                         |
| Husky/lint-staged   | As specified                                                      | —                                                                                                         |

## Assumptions (documented per the "choose the safest option" rule)

1. **npm workspaces, not pnpm/Turborepo** — fewest moving parts; a build orchestrator can be added if build time grows.
2. **ESLint 9** — `eslint-plugin-jsx-a11y` does not yet allow ESLint 10 in its peer range, and accessibility linting is required.
3. **TypeScript 5.9** — chosen conservatively; newer major versions were not evaluated against the toolchain.
4. **Brand tokens** (ink navy, evidence teal, recognition gold) were carried over from the earlier prototype as _proposed_ values (`#0f2040`, Tailwind teal, `#c9a227`); replace with the official palette if it differs.
5. **System font stack** — avoids third-party font requests (privacy and CSP); revisit with self-hosted fonts.
6. **All web/admin pages render dynamically** because a per-request CSP nonce is required. Static caching can be reintroduced selectively (e.g. hash-based CSP for fully static pages).
7. **Public routes** use `/join-impactalign`; legal pages sit at the root (`/privacy`, …) as specified in Phase 1.
8. **Admin is a separate app/origin** and must not be exposed publicly until authentication exists.
9. **Legal pages are placeholders** with no legal claims; the LICENSE is a proprietary placeholder; the security contact is unset.
10. **In-memory rate limiting** is per instance; a Redis-backed store is required before scaling out.

## Consequences

- (+) Fewer frameworks to learn; flexible document model suits evolving SDG frameworks and evidence metadata.
- (−) No database-enforced relations or row-level security: referential integrity and tenant isolation must be enforced in code and tests.
- (−) Vector search strategy is an open decision.
- (−) Dynamic rendering of every page trades CDN cacheability for a strict CSP.

## Alternatives considered

- Keep NestJS + Prisma/PostgreSQL — rejected: contradicts the owner's stack choice.
- Turborepo/pnpm — deferred (see assumption 1).
- Static CSP with `unsafe-inline` — rejected: weakens XSS protection.



========== FILE: docs/decisions/ADR-002-dashboard-preview-and-topic-pages.md ==========

# ADR-002: Dashboard UI preview gate and topic sub-pages

- **Status:** Accepted
- **Date:** 2026-10-11

## Context

The dashboard interface and the public section sub-pages were requested before authentication (Phase 3) and the
database (Phase 4) exist. A dashboard without authentication must never look functional or expose data.

## Decision

1. **Dashboard is a UI preview only.** `/dashboard/*` has its own layout (separate from the marketing shell), 15
   routes from the product spec, empty states, a visible "Preview · not signed in" badge and a note that nothing
   is saved. It contains no data, forms or fake numbers.
2. **Production gate.** `isDashboardPreviewEnabled()` makes the dashboard visible outside production and returns
   404 in production unless `DASHBOARD_PREVIEW_ENABLED=true`. The gate is server-side. It is a stop-gap, **not**
   access control; real server-side, role- and organization-aware authorization replaces it in Phase 3.
3. **Dashboard is never indexed:** `noindex` metadata, `Disallow: /dashboard` in robots, absent from the sitemap.
4. **Topic sub-pages** (`/<section>/<topic>`) are generated from `lib/section-content.ts`. Each is a labelled
   placeholder with breadcrumbs and links to sibling topics; unknown combinations return 404.

## Consequences

- (+) Navigation and information architecture can be reviewed now without implying finished features.
- (+) Safe default in production.
- (−) Routes and nav labels will need to follow the real feature set when Phase 7 is built.
- (−) Section content is code-resident placeholder copy until a content source exists.

========== FILE: docs/privacy/README.md ==========

# Privacy

Phase 1 stores **no personal data**. This folder will hold the privacy-by-design documentation as features land.

## Design commitments (to be implemented and verified in later phases)

- Data minimisation and purpose limitation: collect only what a feature needs.
- Consent captured where required, with a record of what was agreed.
- User data export and account deletion.
- Privacy preferences and retention policies per data type.
- Sensitive evidence documents stored privately and never exposed through public URLs.
- Audit trail for access to sensitive data.

The legal pages (`/privacy`, `/data-protection`, …) are placeholders; the final text needs review by qualified
counsel. Do not publish compliance claims until they are true and verified.



========== FILE: docs/product/phase-1-report.md ==========

# Phase 1 report — Project foundation

## Verification (fresh clone, CI sequence)

| Step                                      | Result                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `npm ci`                                  | pass                                                                       |
| `npm run format:check`                    | pass                                                                       |
| `npm run lint` (0 warnings)               | pass                                                                       |
| `npm run typecheck`                       | pass                                                                       |
| `npm test`                                | pass — 40 tests (config 11, validation 5, database 3, ui 4, api 14, web 3) |
| `npm run build`                           | pass — packages, API, web (22 routes), admin                               |
| `npm audit --omit=dev --audit-level=high` | 0 vulnerabilities                                                          |

Also verified by running the real processes: API health endpoints (200 / 503 with no detail leakage), live Redis
outage and automatic recovery, fail-fast env validation, web security headers + nonce CSP on a production
build, robots/sitemap, Husky pre-commit blocking `any`, and the API Docker runtime install simulated outside Docker.

## Database changes

None applied. `packages/database` defines one model (`AuditLog`, append-only at the Mongoose layer). MongoDB
creates collections/indexes on first use (index auto-build is disabled in production).

## Environment variables added

`NODE_ENV`, `LOG_LEVEL`, `API_HOST`, `API_PORT`, `API_CORS_ORIGINS`, `API_TRUST_PROXY`, `API_SWAGGER_ENABLED`,
`RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS`, `RATE_LIMIT_MAX`, `HEALTH_CHECK_TIMEOUT_MS`, `MONGO_ROOT_USER`, `MONGO_ROOT_PASSWORD`,
`REDIS_PASSWORD`, `MONGODB_URI`, `REDIS_URL`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL`.
Reserved but unused: SMTP, S3, LLM variables (commented in `.env.example`).

## Known limitations

1. **MongoDB not exercised live.** No `mongod` was available; `/health/database` is verified through tests and by
   its failure path only. Run `npm run dev:infra` and check `GET /health/database` returns 200.
2. **Docker files not built or run** (no Docker available). Run `docker compose ... config` and a first build.
3. **Dev-tooling audit findings:** `npm audit` (including dev dependencies) reports high-severity issues in the
   lint chain (`@next/eslint-plugin-next` → `fast-glob` → `micromatch` → `braces`). They do not ship at runtime;
   the CI audit checks production dependencies only.
4. Rate limiter is per-process (needs a Redis store before scaling out).
5. All web/admin pages render dynamically (required by the nonce CSP).
6. Brand tokens are proposed values; legal pages, LICENSE and the security contact are placeholders.
7. Vector search strategy (pgvector replacement) is undecided.
8. No browser/E2E or accessibility audit tooling yet; accessibility is addressed by semantic markup, skip link,
   labelled landmarks, `aria-current`, disclosure-pattern mobile menu, and `jsx-a11y` lint rules.

## Next recommended phase

**Phase 2 — Application architecture:** create the empty feature folders and READMEs for web/api/admin and
add the architectural-rule documentation. Before Phase 3, decide the session strategy (cookie sessions vs tokens)
since it drives CSRF and the web↔API origin setup.



========== FILE: docs/product/product-overview.md ==========

# Product Overview

**ImpactAlign SDG** is an AI-assisted platform that connects real-world activities and evidence to the
Sustainable Development Goals, with human validation and, eventually, accreditation.

## Who it is for

Entrepreneurs, professionals, professors, researchers, organizations, institutions, practitioners,
partners and assessors.

## The chain it makes verifiable

```
SDG Goal → SDG Target → SDG Indicator → Evidence → Impact → Validation → Accreditation
```

## Methodology

**Diagnose → Align → Measure → Validate → Improve**

## Principles

1. **Human-in-the-loop.** AI may suggest; it never makes the final validation or accreditation decision.
   AI output is always labelled as AI-generated.
2. **Organization data is isolated.** One organization can never see another's private data.
3. **Privacy by design.** Data minimisation, purpose limitation, consent where required, export and deletion.
4. **Honest claims.** No invented certifications, legal claims or official SDG data.
5. **Accessible and international.** Responsive, keyboard-accessible, semantic, ready for localisation.

## Brand and navigation

- Product name: **ImpactAlign SDG**; primary call to action: **JOIN IMPACTALIGN**
- Navigation order: HOME · ABOUT · SDGs · HOW IT WORKS · PLATFORM · RESOURCES · COMMUNITY · LOGIN · JOIN IMPACTALIGN
- Legal pages (placeholders): /privacy /terms /cookies /ai-ethics /data-protection /accessibility /security /acceptable-use /disclaimer

## What exists today (Phase 1)

Foundation, a redesigned public site (home + section and topic pages, all clearly marked placeholders), a dashboard
**interface preview** (15 routes, empty states, no data), a placeholder admin app, CI and local Docker data stores.
**No authentication, stored data, submissions or SDG content yet.**

## Earlier prototype

A separate working prototype (Express + SQLite, vanilla JS) explored registration, seven submission types and
a four-step wizard. It is not part of this repository; its submission types and wizard configuration are
candidates to port in the dashboard/evidence phase.

========== FILE: docs/security/security-architecture.md ==========

# Security Architecture

This document separates what **exists in Phase 1** from what is **planned**. Nothing planned should be
described to users as a security guarantee until it is implemented and tested.

## Implemented in Phase 1

| Control                            | Where                                               | Notes                                                                                                                                               |
| ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Secrets out of source              | `.env.example` placeholders, `.gitignore`           | Real `.env*` files are git-ignored                                                                                                                  |
| Fail-fast env validation           | `packages/config/src/env.ts`                        | Errors list variable names, never values. In production it rejects placeholder credentials, non-HTTPS CORS origins and Swagger UI                   |
| Security headers (API)             | `helmet` in `apps/api/src/app.ts`                   | `default-src 'none'`, `frame-ancestors 'none'`, HSTS, nosniff, no-referrer; `X-Powered-By` removed                                                  |
| Security headers + CSP (web/admin) | `next.config.ts`, `proxy.ts`                        | Per-request **nonce-based CSP** (no `unsafe-inline`/`unsafe-eval` in production), HSTS, X-Frame-Options, Referrer-Policy, Permissions-Policy, COOP  |
| CORS allow-list                    | API                                                 | Only configured origins receive CORS headers                                                                                                        |
| Rate limiting                      | `express-rate-limit`                                | Per-IP; health probes exempt. In-memory store (per instance)                                                                                        |
| Body limits + safe parsing         | API                                                 | 100 kB JSON cap; malformed JSON → 400, oversized → 413                                                                                              |
| Secure error handling              | `common/error-handler.ts`                           | Uniform envelope with request ID; 500s hide internals; probe failures never expose hosts                                                            |
| Structured logging                 | `pino` / `pino-http`                                | Authorization, cookies and `*.password/token/secret` redacted                                                                                       |
| NoSQL-injection hardening          | `connectMongo` (`sanitizeFilter`), `objectIdSchema` | Operator objects in untrusted filters are neutralised; ids validated as 24-hex                                                                      |
| Audit-log foundation               | `packages/database` `AuditLog`                      | Append-only at the Mongoose layer (update/delete hooks throw)                                                                                       |
| Dashboard preview gate             | `apps/web/lib/dashboard-gate.ts`                    | `/dashboard` returns 404 in production unless `DASHBOARD_PREVIEW_ENABLED=true`. UI-only stop-gap — **not** access control (see ADR-002)             |
| Crawler controls                   | `robots.ts`, `noindex` on admin/login               | Admin is never indexable                                                                                                                            |
| Container hardening                | `infrastructure/docker`                             | Non-root runtime user, prod-only dependencies, dev ports bound to 127.0.0.1 (**Dockerfiles not built in this environment — see Known limitations**) |
| Supply chain                       | CI audit job, Dependabot                            | `npm audit --omit=dev --audit-level=high`                                                                                                           |

## Planned (not implemented)

- **Authentication & sessions (Phase 3):** strong password hashing, secure cookie sessions, MFA-ready
  design, email verification, one-time reset tokens, login throttling/lockout, CSRF protection for
  cookie-authenticated routes.
- **Authorization (Phase 3):** permissions separate from roles; **server-side** checks on every protected
  endpoint; organization-level access control.
- **Organization isolation (Phase 3–4):** repository layer requiring an org scope + cross-org tests.
- **Evidence uploads (Phase 7):** MIME/extension/size validation, filename sanitisation, private object
  storage, access-controlled downloads, malware-scan integration point, audit events.
- **AI governance (AI phase):** AI output labelled and logged; final validation and accreditation decisions
  are made by humans only (`packages/ai` encodes the label constant and the rule).
- **Distributed rate limiting** (Redis-backed) before multi-instance deployment.

## MongoDB-specific notes

- **Operator injection:** validate every request body/query with Zod before it reaches a query; never
  spread request objects into filters. `sanitizeFilter` is a second line of defence, not the first.
- **Tenant isolation is application-enforced** (no row-level security). Treat any query without an
  organization scope on tenant data as a bug.
- **Audit immutability:** the model hooks only stop accidental use of the Mongoose API. For real
  tamper-resistance, give the application's database user insert-only rights on the audit collection and
  keep admin credentials out of the app.
- **Production database:** dedicated least-privilege users, TLS in transit, encryption at rest, backups
  with tested restores. The Docker dev root user is for local use only.

## Trust boundaries

Browser → (web/admin | API) is untrusted. The API validates all input and is the only component that
talks to MongoDB, Redis, object storage and AI providers. Web and admin hold no secrets.

## Reporting

See [`SECURITY.md`](../../SECURITY.md). The security contact is a placeholder until the owner sets one.



========== FILE: infrastructure/deployment/README.md ==========

# Deployment

Phase 1 ships **templates only** — no environment is provisioned and no secrets live in this repo.

## Production checklist (to complete in a later phase)

- Provide real secrets through the platform's secret manager, never through files in git.
- Use dedicated, least-privilege MongoDB users (not the dev root user) and enable TLS to MongoDB/Redis.
- Set `NODE_ENV=production`; the API refuses placeholder credentials, non-HTTPS CORS origins and
  Swagger UI in production.
- Set `API_TRUST_PROXY` to the number of proxies in front of the API.
- Terminate TLS at the reverse proxy (`../nginx/nginx.conf` is a starting template) and restrict the
  admin host by network until authentication exists.
- Back the API rate limiter with Redis before running more than one API instance (the in-memory
  store is per-process).
- Configure MongoDB backups and a retention policy (see `docs/privacy`).



========== FILE: infrastructure/docker/README.md ==========

# Docker

- `docker-compose.dev.yml` — MongoDB 8 + Redis 7 for local development (ports bound to 127.0.0.1, credentials from `.env`).
- `docker-compose.apps.yml` — optional: also run the API, web and admin in containers.
- `Dockerfile.api`, `Dockerfile.next` — multi-stage, non-root production images.

Start the data stores: `npm run dev:infra` (needs `.env`). **Note:** these files have not been executed in the
environment where Phase 1 was produced (no Docker available) — verify with `docker compose config` and a first build.



========== FILE: infrastructure/nginx/README.md ==========

# nginx

`nginx.conf` is a reverse-proxy **template** (example.org placeholders, TLS paths commented out, admin host to be
restricted by network). Not used by local development.

========== FILE: packages/ai/src/index.ts ==========

/**
 * @impactalign/ai — provider abstraction (interfaces only in Phase 1).
 *
 * Non-negotiable rules encoded here so later phases cannot forget them:
 *  - AI output is always labelled as AI-generated.
 *  - AI never makes the final validation or accreditation decision; a human assessor does.
 */
export const AI_GENERATED_LABEL = 'AI-generated recommendation — requires human review' as const;

export interface AiCompletionRequest {
  readonly prompt: string;
  readonly maxOutputTokens?: number;
}

export interface AiRecommendation {
  readonly text: string;
  readonly generatedByAi: true;
  readonly label: typeof AI_GENERATED_LABEL;
  readonly provider: string;
  readonly model: string;
}

/** Implemented per vendor behind this interface so providers stay swappable. */
export interface AiProvider {
  readonly name: string;
  complete(request: AiCompletionRequest): Promise<AiRecommendation>;
  embed(texts: readonly string[]): Promise<number[][]>;
}

========== FILE: packages/api-client/src/index.test.ts ==========


import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { apiFetch, setCsrfToken } from './index';

describe('shared API client', () => {
  afterEach(() => {
    setCsrfToken(null);
    vi.unstubAllGlobals();
  });

  it('sends the in-memory CSRF token with profile PATCH requests', async () => {
    const fetchMock = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
      ok: true,
      status: 200,
      json: async () => ({ user: { displayName: 'New Name' } }),
    });

    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    setCsrfToken('csrf-test');

    await apiFetch('/users/me', {
      method: 'PATCH',
      body: { displayName: 'New Name' },
    });

    expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/v1/users/me',
      expect.objectContaining({
        method: 'PATCH',
        credentials: 'same-origin',
        headers: expect.objectContaining({
          Accept: 'application/json',
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'X-CSRF-Token': 'csrf-test',
        }),
        body: JSON.stringify({ displayName: 'New Name' }),
      }),
    );
  });
});



========== FILE: packages/api-client/src/index.ts ==========

import type { PublicUser, ApiErrorBody, ApiErrorDetail, AuthResponse } from '@impactalign/types';
/** Error returned by the API (or a network failure, status 0). Messages are safe to show to users. */
export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly status: number,
    readonly code: string,
    message: string,
    readonly details: ApiErrorDetail[] = [],
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ApiError';
  }
}

/** Kept in memory only (never localStorage): sent as X-CSRF-Token on state-changing requests. */
let csrfToken: string | null = null;
export const setCsrfToken = (token: string | null): void => {
  csrfToken = token;
};

const UNSAFE_METHODS = new Set(['POST', 'PUT', 'PATCH', 'DELETE']);

export async function apiFetch<T>(
  path: string,
  init: { method?: string; body?: unknown } = {},
): Promise<T> {
  const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
  const headers: Record<string, string> = { Accept: 'application/json' };
  if (init.body !== undefined) headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
  if (UNSAFE_METHODS.has(method) && csrfToken) headers['X-CSRF-Token'] = csrfToken;

  let response: Response;
  try {
    response = await fetch(`/api/v1${path}`, {
      method,
      headers,
      credentials: 'same-origin',
      ...(init.body !== undefined ? { body: JSON.stringify(init.body) } : {}),
    });
  } catch {
    throw new ApiError(
      0,
      'NETWORK_ERROR',
      'Cannot reach the server. Check your connection and try again.',
    );
  }

  if (response.status === 204) return undefined as T;
  const data: unknown = await response.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!response.ok) {
    const error = (data as ApiErrorBody | null)?.error;
    throw new ApiError(
      response.status,
      error?.code ?? 'UNKNOWN',
      error?.message ?? 'Something went wrong',
      error?.details,
    );
  }
  return data as T;
}

export interface RegisterBody {
  email: string;
  password: string;
  displayName: string;
  accountType: string;
}

export const authApi = {
  register: (body: RegisterBody) =>
    apiFetch<{ message: string }>('/auth/register', { method: 'POST', body }),
  verifyEmail: (token: string) =>
    apiFetch<{ message: string }>('/auth/verify-email', { method: 'POST', body: { token } }),
  async login(email: string, password: string): Promise<AuthResponse> {
    const result = await apiFetch<AuthResponse>('/auth/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: { email, password },
    });
    setCsrfToken(result.csrfToken);
    return result;
  },
  async me(): Promise<AuthResponse> {
    const result = await apiFetch<AuthResponse>('/auth/me');
    setCsrfToken(result.csrfToken);
    return result;
  },
  async logout(): Promise<void> {
    await apiFetch<void>('/auth/logout', { method: 'POST', body: {} });
    setCsrfToken(null);
  },
  updateProfile: (displayName: string) =>
   apiFetch<{ user: PublicUser }>('/users/me', {
      method: 'PATCH',
      body: { displayName },
    }),
  forgotPassword: (email: string) =>
    apiFetch<{ message: string }>('/auth/forgot-password', { method: 'POST', body: { email } }),
  resetPassword: (token: string, password: string) =>
    apiFetch<{ message: string }>('/auth/reset-password', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: { token, password },
    }),
};

/** Turns any error into a friendly sentence. Never shows raw server details. */
export function describeError(error: unknown): string {
  if (!(error instanceof ApiError)) return 'Something went wrong. Please try again.';
  switch (error.code) {
    case 'INVALID_CREDENTIALS':
      return 'Invalid email or password.';
    case 'EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED':
      return 'Please verify your email first. Check your inbox for the verification link.';
    case 'TOO_MANY_ATTEMPTS':
    case 'RATE_LIMITED':
      return 'Too many attempts. Please wait a few minutes and try again.';
    case 'INVALID_OR_EXPIRED_TOKEN':
      return 'This link is invalid or has expired. Please request a new one.';
    case 'VALIDATION_ERROR':
      return 'Please check the highlighted fields and try again.';
    default:
      return error.message;
  }
}


// The package is shared by both Next.js applications; keep request semantics in one place.

========== FILE: packages/auth/src/auth.test.ts ==========

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { Permission, PLATFORM_ROLES, ORGANIZATION_ROLES } from '@impactalign/types';
import {
  canAssignRoles,
  createScryptHasher,
  generateToken,
  hasOrganizationPermission,
  hasPlatformPermission,
  hashToken,
  organizationRolePermissions,
  platformRolePermissions,
  safeEqual,
} from './index';

const cheap = createScryptHasher({ log2N: 4 });

describe('password hashing', () => {
  it('never stores the plaintext and verifies the right password only', async () => {
    const hash = await cheap.hash('correct horse battery staple');
    expect(hash).not.toContain('correct horse');
    expect(hash.startsWith('scrypt$4$8$1$')).toBe(true);
    expect(await cheap.verify('correct horse battery staple', hash)).toBe(true);
    expect(await cheap.verify('wrong password here', hash)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('uses a fresh salt every time', async () => {
    expect(await cheap.hash('same-password-123')).not.toBe(await cheap.hash('same-password-123'));
  });

  it('rejects malformed or tampered stored hashes instead of throwing', async () => {
    expect(await cheap.verify('x', 'garbage')).toBe(false);
    expect(await cheap.verify('x', 'scrypt$99$8$1$AAAA$BBBB')).toBe(false);
    expect(await cheap.verify('x', 'bcrypt$4$8$1$AAAA$BBBB')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('flags hashes made with weaker parameters for rehash', async () => {
    const strong = createScryptHasher({ log2N: 5 });
    expect(strong.needsRehash(await cheap.hash('pw-pw-pw-pw-pw'))).toBe(true);
    expect(cheap.needsRehash(await cheap.hash('pw-pw-pw-pw-pw'))).toBe(false);
    expect(cheap.needsRehash('not-a-hash')).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('tokens', () => {
  it('returns a random token and a different stored hash', () => {
    const a = generateToken();
    const b = generateToken();
    expect(a.token).not.toBe(b.token);
    expect(a.token.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(43);
    expect(a.hash).toBe(hashToken(a.token));
    expect(a.hash).not.toContain(a.token);
  });
  it('compares secrets safely', () => {
    expect(safeEqual('abc', 'abc')).toBe(true);
    expect(safeEqual('abc', 'abd')).toBe(false);
    expect(safeEqual('abc', 'abcd')).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('permission policy', () => {
  it('maps every role to a permission list', () => {
    for (const role of PLATFORM_ROLES) expect(platformRolePermissions[role]).toBeDefined();
    for (const role of ORGANIZATION_ROLES) expect(organizationRolePermissions[role]).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('gives SUPER_ADMIN everything and PUBLIC_USER nothing', () => {
    for (const permission of Object.values(Permission)) {
      expect(hasPlatformPermission(['SUPER_ADMIN'], permission)).toBe(true);
      expect(hasPlatformPermission(['PUBLIC_USER'], permission)).toBe(false);
    }
  });

  it('keeps platform staff out of organizations private data', () => {
    expect(hasPlatformPermission(['ADMIN'], Permission.ORG_READ)).toBe(false);
    expect(hasPlatformPermission(['ADMIN'], Permission.EVIDENCE_UPLOAD)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('distinguishes organization admins from members', () => {
    expect(hasOrganizationPermission('ORGANIZATION_ADMIN', Permission.ORG_MEMBER_MANAGE)).toBe(
      true,
    );
    expect(hasOrganizationPermission('ORGANIZATION_MEMBER', Permission.ORG_MEMBER_MANAGE)).toBe(
      false,
    );
    expect(hasOrganizationPermission('ORGANIZATION_MEMBER', Permission.EVIDENCE_SUBMIT)).toBe(
      false,
    );
  });
});

describe('canAssignRoles (privilege escalation)', () => {
  const base = { actorId: 'a', targetId: 't', targetRoles: ['PUBLIC_USER'] as const };

  it('requires the USER_ROLE_ASSIGN permission', () => {
    const r = canAssignRoles({
      ...base,
      actorRoles: ['PROFESSIONAL'],
      targetRoles: ['PUBLIC_USER'],
      newRoles: ['ASSESSOR'],
    });
    expect(r).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'missing_permission' });
  });
  it('blocks changing your own roles, even for SUPER_ADMIN', () => {
    const r = canAssignRoles({
      actorId: 'a',
      targetId: 'a',
      actorRoles: ['SUPER_ADMIN'],
      targetRoles: ['SUPER_ADMIN'],
      newRoles: ['PUBLIC_USER'],
    });
    expect(r).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'cannot_change_own_roles' });
  });
  it('stops ADMIN granting staff roles or editing staff', () => {
    expect(
      canAssignRoles({
        ...base,
        actorRoles: ['ADMIN'],
        targetRoles: ['PUBLIC_USER'],
        newRoles: ['SUPER_ADMIN'],
      }),
    ).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'cannot_grant_staff_role' });
    expect(
      canAssignRoles({
        ...base,
        actorRoles: ['ADMIN'],
        targetRoles: ['ADMIN'],
        newRoles: ['PUBLIC_USER'],
      }),
    ).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'target_is_staff' });
  });
  it('lets ADMIN assign ordinary roles and SUPER_ADMIN assign anything', () => {
    expect(
      canAssignRoles({
        ...base,
        actorRoles: ['ADMIN'],
        targetRoles: ['PUBLIC_USER'],
        newRoles: ['ASSESSOR'],
      }),
    ).toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(
      canAssignRoles({
        ...base,
        actorRoles: ['SUPER_ADMIN'],
        targetRoles: ['PUBLIC_USER'],
        newRoles: ['ADMIN'],
      }),
    ).toEqual({ ok: true });
  });
});

========== FILE: packages/auth/src/password.ts ==========

import { randomBytes, scrypt as scryptCallback, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';
import { promisify } from 'node:util';

const scrypt = promisify(scryptCallback) as (
  password: string,
  salt: Buffer,
  keylen: number,
  options: { N: number; r: number; p: number; maxmem: number },
) => Promise<Buffer>;

export interface PasswordHasher {
  hash(plain: string): Promise<string>;
  verify(plain: string, stored: string): Promise<boolean>;
  /** True when the stored hash was made with weaker parameters than the current ones. */
  needsRehash(stored: string): boolean;
}

export interface ScryptOptions {
  /** log2 of the CPU/memory cost N. 17 (=128 MiB) follows the OWASP minimum; tests may lower it. */
  readonly log2N?: number;
  readonly r?: number;
  readonly p?: number;
}

const KEY_LENGTH = 64;
const SALT_LENGTH = 16;
const FORMAT = 'scrypt';

/** scrypt (memory-hard) via Node's built-in crypto: no native dependency to build or deploy. */
export function createScryptHasher(options: ScryptOptions = {}): PasswordHasher {
  const log2N = options.log2N ?? 17;
  const r = options.r ?? 8;
  const p = options.p ?? 1;
  const maxmem = 128 * 2 ** log2N * r * 2 + 1024 * 1024;

  const derive = (plain: string, salt: Buffer, n: number, rr: number, pp: number) =>
    scrypt(plain.normalize('NFKC'), salt, KEY_LENGTH, {
      N: n,
      r: rr,
      p: pp,
      maxmem: Math.max(maxmem, 128 * n * rr * 2 + 1024 * 1024),
    });

  return {
    async hash(plain) {
      const salt = randomBytes(SALT_LENGTH);
      const key = await derive(plain, salt, 2 ** log2N, r, p);
      return [FORMAT, log2N, r, p, salt.toString('base64url'), key.toString('base64url')].join('$');
    },

    async verify(plain, stored) {
      const parts = stored.split('$');
      if (parts.length !== 6 || parts[0] !== FORMAT) return false;
      const [, nLog, rStr, pStr, saltB64, keyB64] = parts as [
        string,
        string,
        string,
        string,
        string,
        string,
      ];
      const n = 2 ** Number(nLog);
      const rr = Number(rStr);
      const pp = Number(pStr);
      if (![n, rr, pp].every((v) => Number.isInteger(v) && v > 0) || Number(nLog) > 20)
        return false;
      const expected = Buffer.from(keyB64, 'base64url');
      const actual = await derive(plain, Buffer.from(saltB64, 'base64url'), n, rr, pp);
      return expected.length === actual.length && timingSafeEqual(expected, actual);
    },

    needsRehash(stored) {
      const parts = stored.split('$');
      if (parts.length !== 6 || parts[0] !== FORMAT) return true;
      return Number(parts[1]) < log2N || Number(parts[2]) < r || Number(parts[3]) < p;
    },
  };
}



========== FILE: packages/auth/src/policy.ts ==========

import { Permission, type OrganizationRole, type PlatformRole } from '@impactalign/types';

const P = Permission;

/**
 * Platform-wide permissions per role. Deliberately NO organization data permissions for ADMIN:
 * platform staff do not get blanket access to organizations' private data (isolation rule).
 */
export const platformRolePermissions: Readonly<Record<PlatformRole, readonly Permission[]>> = {
  SUPER_ADMIN: Object.values(Permission),
  ADMIN: [P.ADMIN_ACCESS, P.USER_READ, P.USER_UPDATE, P.USER_ROLE_ASSIGN, P.AUDIT_READ],
  ASSESSOR: [P.ASSESSMENT_REVIEW, P.ACCREDITATION_REVIEW],
  REVIEWER: [P.ASSESSMENT_REVIEW],
  PROFESSIONAL: [
    P.ORG_CREATE,
    P.PROJECT_CREATE,
    P.PROJECT_READ,
    P.PROJECT_UPDATE,
    P.EVIDENCE_UPLOAD,
    P.EVIDENCE_SUBMIT,
  ],
  RESEARCHER: [
    P.ORG_CREATE,
    P.PROJECT_CREATE,
    P.PROJECT_READ,
    P.PROJECT_UPDATE,
    P.EVIDENCE_UPLOAD,
    P.EVIDENCE_SUBMIT,
  ],
  ACADEMIC: [
    P.ORG_CREATE,
    P.PROJECT_CREATE,
    P.PROJECT_READ,
    P.PROJECT_UPDATE,
    P.EVIDENCE_UPLOAD,
    P.EVIDENCE_SUBMIT,
  ],
  PARTNER: [P.ORG_CREATE],
  PUBLIC_USER: [],
};

/** Permissions a member holds inside ONE organization (never applies to other organizations). */
export const organizationRolePermissions: Readonly<
  Record<OrganizationRole, readonly Permission[]>
> = {
  ORGANIZATION_ADMIN: [
    P.ORG_READ,
    P.ORG_UPDATE,
    P.ORG_MEMBER_MANAGE,
    P.PROJECT_CREATE,
    P.PROJECT_READ,
    P.PROJECT_UPDATE,
    P.EVIDENCE_UPLOAD,
    P.EVIDENCE_SUBMIT,
  ],
  ORGANIZATION_MEMBER: [
    P.ORG_READ,
    P.PROJECT_CREATE,
    P.PROJECT_READ,
    P.PROJECT_UPDATE,
    P.EVIDENCE_UPLOAD,
  ],
};

export function hasPlatformPermission(
  roles: readonly PlatformRole[],
  permission: Permission,
): boolean {
  return roles.some((role) => platformRolePermissions[role]?.includes(permission) === true);
}

export function hasOrganizationPermission(role: OrganizationRole, permission: Permission): boolean {
  return organizationRolePermissions[role]?.includes(permission) === true;
}

const STAFF_ROLES: readonly PlatformRole[] = ['SUPER_ADMIN', 'ADMIN'];
const isStaff = (roles: readonly PlatformRole[]) => roles.some((r) => STAFF_ROLES.includes(r));

export type RoleAssignmentDecision = { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: string };

export interface RoleAssignmentRequest {
  readonly actorId: string;
  readonly actorRoles: readonly PlatformRole[];
  readonly targetId: string;
  readonly targetRoles: readonly PlatformRole[];
  readonly newRoles: readonly PlatformRole[];
}

/**
 * Privilege-escalation rules for changing someone's platform roles:
 *  - the actor needs USER_ROLE_ASSIGN;
 *  - nobody changes their own roles;
 *  - only SUPER_ADMIN may grant or revoke ADMIN/SUPER_ADMIN, or modify a staff account.
 */
export function canAssignRoles(request: RoleAssignmentRequest): RoleAssignmentDecision {
  const { actorId, actorRoles, targetId, targetRoles, newRoles } = request;
  if (!hasPlatformPermission(actorRoles, Permission.USER_ROLE_ASSIGN)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'missing_permission' };
  }
  if (actorId === targetId) return { ok: false, reason: 'cannot_change_own_roles' };

  const actorIsSuper = actorRoles.includes('SUPER_ADMIN');
  if (!actorIsSuper) {
    if (isStaff(targetRoles)) return { ok: false, reason: 'target_is_staff' };
    if (isStaff(newRoles)) return { ok: false, reason: 'cannot_grant_staff_role' };
  }
  return { ok: true };
}



========== FILE: packages/auth/src/tokens.ts ==========

import { createHash, randomBytes, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';

export interface GeneratedToken {
  /** Give this to the user (cookie or email link). Never store it. */
  token: string;
  /** Store this. A database leak then does not reveal usable tokens. */
  hash: string;
}

export function hashToken(token: string): string {
  return createHash('sha256').update(token).digest('hex');
}

/** 256 bits of randomness, URL-safe. */
export function generateToken(bytes = 32): GeneratedToken {
  const token = randomBytes(bytes).toString('base64url');
  return { token, hash: hashToken(token) };
}

/** Constant-time string comparison (safe for secrets of different lengths). */
export function safeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {
  const left = createHash('sha256').update(a).digest();
  const right = createHash('sha256').update(b).digest();
  return timingSafeEqual(left, right);
}

Shared environment schemas, site/brand config and security header/CSP builders

Built to `dist/` with `npm run build`; consumed by apps through `main`/`types`.

========== FILE: packages/config/src/env.test.ts ==========

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { EnvValidationError, parseApiEnv, parseWebEnv } from './env';

const base = {
  API_CORS_ORIGINS: 'http://localhost:3000, http://localhost:3001',
  MONGODB_URI: 'mongodb://user:pw@localhost:27017/db',
  REDIS_URL: 'redis://localhost:6379',
};

describe('parseApiEnv', () => {
  it('applies defaults and parses lists', () => {
    const env = parseApiEnv(base);
    expect(env.API_PORT).toBe(4000);
    expect(env.NODE_ENV).toBe('development');
    expect(env.API_SWAGGER_ENABLED).toBe(false);
    expect(env.API_CORS_ORIGINS).toEqual(['http://localhost:3000', 'http://localhost:3001']);
  });

  it('fails when required variables are missing, naming them but never their values', () => {
    try {
      parseApiEnv({ MONGODB_URI: 'mongodb://user:supersecret@host/db' });
      expect.unreachable();
    } catch (err) {
      expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(EnvValidationError);
      const message = (err as Error).message;
      expect(message).toContain('API_CORS_ORIGINS');
      expect(message).toContain('REDIS_URL');
      expect(message).not.toContain('supersecret');
    }
  });

  it('rejects connection strings with the wrong scheme', () => {
    expect(() => parseApiEnv({ ...base, MONGODB_URI: 'postgres://x' })).toThrow(/MONGODB_URI/);
    expect(() => parseApiEnv({ ...base, REDIS_URL: 'http://x' })).toThrow(/REDIS_URL/);
  });

  it('refuses unsafe settings in production', () => {
    const prod = { ...base, NODE_ENV: 'production' };
    expect(() => parseApiEnv(prod)).toThrow(/https/);
    const secure = {
      ...prod,
      API_CORS_ORIGINS: 'https://app.example.org',
      MONGODB_URI: 'mongodb://u:p@db.internal/db',
      REDIS_URL: 'rediss://:p@cache.internal:6379',
      APP_WEB_URL: 'https://app.example.org',
      EMAIL_TRANSPORT: 'disabled',
    };
    expect(parseApiEnv(secure).NODE_ENV).toBe('production');
    expect(() =>
      parseApiEnv({ ...secure, MONGODB_URI: 'mongodb://u:change-me@db.internal/db' }),
    ).toThrow(/placeholder/);
    expect(() => parseApiEnv({ ...secure, API_SWAGGER_ENABLED: 'true' })).toThrow(/disabled/);
    expect(() => parseApiEnv({ ...secure, EMAIL_TRANSPORT: 'console' })).toThrow(/EMAIL_TRANSPORT/);
    expect(() => parseApiEnv({ ...secure, APP_WEB_URL: 'http://app.example.org' })).toThrow(
      /APP_WEB_URL/,
    );
  });
});

describe('parseWebEnv', () => {
  it('provides local defaults and validates URLs', () => {
    expect(parseWebEnv({}).NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL).toBe('http://localhost:4000');
    expect(() => parseWebEnv({ NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL: 'not-a-url' })).toThrow(EnvValidationError);
  });
});



========== FILE: packages/config/src/env.ts ==========

import { z } from 'zod';

const booleanString = z.enum(['true', 'false']).transform((value) => value === 'true');

const commaSeparatedUrls = z
  .string()
  .min(1)
  .transform((value) =>
    value
      .split(',')
      .map((item) => item.trim())
      .filter(Boolean),
  )
  .pipe(z.array(z.url()).min(1));

const PLACEHOLDER_MARKER = 'change-me';

/** Environment contract for apps/api. Parsed once at startup; the process exits on failure. */
export const apiEnvSchema = z
  .object({
    NODE_ENV: z.enum(['development', 'test', 'production']).default('development'),
    API_HOST: z.string().min(1).default('0.0.0.0'),
    API_PORT: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(65535).default(4000),
    LOG_LEVEL: z
      .enum(['fatal', 'error', 'warn', 'info', 'debug', 'trace', 'silent'])
      .default('info'),
    API_CORS_ORIGINS: commaSeparatedUrls,
    API_TRUST_PROXY: z.coerce.number().int().min(0).max(10).default(0),
    API_SWAGGER_ENABLED: booleanString.default(false),
    RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS: z.coerce.number().int().min(1000).default(60_000),
    RATE_LIMIT_MAX: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).default(120),
    /** Public web origin used to build links in emails (verify email, reset password). */
    APP_WEB_URL: z.url().default('http://localhost:3000'),
    /** 'console' prints emails (incl. links) to the log — development only. 'disabled' sends nothing. */
    EMAIL_TRANSPORT: z.enum(['console', 'disabled']).default('console'),
    SESSION_IDLE_MINUTES: z.coerce.number().int().min(5).max(1440).default(720),
    SESSION_ABSOLUTE_HOURS: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(720).default(168),
    COOKIE_SAME_SITE: z.enum(['lax', 'strict', 'none']).default('lax'),
    AUTH_MAX_FAILED_ATTEMPTS: z.coerce.number().int().min(3).max(20).default(5),
    AUTH_LOCKOUT_MINUTES: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(1440).default(15),
    /** Per-IP limit for credential endpoints (login, register, forgot/reset password). */
    AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_MAX: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).default(20),
    HEALTH_CHECK_TIMEOUT_MS: z.coerce.number().int().min(100).max(30_000).default(2_000),
    MONGODB_URI: z
      .string()
      .min(1)
      .refine((v) => v.startsWith('mongodb://') || v.startsWith('mongodb+srv://'), {
        message: 'must start with mongodb:// or mongodb+srv://',
      }),
    REDIS_URL: z
      .string()
      .min(1)
      .refine((v) => v.startsWith('redis://') || v.startsWith('rediss://'), {
        message: 'must start with redis:// or rediss://',
      }),
  })
  .superRefine((env, ctx) => {
    if (env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') return;
    const addIssue = (path: string, message: string) =>
      ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [path], message });

    if (env.MONGODB_URI.includes(PLACEHOLDER_MARKER)) {
      addIssue('MONGODB_URI', 'contains a placeholder credential; set a real secret');
    }
    if (env.REDIS_URL.includes(PLACEHOLDER_MARKER)) {
      addIssue('REDIS_URL', 'contains a placeholder credential; set a real secret');
    }
    if (env.API_CORS_ORIGINS.some((origin) => !origin.startsWith('https://'))) {
      addIssue('API_CORS_ORIGINS', 'must use https:// origins in production');
    }
    if (env.EMAIL_TRANSPORT === 'console') {
      addIssue('EMAIL_TRANSPORT', "'console' would log secret links; use a real email transport");
    }
    if (!env.APP_WEB_URL.startsWith('https://')) {
      addIssue('APP_WEB_URL', 'must use https:// in production');
    }
    if (env.API_SWAGGER_ENABLED) {
      addIssue('API_SWAGGER_ENABLED', 'API docs UI must be disabled in production');
    }
  });

export type ApiEnv = z.infer<typeof apiEnvSchema>;

export class EnvValidationError extends Error {
  readonly issues: readonly string[];
  constructor(scope: string, issues: readonly string[]) {
    super(`Invalid ${scope} environment:\n${issues.map((i) => `  - ${i}`).join('\n')}`);
    this.name = 'EnvValidationError';
    this.issues = issues;
  }
}

function formatIssues(error: z.ZodError): string[] {
  // Only variable names and messages — never the offending values (they may be secrets).
  return error.issues.map((issue) => `${issue.path.join('.') || 'env'}: ${issue.message}`);
}

export function parseApiEnv(source: Record<string, string | undefined>): ApiEnv {
  const result = apiEnvSchema.safeParse(source);
  if (!result.success) throw new EnvValidationError('API', formatIssues(result.error));
  return result.data;
}

/** Environment contract for the Next.js apps (public values only — never secrets). */
export const webEnvSchema = z.object({
  NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL: z.url().default('http://localhost:3000'),
  NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL: z.url().default('http://localhost:4000'),
});

export type WebEnv = z.infer<typeof webEnvSchema>;

export function parseWebEnv(source: Record<string, string | undefined>): WebEnv {
  const result = webEnvSchema.safeParse(source);
  if (!result.success) throw new EnvValidationError('web', formatIssues(result.error));
  return result.data;
}

========== FILE: packages/config/src/security.test.ts ==========

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { buildContentSecurityPolicy, staticSecurityHeaders } from './security';

describe('buildContentSecurityPolicy', () => {
  it('is nonce-based and blocks framing, plugins and unsafe-eval in production', () => {
    const csp = buildContentSecurityPolicy({ nonce: 'abc123', isDev: false });
    expect(csp).toContain("script-src 'self' 'nonce-abc123' 'strict-dynamic'");
    expect(csp).toContain("frame-ancestors 'none'");
    expect(csp).toContain("object-src 'none'");
    expect(csp).toContain('upgrade-insecure-requests');
    expect(csp).not.toContain('unsafe-eval');
    expect(csp).not.toContain('unsafe-inline');
  });

  it('only relaxes eval/inline styles in development', () => {
    const csp = buildContentSecurityPolicy({ nonce: 'n', isDev: true });
    expect(csp).toContain("'unsafe-eval'");
    expect(csp).not.toContain('upgrade-insecure-requests');
  });

  it('allows only listed connect-src origins', () => {
    const csp = buildContentSecurityPolicy({
      nonce: 'n',
      isDev: false,
      connectSrc: ['https://api.example.org'],
    });
    expect(csp).toContain("connect-src 'self' https://api.example.org");
  });

  it('ships the baseline static headers', () => {
    const keys = staticSecurityHeaders.map((h) => h.key);
    expect(keys).toEqual(
      expect.arrayContaining([
        'Strict-Transport-Security',
        'X-Content-Type-Options',
        'X-Frame-Options',
      ]),
    );
  });
});



========== FILE: packages/config/src/security.ts ==========

export interface CspOptions {
  readonly nonce: string;
  readonly isDev: boolean;
  /** Origins the browser may call with fetch/XHR (e.g. the API). */
  readonly connectSrc?: readonly string[];
}

/**
 * Strict, nonce-based Content-Security-Policy for the Next.js apps.
 * `unsafe-eval` / inline styles are allowed in development only (React refresh, HMR).
 */
export function buildContentSecurityPolicy({ nonce, isDev, connectSrc = [] }: CspOptions): string {
  const directives: Record<string, string[]> = {
    'default-src': ["'self'"],
    'script-src': [
      "'self'",
      `'nonce-${nonce}'`,
      "'strict-dynamic'",
      ...(isDev ? ["'unsafe-eval'"] : []),
    ],
    'style-src': ["'self'", ...(isDev ? ["'unsafe-inline'"] : [`'nonce-${nonce}'`])],
    'img-src': ["'self'", 'data:', 'blob:'],
    'font-src': ["'self'"],
    'connect-src': ["'self'", ...connectSrc],
    'object-src': ["'none'"],
    'base-uri': ["'self'"],
    'form-action': ["'self'"],
    'frame-ancestors': ["'none'"],
    ...(isDev ? {} : { 'upgrade-insecure-requests': [] }),
  };
  return Object.entries(directives)
    .map(([name, values]) => (values.length > 0 ? `${name} ${values.join(' ')}` : name))
    .join('; ');
}

export interface HeaderEntry {
  readonly key: string;
  readonly value: string;
}

/** Static security headers applied to every Next.js response (CSP is added per-request). */
export const staticSecurityHeaders: readonly HeaderEntry[] = [
  { key: 'Strict-Transport-Security', value: 'max-age=63072000; includeSubDomains' },
  { key: 'X-Content-Type-Options', value: 'nosniff' },
  { key: 'X-Frame-Options', value: 'DENY' },
  { key: 'Referrer-Policy', value: 'strict-origin-when-cross-origin' },
  { key: 'Permissions-Policy', value: 'camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=(), payment=()' },
  { key: 'Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy', value: 'same-origin' },
];

/** Per-request CSP nonce (base64 of a random UUID), usable in Node and Edge runtimes. */
export function generateNonce(): string {
  return btoa(crypto.randomUUID());
}

========== FILE: packages/database/src/audit-log.test.ts ==========

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { AuditLog } from './models/audit-log.model';

describe('AuditLog model', () => {
  it('requires an action and outcome', () => {
    const error = new AuditLog({}).validateSync();
    expect(error?.errors['action']).toBeDefined();
    expect(error?.errors['outcome']).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('rejects unknown outcomes and accepts a valid entry', () => {
    expect(
      new AuditLog({ action: 'X', outcome: 'maybe' }).validateSync()?.errors['outcome'],
    ).toBeDefined();
    expect(new AuditLog({ action: 'X', outcome: 'success' }).validateSync()).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('blocks updates and deletes (append-only)', async () => {
    await expect(AuditLog.updateOne({}, { action: 'Y' }).exec()).rejects.toThrow(/immutable/);
    await expect(AuditLog.deleteMany({}).exec()).rejects.toThrow(/immutable/);
  });
});



========== FILE: packages/database/src/connection.ts ==========

import mongoose from 'mongoose';

export interface MongoConnectOptions {
  readonly uri: string;
  readonly serverSelectionTimeoutMS?: number;
  readonly appName?: string;
}

/**
 * Opens the shared Mongoose connection. Credentials come from the URI supplied via
 * environment variables — nothing is hard-coded here.
 */
export async function connectMongo(options: MongoConnectOptions): Promise<typeof mongoose> {
  mongoose.set('strictQuery', true);
  return mongoose.connect(options.uri, {
    serverSelectionTimeoutMS: options.serverSelectionTimeoutMS ?? 5_000,
    appName: options.appName ?? 'impactalign-api',
    autoIndex: process.env['NODE_ENV'] !== 'production',
    // Reject operators in query filters that were built from untrusted input.
    sanitizeFilter: true,
  });
}

export async function disconnectMongo(): Promise<void> {
  await mongoose.disconnect();
}

/** Round-trips to the server; throws when the database is unreachable. */
export async function pingMongo(): Promise<void> {
  const db = mongoose.connection.db;
  if (mongoose.connection.readyState !== 1 || !db) {
    throw new Error('MongoDB connection is not ready');
  }
  await db.admin().ping();
}

========== FILE: packages/database/src/models/audit-log.model.ts ==========

import { Schema, model, type InferSchemaType, type Model } from 'mongoose';

/**
 * Append-only audit trail for security- and compliance-relevant actions.
 * Foundation only: later phases add the concrete event catalogue (LOGIN_SUCCESS, ...).
 * Application code must only ever INSERT; update and delete operations are blocked below.
 */
const auditLogSchema = new Schema(
  {
    action: { type: String, required: true, trim: true, maxlength: 100, index: true },
    actorId: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, default: null, index: true },
    /** Tenant scope. Null only for platform-level events. */
    organizationId: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, default: null, index: true },
    resourceType: { type: String, trim: true, maxlength: 100 },
    resourceId: { type: String, trim: true, maxlength: 100 },
    outcome: { type: String, enum: ['success', 'failure'], required: true },
    requestId: { type: String, trim: true, maxlength: 100 },
    ipAddress: { type: String, trim: true, maxlength: 64 },
    /** Never store secrets, tokens, passwords or document contents here. */
    metadata: { type: Schema.Types.Mixed, default: undefined },
  },
  { timestamps: { createdAt: true, updatedAt: false }, versionKey: false },
);

auditLogSchema.index({ createdAt: -1 });
auditLogSchema.index({ organizationId: 1, createdAt: -1 });

const immutable = (): never => {
  throw new Error('Audit log entries are immutable');
};
for (const hook of [
  'updateOne',
  'updateMany',
  'findOneAndUpdate',
  'findOneAndReplace',
  'replaceOne',
  'deleteOne',
  'deleteMany',
  'findOneAndDelete',
] as const) {
  auditLogSchema.pre(hook, immutable);
}

export type AuditLogDocument = InferSchemaType<typeof auditLogSchema>;

export const AuditLog: Model<AuditLogDocument> = model<AuditLogDocument>(
  'AuditLog',
  auditLogSchema,
);

import { Schema, model, type InferSchemaType, type Model } from 'mongoose';
import { PLATFORM_ROLES } from '@impactalign/types';

const userSchema = new Schema(
  {
    email: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      unique: true,
      lowercase: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 254,
    },
    displayName: { type: String, required: true, trim: true, maxlength: 80 },
    /** Never returned by default; select it explicitly with .select('+passwordHash'). */
    passwordHash: { type: String, required: true, select: false },
    roles: {
      type: [{ type: String, enum: [...PLATFORM_ROLES] }],
      default: ['PUBLIC_USER'],
    },
    status: { type: String, enum: ['active', 'disabled'], default: 'active' },
    emailVerifiedAt: { type: Date, default: null },
    /** Reserved for multi-factor authentication (not implemented yet). */
    mfa: {
      enabled: { type: Boolean, default: false },
      secretEncrypted: { type: String, select: false, default: null },
    },
    deletedAt: { type: Date, default: null },
  },
  { timestamps: true },
);

export type UserDocument = InferSchemaType<typeof userSchema>;
export const User: Model<UserDocument> = model<UserDocument>('User', userSchema);



========== FILE: packages/database/src/seed/sdg.seed.ts ==========

import { SDG } from '../models/sdg.models';

const SDG_RECORDS = Array.from({ length: 17 }, (_, index) => {
  const number = index + 1;

  return {
    number,
    code: `SDG-${String(number).padStart(2, '0')}`,
  };
});

/**
 * Creates the 17 SDG catalogue records.
 * Safe to execute repeatedly without creating duplicates.
 *
 * Official titles, descriptions, targets and indicators
 * must be populated from verified official sources.
 */
export async function seedSDGs(): Promise<number> {
  const operations = SDG_RECORDS.map((record) => ({
    updateOne: {
      filter: { number: record.number },
      update: {
        $setOnInsert: {
          ...record,
          title: null,
          description: null,
          officialUrl: null,
          metadata: null,
          deletedAt: null,
        },
      },
      upsert: true,
    },
  }));

  const result = await SDG.bulkWrite(operations);

  return result.upsertedCount;
}

export { SDG_RECORDS };

export interface ApiErrorDetail {
  path: string;
  message: string;
}

/** Standard error envelope returned by every API error response. */
export interface ApiErrorBody {
  error: {
    code: string;
    message: string;
    requestId: string;
    /** Field-level problems for validation errors (paths and messages only, never values). */
