========== FILE: packages/types/src/auth.ts ==========

/** All user roles. Platform roles live on the user; organization roles live on a membership. */
export const Role = {
  SUPER_ADMIN: 'SUPER_ADMIN',
  ADMIN: 'ADMIN',
  ASSESSOR: 'ASSESSOR',
  REVIEWER: 'REVIEWER',
  ORGANIZATION_ADMIN: 'ORGANIZATION_ADMIN',
  ORGANIZATION_MEMBER: 'ORGANIZATION_MEMBER',
  PROFESSIONAL: 'PROFESSIONAL',
  RESEARCHER: 'RESEARCHER',
  ACADEMIC: 'ACADEMIC',
  PARTNER: 'PARTNER',
  PUBLIC_USER: 'PUBLIC_USER',
} as const;
export type Role = (typeof Role)[keyof typeof Role];

/** Roles assignable to a user account (platform-wide). */
export const PLATFORM_ROLES = [
  'SUPER_ADMIN',
  'ADMIN',
  'ASSESSOR',
  'REVIEWER',
  'PROFESSIONAL',
  'RESEARCHER',
  'ACADEMIC',
  'PARTNER',
  'PUBLIC_USER',
] as const;
export type PlatformRole = (typeof PLATFORM_ROLES)[number];

/** Roles a user holds inside one organization (stored on the membership). */
export const ORGANIZATION_ROLES = ['ORGANIZATION_ADMIN', 'ORGANIZATION_MEMBER'] as const;
export type OrganizationRole = (typeof ORGANIZATION_ROLES)[number];

/** The only roles a person may pick for themselves at registration. Never staff roles. */
export const SELF_REGISTRATION_ROLES = [
  'PROFESSIONAL',
  'RESEARCHER',
  'ACADEMIC',
  'PARTNER',
  'PUBLIC_USER',
] as const;
export type SelfRegistrationRole = (typeof SELF_REGISTRATION_ROLES)[number];

/** Permissions are separate from roles; roles are mapped to permissions in @impactalign/auth. */
export const Permission = {
  USER_READ: 'USER_READ',
  USER_UPDATE: 'USER_UPDATE',
  USER_ROLE_ASSIGN: 'USER_ROLE_ASSIGN',
  ORG_CREATE: 'ORG_CREATE',
  ORG_READ: 'ORG_READ',
  ORG_UPDATE: 'ORG_UPDATE',
  ORG_MEMBER_MANAGE: 'ORG_MEMBER_MANAGE',
  PROJECT_CREATE: 'PROJECT_CREATE',
  PROJECT_READ: 'PROJECT_READ',
  PROJECT_UPDATE: 'PROJECT_UPDATE',
  EVIDENCE_UPLOAD: 'EVIDENCE_UPLOAD',
  EVIDENCE_SUBMIT: 'EVIDENCE_SUBMIT',
  ASSESSMENT_REVIEW: 'ASSESSMENT_REVIEW',
  ACCREDITATION_REVIEW: 'ACCREDITATION_REVIEW',
  AUDIT_READ: 'AUDIT_READ',
  ADMIN_ACCESS: 'ADMIN_ACCESS',
} as const;
export type Permission = (typeof Permission)[keyof typeof Permission];

/** Security-relevant events written to the audit log. */
export const AuditEvent = {
  USER_REGISTERED: 'USER_REGISTERED',
  EMAIL_VERIFIED: 'EMAIL_VERIFIED',
  LOGIN_SUCCESS: 'LOGIN_SUCCESS',
  LOGIN_FAILED: 'LOGIN_FAILED',
  LOGOUT: 'LOGOUT',
  PASSWORD_CHANGED: 'PASSWORD_CHANGED',
  PASSWORD_RESET: 'PASSWORD_RESET',
  ROLE_CHANGED: 'ROLE_CHANGED',
  PERMISSION_CHANGED: 'PERMISSION_CHANGED',
  ORGANIZATION_CREATED: 'ORGANIZATION_CREATED',
} as const;
export type AuditEvent = (typeof AuditEvent)[keyof typeof AuditEvent];

/** User fields that are safe to send to a client. Never includes hashes or tokens. */
export interface PublicUser {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  displayName: string;
  roles: PlatformRole[];
  emailVerified: boolean;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface AuthResponse {
  user: PublicUser;
  /** Send back in the X-CSRF-Token header on state-changing requests. */
  csrfToken: string;
}

export interface PublicOrganization {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  createdAt: string;
  /** The caller's role in this organization. */
  role: OrganizationRole;
}

export interface OrganizationMemberView {
  userId: string;
  displayName: string;
  role: OrganizationRole;
}



========== FILE: packages/types/src/health.ts ==========

export type HealthStatus = 'ok' | 'down';

export interface HealthCheckResult {
  status: HealthStatus;
  /** Which component was checked. */
  check: 'liveness' | 'database' | 'redis';
  /** ISO-8601 timestamp. */
  timestamp: string;
  /** Round-trip time of the dependency probe, when applicable. */
  latencyMs?: number;
}

export interface LivenessResult extends HealthCheckResult {
  check: 'liveness';
  service: string;
  version: string;
  uptimeSeconds: number;
}

========== FILE: packages/ui/src/components/badge.tsx ==========

import * as React from 'react';
import { cva, type VariantProps } from 'class-variance-authority';
import { cn } from '../lib/cn';

export const badgeVariants = cva(
  'inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide',
  {
    variants: {
      tone: {
        neutral: 'bg-navy-100 text-navy-900',
        gold: 'bg-gold-400/20 text-navy-900 ring-1 ring-inset ring-gold-500',
        teal: 'bg-teal-50 text-teal-800 ring-1 ring-inset ring-teal-600/30',
        onDark: 'bg-white/10 text-white ring-1 ring-inset ring-white/30',
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: { tone: 'neutral' },
  },
);

export interface BadgeProps
  extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLSpanElement>, VariantProps<typeof badgeVariants> {}

export function Badge({ className, tone, ...props }: BadgeProps) {
  return <span className={cn(badgeVariants({ tone }), className)} {...props} />;
}



========== FILE: packages/ui/src/components/button.tsx ==========

import * as React from 'react';
import { Slot } from '@radix-ui/react-slot';
import { cva, type VariantProps } from 'class-variance-authority';
import { cn } from '../lib/cn';

export const buttonVariants = cva(
  'inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition-colors focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-teal-600 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50',
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        primary: 'bg-navy-900 text-white hover:bg-navy-800',
        accent: 'bg-gold-500 text-navy-950 hover:bg-gold-400',
        outline: 'border border-navy-900 text-navy-900 hover:bg-navy-50',
        ghost: 'text-navy-900 hover:bg-navy-50',
      },
      size: {
        sm: 'h-9 px-3',
        md: 'h-11 px-5',
        lg: 'h-12 px-7 text-base',
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: { variant: 'primary', size: 'md' },
  },
);

export interface ButtonProps
  extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>, VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> {
  /** Render the child element (e.g. a Next.js Link) with button styling. */
  asChild?: boolean;
}

export const Button = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ className, variant, size, asChild = false, type, ...props }, ref) => {
    const Comp = asChild ? Slot : 'button';
    return (
      <Comp
        ref={ref}
        className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size }), className)}
        {...(asChild ? {} : { type: type ?? 'button' })}
        {...props}
      />
    );
  },
);
Button.displayName = 'Button';

import * as React from 'react';
import { cva, type VariantProps } from 'class-variance-authority';
import { cn } from '../lib/cn';

export const cardVariants = cva('rounded-xl border p-6', {
  variants: {
    tone: {
      light: 'border-navy-100 bg-white shadow-sm',
      muted: 'border-navy-100 bg-navy-50',
      dark: 'border-white/10 bg-white/5 text-white',
    },
    interactive: {
      true: 'transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-md focus-within:ring-2 focus-within:ring-teal-600',
      false: '',
    },
  },
  defaultVariants: { tone: 'light', interactive: false },
});

export interface CardProps
  extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>, VariantProps<typeof cardVariants> {}

export function Card({ className, tone, interactive, ...props }: CardProps) {
  return <div className={cn(cardVariants({ tone, interactive }), className)} {...props} />;
}

========== FILE: packages/ui/src/components/skip-link.tsx ==========

import { cn } from '../lib/cn';

export interface SkipLinkProps {
  readonly targetId?: string;
  readonly className?: string;
}

/** First focusable element on every page — lets keyboard users bypass navigation. */
export function SkipLink({ targetId = 'main-content', className }: SkipLinkProps) {
  return (
    <a
      href={`#${targetId}`}
      className={cn(
        'sr-only focus:not-sr-only focus:fixed focus:left-4 focus:top-4 focus:z-50 focus:rounded-md focus:bg-white focus:px-4 focus:py-2 focus:font-semibold focus:text-navy-900 focus:shadow-lg',
        className,
      )}
    >
      Skip to main content
    </a>
  );
}

========== FILE: packages/ui/src/lib/cn.ts ==========

import { clsx, type ClassValue } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';

/** Merge conditional class names and resolve Tailwind conflicts (shadcn/ui convention). */
export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]): string {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs));
}



========== FILE: packages/ui/src/ui.test.tsx ==========

// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { cleanup, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { Button, SkipLink, cn } from './index';

afterEach(cleanup);

describe('cn', () => {
  it('resolves conflicting Tailwind classes', () => {
    expect(cn('px-2', 'px-4')).toBe('px-4');
  });
});

describe('Button', () => {
  it('defaults to type="button" so it never submits forms accidentally', () => {
    render(<Button>Save</Button>);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }).getAttribute('type')).toBe('button');
  });

  it('can render a link with button styling', () => {
    render(
      <Button asChild>
        <a href="/join-impactalign">JOIN IMPACTALIGN</a>
      </Button>,
    );
    const link = screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'JOIN IMPACTALIGN' });
    expect(link.getAttribute('href')).toBe('/join-impactalign');
    expect(link.getAttribute('type')).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('SkipLink', () => {
  it('targets the main landmark', () => {
    render(<SkipLink />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: /skip to main content/i }).getAttribute('href')).toBe(
      '#main-content',
    );
  });
});

describe('Badge and Card', () => {
  it('render their content', async () => {
    const { Badge, Card } = await import('./index');
    render(
      <Card>
        <Badge tone="gold">Planned</Badge>
      </Card>,
    );
    expect(screen.getByText('Planned')).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

describe('EmptyState', () => {
  it('shows title and description', async () => {
    const { EmptyState } = await import('./index');
    render(<EmptyState title="Nothing here yet" description="Coming in a later phase." />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Nothing here yet')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText('Coming in a later phase.')).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

========== FILE: packages/validation/README.md ==========

# @impactalign/validation

Shared Zod validation schemas

Built to `dist/` with `npm run build`; consumed by apps through `main`/`types`.



========== FILE: packages/validation/package.json ==========



========== FILE: packages/validation/src/auth.ts ==========

import { z } from 'zod';
import { ORGANIZATION_ROLES, PLATFORM_ROLES, SELF_REGISTRATION_ROLES } from '@impactalign/types';

/** Normalised (trimmed, lower-cased) email address. */
export const emailSchema = z
  .string()
  .trim()
  .toLowerCase()
  .pipe(z.email({ message: 'Enter a valid email address' }).max(254));

const COMMON_PASSWORDS = new Set([
  'password1234',
  'passwordpassword',
  '123456789012',
  'qwertyuiop12',
  'iloveyou1234',
  'administrator',
  'welcome12345',
]);

/** New-password policy: length over complexity rules (NIST SP 800-63B style). */
export const passwordSchema = z
  .string()
  .min(12, 'Use at least 12 characters')
  .max(128, 'Use at most 128 characters')
  .refine((value) => !COMMON_PASSWORDS.has(value.toLowerCase()), {
    message: 'This password is too common',
  });

const tokenSchema = z.string().min(20).max(200);

/** Strict objects: unknown keys (e.g. a client-supplied "roles") are rejected, never ignored. */
export const registerSchema = z
  .object({
    email: emailSchema,
    password: passwordSchema,
    displayName: z.string().trim().min(2).max(80),
    accountType: z.enum(SELF_REGISTRATION_ROLES).default('PUBLIC_USER'),
  })
  .strict()
  .refine(
    (value) => {
      const local = value.email.split('@')[0] ?? '';
      return local.length < 4 || !value.password.toLowerCase().includes(local);
    },
    { path: ['password'], message: 'Password must not contain your email name' },
  );

export const loginSchema = z
  .object({ email: emailSchema, password: z.string().min(1).max(128) })
  .strict();

export const verifyEmailSchema = z.object({ token: tokenSchema }).strict();
export const forgotPasswordSchema = z.object({ email: emailSchema }).strict();
export const resetPasswordSchema = z
  .object({ token: tokenSchema, password: passwordSchema })
  .strict();
export const changePasswordSchema = z
  .object({ currentPassword: z.string().min(1).max(128), newPassword: passwordSchema })
  .strict();

export const updateProfileSchema = z
  .object({ displayName: z.string().trim().min(2).max(80) })
  .strict();

export const assignRolesSchema = z
  .object({ roles: z.array(z.enum(PLATFORM_ROLES)).min(1).max(PLATFORM_ROLES.length) })
  .strict();

export const createOrganizationSchema = z
  .object({ name: z.string().trim().min(2).max(120) })
  .strict();

export const organizationRoleSchema = z.enum(ORGANIZATION_ROLES);

export type RegisterInput = z.infer<typeof registerSchema>;
export type LoginInput = z.infer<typeof loginSchema>;



========== FILE: packages/validation/src/common.ts ==========

import { z } from 'zod';

/** MongoDB ObjectId as a 24-char hex string. */
export const objectIdSchema = z.string().regex(/^[a-f\d]{24}$/i, 'Invalid id');

export const paginationQuerySchema = z.object({
  page: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).default(1),
  pageSize: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(100).default(20),
});

export type PaginationQuery = z.infer<typeof paginationQuerySchema>;

========== FILE: packages/validation/src/sdg.ts ==========

import { z } from 'zod';

/** Route param: SDG number 1-17, parsed to a number. */
export const sdgNumberParamSchema = z.object({
  id: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^(?:[1-9]|1[0-7])$/, 'id must be an SDG number from 1 to 17')
    .transform(Number),
});

export type SdgNumberParam = z.infer<typeof sdgNumberParamSchema>;


========== FILE: packages/validation/src/validation.test.ts ==========

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { healthCheckResultSchema, livenessResultSchema } from './health';
import { objectIdSchema, paginationQuerySchema } from './common';

describe('objectIdSchema', () => {
  it('accepts 24-char hex and rejects injection-style input', () => {
    expect(objectIdSchema.safeParse('64b7f0c2e1a4d3a1b2c3d4e5').success).toBe(true);
    expect(objectIdSchema.safeParse('{"$ne":null}').success).toBe(false);
    expect(objectIdSchema.safeParse('short').success).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('paginationQuerySchema', () => {
  it('coerces strings and applies defaults', () => {
    expect(paginationQuerySchema.parse({})).toEqual({ page: 1, pageSize: 20 });
    expect(paginationQuerySchema.parse({ page: '3', pageSize: '50' })).toEqual({
      page: 3,
      pageSize: 50,
    });
  });
  it('caps page size', () => {
    expect(paginationQuerySchema.safeParse({ pageSize: '1000' }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(paginationQuerySchema.safeParse({ page: '0' }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('health schemas', () => {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  it('validates dependency results', () => {
    expect(
      healthCheckResultSchema.safeParse({ status: 'ok', check: 'redis', timestamp: now }).success,
    ).toBe(true);
    expect(
      healthCheckResultSchema.safeParse({ status: 'maybe', check: 'redis', timestamp: now })
        .success,
    ).toBe(false);
  });
  it('requires liveness fields', () => {
    expect(
      livenessResultSchema.safeParse({ status: 'ok', check: 'liveness', timestamp: now }).success,
    ).toBe(false);
  });
});

========== FILE: scripts/README.md ==========

# scripts

- `run-workspaces.mjs` — runs an npm script across workspaces in dependency order.
- `check-env.mjs` — validates `.env` against the API contract (prints names, never values).
- `clean.mjs` — removes build output.
